# Master Lore Dossier: *Katie & Nathaniel* (`books/katie`) & The Political Rise of U.S. Senator Katie Prairie (`D-Virginia`)

This canonical lore dossier locks in the family tree, mobility/medical baseline, the 2005 five-bus escape and **Roosevelt Rehabilitation Center (`RRC`)** `PHP` arc, the 2008 *Ideas for Coastal Virginia Transit* four-bus mayoral loop (`#29` $\rightarrow$ `#20` $\rightarrow$ `#15` $\rightarrow$ `#12`), the Virginia Beach Municipal Center evening bus curfew catch-22, and the step-by-step political career, policy platform, and GOP rebuttal matrix of **U.S. Senator Katie Prairie (`D-Virginia`)** as told through the first-person POV of her fraternal twin brother **Nathaniel ("Nate") Prairie**.

---

## 1. Core Twin Identity, Mobility Baseline, & Family Canon

- **Fraternal Twins Katie & Nathaniel ("Nate") Prairie**:
  - **Date of Birth**: **April 8, 1985** (Virginia Beach, VA).
  - **Neurotype, Sibling Sanctuary, Deep-Pressure Hugs & Cuddles, & Lifelong Confined-Space Closet Sleeping Preference**:
    - Both Katie and Nate are autistic and share an unbreakable lifelong twin sanctuary (*"Twin Brothers and Twin Sisters never leave each other behind"*).
    - **Deep-Pressure Hugs, Cuddling, & Close Embraces (Prose & Illustration Rule)**: In addition to holding hands across their wheelchair armrests, **Nate and Katie deeply love to hug and cuddle with each other**. The deep pressure of a close embrace is profoundly soothing to both of their autistic nervous systems and makes them feel the most secure—whether flipping up their inner wheelchair armrests to lean across and wrap their arms tightly around each other in a long, close hug, resting their heads against each other's shoulders, or cuddling together on a sofa or inside their sleeping bag. Across narrative prose and scene illustrations, regularly depict Nate and Katie hugging and cuddling close together, not just holding hands.
    - **Lifelong Confined-Space Closet Sleeping Preference**: Beginning on August 9, 2003 in `Apartment A6` at `801 Greenway Court` (`books/katie/e004/c002/p002.md`) and continuing for the rest of their lives, **sleeping cuddled up together inside a single shared sleeping bag in a confined space (such as their apartment or bedroom closet) is their preferred everyday bedroom**—because for autism sensory and emotional-regulation reasons, the enclosed, dark, confined space makes them feel completely secure, while their regular full-sized or queen bed in the main room rarely gets used. (By the `2044`/`2045` Presidential race cycle—after nearly two decades in the Senate—U.S. Senator Katie Prairie has risen to become the **most powerful woman in the United States Senate**, to the point where every Democrat seeking to run for President in the `2045` inauguration cycle actively seeks out her endorsement, even as she and Nate still return home every night to sleep cuddled up together inside their shared sleeping bag in their confined closet sanctuary.)
  - **POV Standard**: Every chapter of `books/katie` is written in **First-Person Past-Tense Novel POV (`"I"`)**—predominantly `pov: "Nathaniel"`, with select milestone chapters in `pov: "Katie"` (such as her five-bus escape from Williamsburg in `e005/c001/p005.md` and her closing U.S. Senate impeachment trial speech in `e006/c004/p002.md`).
  - **Strict Constitutional Officeholder vs. 50–50 Private-Citizen Partner Boundary (`5 U.S.C. § 3110`)**:
    - **In Private Life, Homes, Finances, & Software (Strictly 50–50 — Later Expanding Into Their Four-Person Family Sanctuary With Rescued Cousin `Chelsea` & Redeemed Cousin `Brittany`)**: After surviving their fourteen-month separation with Brandon Mercer and Tiffany Sloan, Katie and Nate make a lifelong pact on **Sunday evening, November 13, 2005** (`books/katie/e005/c002/p001.md`) never to look for romance, boyfriends, girlfriends, husbands, or wives ever again—choosing each other as lifelong **50–50 domestic and business partners** alongside their father **David Prairie** (`until David's passing before 2032`), and later welcoming home their two beloved female cousins to live with them full-time (`splitting their lives together across Arvin Road in Virginia Beach and Sunset Drive in Alexandria so the four of them are never separated`): (a) their severely developmentally disabled older paternal cousin **Chelsea** (`whom Katie and Nate rescue out of an abusive Rhode Island group home after living alone on Tonomy Hill didn't work out`), and (b) their redeemed maternal cousin **Brittany** (`born March 21, 1985—whom Nate rescues at her aborted wedding on Saturday, March 19, 2016 right after Katie rips apart HB 663 and the 2016 General Assembly adjourns sine die, when Nate says "I object!" during "Speak now or forever hold your peace" and Brittany tearfully tells the officiant she wants to go live with her cousin just as she promised back in high school`). Between the twins, there is zero need for privacy (`helping each other shower on the padded transfer bench and installing a custom extra-wide two-person roll-up hydrotherapy soaking tub at 737 Arvin Road in Spring 2007 and 741 Lord Nelson Drive in October 2019`); and while traumatized Katie and Nate spend over thirteen years (`2003–2016+`) only feeling safe sleeping inside a single shared sleeping bag on hard carpeted closet floors, **having Brittany and Chelsea safely under their roof finally convinces Katie and Nate—after months of gentle, patient coaxing—to come out of the closet and sleep together in modest cozy sleepwear on a custom wheelchair-transfer-height four-person cuddle nest platform bed** (`where Katie and Nate sheepishly admit that sleeping on carpeted closet floors really did hurt, and where Nate's favorite part of life is cuddling with the three women he loves: his twin sister Katie, his maternal cousin Brittany, and his paternal cousin Chelsea`, [`books/katie/e011/c007/p001.md`](file:///Users/michael/Developer/raggiesoft-servers/raggiesoft-narratives/books/katie/e011/c007/p001.md)).
    - **In Public & Constitutional Office (Strictly 100% Katie Prairie)**: Only **Katie Prairie** is on the ballot, only Katie holds the elected title (**Virginia Beach City Councilwoman**, **CVTA Voting Commissioner**, **Virginia State Delegate**, **Virginia State Senator**, and **United States Senator**), only Katie sits at the legislative dais or floor desk during sessions, and only Katie casts official votes. Under federal anti-nepotism law (**`5 U.S.C. § 3110`**, which bars a Member of Congress from hiring a sibling onto a paid government payroll), **Nate never takes a dime of taxpayer salary**; he remains a private citizen, earns their living through their 50–50 software business, sits beside Katie in her private office (`Dirksen Room 511`) and committee prep as her unpaid chief data strategist, and watches formal floor sessions and roll-call votes from the accessible front row of the **Family / Public Gallery** alongside Dad.
    - **Katie's Lifelong Autistic Habit of Answering Her Own Office Phone Directly (`City Councilwoman`, `State Delegate`, `State Senator`, & `U.S. Senator`)**: Whereas almost no constitutional officer in local, state, or federal government ever picks up their own main office switchboard line (relying instead on receptionists, legislative assistants, staff assistants, and press secretaries to screen calls and take messages), **whenever Katie Prairie is in her office at her desk and can reach the telephone, she answers her own phone directly**:
      - **Virginia Beach City Council (`e006/c002/p002.md`)**: *"Councilwoman Katie Prairie's office, this is Katie speaking."*
      - **Virginia House of Delegates (`e007/c003/p002.md`)**: *"Delegate Katie Prairie's office, this is Katie speaking"* (at which point in February 2016 people in Richmond and Washington are still learning that Katie answers her own phone directly, genuinely startling *Meet the Nation* Senior Producer **Arthur Vance** when he calls the main Room 518 switchboard line expecting a legislative aide).
      - **Senate of Virginia (`e008/c002/p001.md`)**: *"Senator Katie Prairie's office, this is Katie speaking."*
      - **United States Senate (`e009/c004/p001.md`)**: *"Senator Katie Prairie's office, this is Katie speaking"* (in `Room 511` of the **Dirksen Senate Office Building**).
      - **Why She Does It**: In Katie's literal, rule-abiding autistic mind, it is *her* office, her name is on the door, the voters hired her to do the job, and sitting three feet away from a ringing telephone without picking it up would be rude and dishonest—so answering the phone herself is the polite, honest thing to do so constituents know she is actually at her desk working for them.
- **Full-Time Power Wheelchairs (`September 2001+`, Age 16, Prescribed by Primary Care Physician `Dr. Evelyn Carter`) & Healthy Lungs (`No Asthma for Nate or Katie`)**:
  - Born with progressive pediatric neuromuscular weakness in their lower legs, Nate and Katie walked with increasing fatigue through age 15 and early age 16 until their legs gave out on the third Friday of September 2001 (Junior year at **Northwood High School**, `1668 Kempsville Rd`, real-life Tallwood High School), when Virginia Beach EMS rushed both twins `4.4 miles` straight north up Kempsville Road to the **Crossroads Memorial Hospital Emergency Room** (*real-life Sentara Leigh Hospital*, `830 Kempsville Rd`, the closest ER to Northwood High School—less than half the distance of Bayside, and ten years before Princess Anne Hospital opened in 2011), where their Primary Care Physician **Dr. Evelyn Carter** met them and their father David (`books/katie/e003/c002/p005.md` & `e003/c003/p002.md`) to review their genetic and nerve-conduction results and prescribe their powerchairs.
  - From September 2001 onward, both Nate and Katie use **compact `SwiftChair` power wheelchairs** full-time from the moment they wake up until they transfer into bed at night.
  - Neither Nate nor Katie has asthma (their lungs and upper bodies are 100% healthy; only their cousin **Chelsea** in Newport, Rhode Island has chronic asthma caused by her mother Aunt Liz's smoking).
  - Neither Nate nor Katie drives a motor vehicle; **Coastal Virginia Transit Authority (`CVTA`)** buses are their everyday lifeline across Hampton Roads.
- **Financial Independence (`Zero Government Benefits — Ever`)**:
  - Neither Katie, Nate, nor their father David has ever received SSI, SNAP, Medicaid, or any government welfare benefits. Since completing their vocational and college training, Katie and Nate have been 100% financially self-supporting software developers (`PHP` / `Quantum Studio 2026` / `Quantum Code`), buying their **Kempsville** home in Virginia Beach 50–50, paying full Virginia Beach property taxes, and purchasing their own `SwiftChair` power wheelchairs and private healthcare out of pocket.
- **Lifelong Sobriety Vow, Lethal Medication Contraindication (`Alcohol & Tobacco = Known Side Effect of Death`), & Canonical Drink Preferences (`No Alcohol & No Coffee for Nate and Katie`)**:
  - After witnessing their mother's alcoholism on the cruise ship *Sunward Tropics* (August 2001) and hiding behind a locked bedroom door eating dry toasted oat cereal out of a cardboard box and drinking ginger ale during their cousin **Brittany's** unauthorized New Year's Eve rave (`books/katie/e004/c001/p001.md`), both Nate and Katie vow at midnight on January 1, 2003 never to drink alcohol—not even a twenty-first birthday drink.
  - **Medical Contraindication (`Katie`, `Nate`, & `David`)**: In addition to their personal sobriety vow, **Katie, Nate, and their father David all take a daily maintenance prescription medication where mixing with alcohol or tobacco has a known side effect of death**. In Washington, D.C. and Richmond, this gives Senator Katie Prairie, Nate, and David an ironclad, indisputable medical reason never to drink wine, champagne, or cocktails—or touch cigars/tobacco—with other Senators, lobbyists, or donors.
  - **Nate & Katie's Drink Preferences (`No Alcohol & No Coffee`)**: Neither Nate nor Katie likes the taste of coffee, and they cannot drink any alcoholic products. Instead, they drink a normal everyday variety of non-alcoholic, non-coffee beverages—including **milk, juice, soda (such as ginger ale), chocolate milk, hot chocolate, flavored water, sports drinks, and plain water** (non-exhaustive list). Their father **David** enjoys hot black coffee, while Katie and Nate always stick to hot chocolate, chocolate milk, juice, milk, soda/ginger ale, flavored water, or sports drinks.

Primary Residence in Virginia Beach (`737 Arvin Road`, off `Military Highway [US-13]` & `Indian River Road`)**: Originally rented in August 2001 following the separation/divorce from their alcoholic mother Brenda, **David bought this single-story three-bedroom brick ranch directly from the landlord**, and it served as the **Primary Residence on his Virginia Driver's License** through February 2007.

Second House in Alexandria (`1921 King St` / `"Sunset Drive"`, Purchased in `March 2007` When David Gets His Job in Tysons Corner)**: Once David lands his corporate systems-engineering job in **Tysons Corner** in March 2007, he buys a second house on **Sunset Drive** (`1921 King St, Alexandria, VA`, immediately west of the King Street railroad underpass) and **updates his Virginia Driver's License primary residence to Sunset Drive in Alexandria** (which the twins later use as their Northern Virginia operating base when Katie is elected to the U.S. Senate).   - **Paternal Grandmother (`Grandma`), Aunt Elizabeth ("Aunt Liz"), & Cousin Chelsea (`Paternal Side Living in Newport, Rhode Island — NEVER Newport News, VA`)**:
    - **Grandma & Aunt Liz (`Both Living in Newport, Rhode Island; Both Deceased by 2004`)**: David Prairie's mother (**Grandma**, Katie and Nate's paternal grandmother) and David's sister **Aunt Elizabeth ("Aunt Liz")**—the only smoker in the Prairie family (`books/katie/e001/c003/p001.md`, where Grandma, Aunt Liz, and 12-year-old Chelsea ride the southbound `ARTS` train down *from* **Newport, Rhode Island** to detrain at the **Newport News `ARTS` Station** across the harbor from Virginia Beach)—both lived in **Newport, Rhode Island**. Because Aunt Liz smoked heavily throughout her pregnancy and inside her home, her daughter **Chelsea** was born developmentally disabled and with severe chronic asthma. Between New Year's Eve 2002 (`books/katie/e004/c001/p001.md`) and Christmas 2004 (`books/katie/e005/c001/p004.md`), both Grandma and Aunt Liz pass away in Rhode Island (Aunt Liz dying from smoking-related illness), leaving Chelsea with no surviving family in Rhode Island and making Nate, Katie, and David in Virginia her only living family.
    - **Chelsea (`Born ~1982, Three Years Older Than Nate and Katie` — Rescued by Katie & Nate Out of an Abusive Rhode Island Group Home to Live Full-Time With Them)**: In 2004, lives in a ground-floor unit in the aging World War II–era defense-worker public housing buildings on **"Dirty Old Tonomy Hill"** in **Newport, Rhode Island** (served by **`OSTA Route 63`** from the **Newport Gateway Center**). Because Chelsea is **severely developmentally disabled**, trying to live alone in Rhode Island after Aunt Liz and Grandma died in 2004 never worked out—and when Rhode Island social services placed her in a group home that was abusing her and neglecting her chronic asthma, **Katie and Nate rescued Chelsea out of that abusive Rhode Island group home** and brought her home to live with them permanently (`splitting time full-time with Katie, Nate, and Brittany between Arvin Road in Virginia Beach and Sunset Drive in Alexandria so the four of them are never separated`).
    - **Chelsea's Medical & Developmental Profile**: Chelsea is **severely developmentally disabled** (`books/katie/e001/c003/p001.md`, `e005/c001/p004.md`, & `e011/c007/p001.md`): she can walk on her own feet (though she struggles with her gait), her speech is slurred, she has **chronic asthma** (requiring her to keep both a **regular maintenance inhaler** and a **rescue inhaler** with her at all times), and from childhood the family constantly had to remind her to stay dressed because she does not grasp conventional modesty filters when stepping out of the shower or getting ready for bed—especially when someone she loves is in distress.
    - **CRITICAL CANON RULE (`Newport, RI` vs. `Newport News, VA`)**: Never confuse **Newport, Rhode Island** (where Grandma, Aunt Liz, and Cousin Chelsea lived) with **Newport News, Virginia** (merely the Virginia `ARTS` passenger rail terminal across the HRBT prior to Norfolk rail service). On New Year's Eve 2002 (`books/katie/e004/c001/p001.md`), Grandma, Aunt Liz, and Chelsea are all at home up in **Newport, Rhode Island**, and **David Prairie drives nine hours north to Newport, RI** to check on Grandma (who has winter bronchitis) over the New Year while 17-year-old Katie and Nate stay home in Virginia Beach—which is the exact reason Katie and Nate cannot simply call Dad from their locked bedroom to come straight home that night (had Dad been anywhere in Hampton Roads, Nate and Katie would have quietly called him to come to the house immediately!). Chelsea is **NEVER** the cousin who throws the drunken New Year's Eve party, and prior to Katie and Nate rescuing her from the abusive Rhode Island group home, never claim that an early Virginia state Medicaid waiver bill gave Chelsea a waiver slot while she was still in Rhode Island. In adulthood, **Chelsea has a major dedicated story arc on Christmas Eve 2004 (`books/katie/e005/c001/p004.md`)**, and later lives happily with Katie, Nate, and redeemed Cousin Brittany in their four-person household (`books/katie/e011/c007/p001.md`).
  - **Maternal Cousin Brittany (`Born March 21, 1985` — `Blizzard of '96 First Group Cuddle` $\rightarrow$ `Inseparable 9th/10th Grade Northwood High Trio` $\rightarrow$ `11th Grade Drift & Turning Down a Cigarette` $\rightarrow$ `Dec 31, 2002 Drunken New Year's Eve` $\rightarrow$ `March 19, 2016 Aborted-Wedding Redemption After HB 663` $\rightarrow$ `Living Full-Time With Katie, Nate, & Chelsea`)**:
    - **Visual & Physical Baseline (`ai-staging/raw-images/characters/katie-nathaniel/brittany_maternal_cousin_age_16_baseline.jpg`)**: Maternal cousin (from estranged mother Brenda Walsh's side of the family, born **March 21, 1985**—eighteen days older than Katie and Nate, Class of 2003 cohort). Derived from Katie's maternal genetics with softer/rounder cousin facial features, **thin silver wire-rimmed oval glasses**, **long hair like Katie in natural `Medium-Blond-Mix [11/15/27]`** (dimensional warm caramel-ash / dark-golden medium blonde, noticeably darker and richer than Katie and Nate's pale blonde, softly threaded with silver by 2043/2044), **curvy and overweight build**, **neurotypical**, and **walks completely normally with zero mobility devices**.
    - **`[INTERNAL LORE DATA ONLY — NEVER SURFACE STREET ADDRESS IN NOVEL PROSE]` Home Geography, The Impassable `Macgregory Street` Curb Trap, & SRO Officer Whitehead's Legal Line**: Brittany's home address is **`5600 Earlton Ct, Virginia Beach, VA 23464`** (which in real-life VBCPS is zoned for *Centerville Elementary* and *Brandon Middle* before feeding into *Tallwood / Northwood High School*—explaining why Brittany was zoned for a different elementary and middle school while Nate and Katie attended Northwood Elementary and Morrison Middle School). **Walking Route (`< 0.25 miles / 5 minutes`)**: Exit the house by turning **right onto Earlton Ct**, then **left onto Macgregory St**, following **Macgregory Street** straight to where it **dead-ends (`1713 Macgregory St`) directly at the chain-link fenceline of `Lions Way`** (`ai-staging/raw-images/characters/katie-nathaniel/northwood_macgregory_st_lions_way_smokers_corner_gate_ref.jpg` & `northwood_macgregory_st_lions_way_smokers_corner_curb_closeup_ref.jpg`). Right beside the utility pole sits the narrow pedestrian gate opening in the chain-link fence—**and as shown in the zoomed-in Street View, the narrow walkway through the fence ends at a sheer, unbroken vertical yellow-painted concrete curb dropping onto `Lions Way` with ZERO ADA curb ramp anywhere** (`wheelchairs physically CANNOT cross this curb at all!`). Because the `Macgregory Street` asphalt turnaround is a public City of Virginia Beach street *just* off school property—and Virginia law in `1999–2003` (`Va. Code § 18.2-371.2`) allowed **18-year-olds** to legally possess and smoke cigarettes off school grounds—legally there is **nothing** SRO **Officer Whitehead** can do to stop 18-year-old seniors from smoking on `Macgregory Street` (`Smoker's Corner`) before `7:15 AM` or after `2:00 PM` (he stakes out the fence watching for under-18 minors taking a cigarette, anyone stepping back inside the chain-link gate with a lit cigarette, or truancy during school hours). *(Shared Northwood Crossover: This exact uncut curb is also why **Luna and Leo Porter** in `/books/luna-leo` **have** to turn down invitations/cigarettes at `Smoker's Corner`—even though they would refuse anyway, Leo's wheelchair physically **cannot** cross the curb to reach `Smoker's Corner`!)*
    - **Blizzard of '96 Origin (`Saturday–Sunday, January 6–7, 1996`, Age 10 — `books/katie/e002/c001/p001–p002.md`)**: Dropped off Saturday morning before the storm, 10-year-old Brittany gets snowed and iced in at the Prairie house when the real-life **Blizzard of '96** hits Tidewater (several inches of wind-packed snow changing Saturday evening to driving sleet, freezing rain, 35-mph northeast Atlantic gales, coastal oceanfront flooding, and half an inch of solid glaze ice locking Kempsville roads shut). Shaken by Aunt Brenda and Uncle David shouting earlier and stranded away from her mom, Brittany sits curled anxiously at the end of the tan living-room sofa until **Katie and Nate open their blanket and invite Brittany into a three-way group cuddle for the very first time**—establishing the warm cuddling/hugging trio bond they carry into high school.
    - **Real-Life School-Bus vs. Walking-Zone Permission Mechanics (`Before SwiftChairs` vs. `After SwiftChairs`)**:
      1. **`9th & 10th Grade (1999–2001, Ambulatory + Regular School Bus)`**: VBCPS prohibits ad-hoc "bus passes" onto another student's bus, *but* because Brittany's house is inside Northwood High's **Walking Zone** right across the `Lions Way` / `Macgregory St` fence gate, no second bus seat is required. Regular high-school buses do not take afternoon roll call at `2:00 PM` dismissal, and David files a **standing Written Parental Alternate Dismissal / Walker Authorization** in Northwood's Main Office (with Brittany's mother listed as an authorized emergency contact). Whenever the twins don't want to take their regular yellow school bus home, all three cousins walk out together across `Lions Way` through the `Macgregory Street` fenceline gate to Brittany's house, spending afternoons cuddling, hugging, doing homework, and planning to live together as three when they grow up.
      2. **`11th & 12th Grade (Fall 2001–2003, SwiftChair Powerchairs + Special Needs Lift Bus + Impassable Curb Trap)`**: Once Katie and Nate enter their `SwiftChair` power wheelchairs in October 2001, **two things cut off after-school walks to Brittany's house simultaneously**: (a) **physically**, their `SwiftChairs` **cannot cross the uncut vertical concrete curb** at the `Macgregory Street` gate at all, so they can no longer go to Brittany's house from school, and (b) **administratively**, they now ride a **Special Needs Lift-Equipped School Bus** (`IEP / 504 Related Service Transportation` loading at `Stairway A`), where the Bus Driver and Bus Aide check every rider onto the hydraulic lift by clipboard manifest at `2:00 PM` (with David signing an **Unattended Home Self-Release Waiver** so the lift bus can drop the twins off at their ramped ranch house at `737 Arvin Rd`, while any off-campus parent/walker release requires formal pre-dismissal Main Office manifest sign-off so Special Needs Dispatch isn't alerted).
    - **11th-Grade Drift, Turning Down an Offered Cigarette, & the Failed Dec 31, 2002 New Year's Rescue**: Even though David still allows Brittany to remain part of the family after divorcing Brenda in August 2001, **in 11th grade Brittany drifts apart and falls in with the wrong crowd** (`Tyler` and hard-partying upperclassmen hanging out right outside the `Macgregory Street` gate at `Smoker's Corner`). When Brittany is **offered a cigarette by that crowd, she turns it down** (both keeping all visual/prose content 100% policy-safe and convincing Nate and Katie that the sweet cousin they loved is still in there somewhere!). Because of that glimmer of hope, when David drives nine hours north to **Newport, Rhode Island** for New Year's 2002/2003 to check on Grandma, Aunt Liz, and Chelsea, **Nate and Katie invite Brittany over on December 31, 2002 hoping to stage a rescue from the wrong crowd**—only to discover the cousin they loved is gone when she invites `Tyler`, `Jenna`, and 21-year-old `Derek` with gas-station alcohol into their living room (`books/katie/e004/c001/p001–p002.md`), leading to a fourteen-year estrangement.
    - **March 19, 2016 Redemption Arc (`Seeing Katie Rip Apart HB 663` $\rightarrow$ `Nate Saying "I Object!" at Brittany's Aborted Wedding` $\rightarrow$ `Coming Out of the Closet Into the Four-Person Cuddle Nest`)**:
      1. **February 2016 (`HB 663` Opens Brittany's Eyes)**: Fourteen years later, watching Nate's viral February 4, 2016 floor video of 30-year-old Delegate Katie Prairie fearlessly ripping apart `HB 663` opens Brittany's eyes to what she threw away and who her cousins truly are—right as one of those old wrong-crowd men (`Tyler`) is pressuring 30-year-old Brittany into marrying him.
      2. **Saturday, March 19, 2016 (`Nearest Weekend to Brittany's March 21st 31st Birthday, Right After the 2016 General Assembly Adjourns Sine Die on Friday, March 11, 2016`)**: At the wedding ceremony on **Saturday, March 19, 2016**, when the officiant says *"Speak now or forever hold your peace,"* **Nate speaks up and says, *"I object!"*** Realizing Nate is 100% right to object, Brittany breaks down in tears and tells the officiant right there at the altar to stop the wedding—declaring that **she wants to go live with her cousin, just as she promised him back in high school**!
      3. **Full-Time Four-Person Household (`Arvin Road` & `Sunset Drive`) & Coming Out of the Closet Into the Four-Person Nest**: From March 2016 onward, Brittany, Katie, Nate, and rescued Cousin Chelsea live together full-time (traveling together between `Arvin Road` in Virginia Beach and `Sunset Drive` in Alexandria during Senate sessions so the four of them are never separated). Although it takes months of patient convincing from Brittany and Chelsea to get Katie and Nate to stop sleeping in closets (`their trauma habit since August 2003`), once Katie and Nate finally settle into their custom wheelchair-transfer-height **four-person cuddle nest** platform bed with Chelsea and Brittany, **they sheepishly admit that sleeping on carpeted closet floors really *did* hurt**—and cuddling in that nest with the three women he loves (`Katie`, `Brittany`, and `Chelsea`) becomes Nate's favorite part of life (`books/katie/e011/c007/p001.md`).

- **Canonical Software Engineering & Coding Education Timeline (`1997–2026+`)**:
  1. **`1997` (7th Grade at Morrison Middle School, Age 12 — Learning `HTML` & Inspiring David to Attend `CCHR`)**:
     - In 1997, as twelve-year-old 7th graders at **Morrison Middle School** (*real-life Brandon Middle School*) while living at `1846 Delaney St`, Nate and Katie learn **`HTML`** and begin hand-coding their first web pages together.
     - Seeing his twelve-year-old twins building real web pages in HTML in 1997 is what directly **inspires their father David Prairie to go back to school at `CCHR` (*Community College of Hampton Roads*, Virginia Beach Campus)** to better himself and earn his computer science degree (buying the rented house at `737 Arvin Rd` from the landlord as his Driver's License primary residence until **March 2007**, when Nate and Katie's West Ocean View apartment lease ends and David lands his new job in **Tysons Corner**, prompting David to buy his second house at `1921 King St` / *"Sunset Drive"* in Alexandria and change his Driver's License primary residence to Alexandria while **keeping `737 Arvin Rd` in Virginia Beach** so the twins can keep their autistic routine in Hampton Roads).
  2. **`2001` (Age 16, Year of the *Sunward Tropics* Cruise — Learning `Quantum Basic 6.0` Alongside David)**:
     - In the summer and fall of 2001 (the year of the *Sunward Tropics* cruise, the parents' divorce and move to `737 Arvin Rd`, and their transition to full-time `SwiftChair` power wheelchairs in September 2001), Katie and Nate learn **`Quantum Basic 6.0`** alongside David—reading David's mylar-wrapped *Quantum Basic 5.0* library textbooks cover to cover to get a head start on his fall *Quantum Basic 6.0* course at CCHR (`books/katie/e003/c001/p001.md` & `e003/c002/p004.md`).
  3. **`March 4–16, 2007` (Age 21 — Twelve Unforgettable Days at `Roosevelt Rehabilitation Center [RRC]`, Walking Out With `Tina`, & Mastering `PHP` on Their Laptops)**:
     - In **February–March 2007** (`books/katie/e005/c002/p006.md`), after finishing their Associate of Applied Science (`A.A.S.`) degrees at **`CCHR`** (`December 2006`), Nate and Katie want to learn **server-side `PHP` and `SQL` databases** to outgrow hosted `BlogSphere` right as their West Ocean View apartment lease (`233 W. Ocean View Ave`) expires (`Wednesday, February 28, 2007`) and David lands his new systems engineering job in **Tysons Corner** (buying `1921 King St` / *"Sunset Drive"* in Alexandria while keeping `737 Arvin Rd` in Virginia Beach). Their CCHR Disability Support Services counselor (**Mrs. Evans**) points them to **`DARS`** (which neither twin has ever heard of before), and the regional DARS counselor on Newtown Road pitches **Roosevelt Rehabilitation Center (`RRC`, `243 Roosevelt Avenue, Fishersville, VA`)** and its **`Web Applications Developer` (`BIT-204`)** course with a glossy brochure showing a sunny mountain campus and a two-bed college-style dorm room in **Barton Hall**. **Crucial Expectation vs. Reality Rule**: Nate and Katie were never interested in the "rehabilitation" part of RRC—they were only ever interested in the **`Web Applications Developer` (`PHP` & `SQL`) class**, and fully intended to treat RRC just like going to college (*go to class, sleep in the dorm, and be done with it*). Looking at the two-bed dorm room in the brochure, they know colleges normally separate men and women so boyfriends and girlfriends don't have a messy breakup—so as twin brother and sister who have been at each other's side since they were little and lived together their entire lives except when separated by their abusers (`Tiffany` & `Brandon`), they assume RRC will let them share a room, agree to put up with two narrow `Twin XL` beds on paper, and plan to unroll their sleeping bag lengthwise inside a `3x6` dorm room closet and close the closet doors at bedtime! When Sunday check-in (`March 4, 2007`) hits like a bucket of ice water—putting them in separate wings with no opposite-gender room visits—Linda says the rule exists so male and female clients don't "hook up." Because of their autism, Nate and Katie don't know what the slang phrase "hook up" means and have to be told literally that it means to have sex—whereupon they earnestly tell Linda that they know brothers and sisters aren't supposed to have sex with each other (even though they don't understand why), so of course they won't have sex! They remain polite though unhappy at the counter until a staffer tries to grab Katie's wheelchair push-handles without asking and Katie goes off on him, after which they present to RRC staff as two aggressive opposite-gender siblings who refuse to follow orders to separate.
     - After **12 days (`Sunday, March 4 – Friday, March 16, 2007`, with Dad packing up their Barton Hall rooms on `Sunday, March 18, 2007`)**—having rushed the 2-week Vocational Evaluation in 1 week, remained stuck as **Level 2** clients in **Barton Hall** (since moving to Cantor Hall is part of earning Level 3), gotten written up on Tuesday night (`March 13`) for locking themselves inside a seasonal closet after Katie's pool assault, called the staffer's bluff over butt-in-seats funding, and watched the administration sweep Chris's Wednesday-night (`March 14`) assault of **Natalie** under the rug without ever holding their Thursday counselor write-up meeting—Katie and Nate copy Mr. Alderman's **`PHP`** and **`SQL`** curriculum onto their personal laptops, ride to **Luray** with **Tina** on Friday afternoon (`March 16`), and return home to **`737 Arvin Rd` in Virginia Beach** on Sunday (`March 18`). Because they walked out after 12 days without completing the program, **Nate and Katie are technically RRC dropouts** (`Status 28` unsuccessful closures in DARS's database, listing only their **Advanced Studies Diploma from Virginia Beach City Public Schools [Northwood High School]** and **Associate of Applied Science [`A.A.S.`] from the Community College of Hampton Roads** on their resumes and biographies), and not long after working on the copied modules unofficially from home for a few days, they decide to stop doing the RRC modules altogether and start doing their own thing.
  4. **`March 2007 – 2026+` (Returning to `737 Arvin Rd` in Kempsville, Buying `741 Lord Nelson Dr` 50–50 in `October 2019` When `CVTA Route 24` Launches, & 19+ Years of Production Websites & Desktop Applications)**:
     - Returning to their familiar brick ranch at **`737 Arvin Rd` off Military Highway & Indian River Road in Kempsville** in **March 2007** (preserving their autistic routine), Katie and Nate rebuild ***Ideas for Coastal Virginia Transit*** in custom **`PHP`**, film the viral 2008 four-bus mayoral loop (`#29` $\rightarrow$ `#20` $\rightarrow$ `#15` $\rightarrow$ `#12`), win elections to the **Virginia Beach City Council (`2010`)** and **Virginia House of Delegates (`2013`, `2015`, `2017`)**, and then in **October 2019—the exact month CVTA (`HRT`) launches the brand-new `Route 24 (Kempsville Rd / Witchduck Rd)` corridor**—purchase their second Virginia Beach home at **`741 Lord Nelson Dr, Virginia Beach, VA 23464`** **50–50 together**, spending nineteen-plus years building and maintaining a network of production websites and desktop applications (`HTML5`, `CSS3`, `JavaScript`, `PHP`, `Quantum Basic 6.0`, `Quantum Studio 2026`, `Quantum Code`).

- **The 2004–2005 Toxic Relationships (`Brandon Mercer` & `Tiffany Sloan`), the November 2005 Five-Bus Escape (`Age 20`), & The March 2007 Move to Alexandria / `RRC` `PHP` Arc (`Age 21–22`)**:
  - **First Post-High-School Apartment Together (`July 1, 2003 – Summer 2004`, Age 18–19 — `801 Greenway Ct, Apartment A6, Norfolk, VA`, Mirror Image Across the Hall from `Unit A5`, While David Still Lives at `737 Arvin Rd` in Virginia Beach)**:
    - After turning 18 on **April 8, 2003** and graduating from **Northwood High School** (*real-life Tallwood High School*) in **June 2003**, Katie and Nate move into their first accessible ground-floor studio apartment together on **July 1, 2003** at **`801 Greenway Ct, Apartment A6, Norfolk, VA 23507`** (in Norfolk's West Ghent neighborhood, paid for by their father **David Prairie** so his children can have a safe, independent place of their own now that they are 18 and no longer covered by the family-court custody order against their alcoholic mother **Brenda**), while David continues living nearby in Hampton Roads at **`737 Arvin Rd, Virginia Beach, VA 23464`** (where Katie still keeps her childhood dresser, extra clothes, TV/DVD player, and bedroom furniture).
    - **Canonical `801 Greenway Court` 20-Unit Identical Layout & `A5`/`A6` Mirror-Image Architecture** *(Full Universal Lore Bible & Google Street View Reference Images in [`universal-lore/locations/801-greenway-court/801-greenway-court.md`](file:///Users/michael/Developer/raggiesoft-servers/raggiesoft-narratives/universal-lore/locations/801-greenway-court/801-greenway-court.md))*:
      - Owned by husband-and-wife landlords **Dave and Deb**, `801 Greenway Court` is a two-story, flat-roofed red-brick apartment building containing **20 apartments (`A1–A10` downstairs on the 1st floor; `B1–B10` upstairs on the 2nd floor)**. The front glass-and-aluminum entrance door stays **locked 24/7** and requires the tenant's brass apartment key to enter the lobby (where Dave installs a nonslip aluminum modular ramp over the three shallow rubber-treaded lobby steps).
      - **All 20 apartments in the building share the exact same layout**, and **`Apartment A5`** and **`Apartment A6`** directly across the back first-floor hallway from each other are **exact left/right mirror images of each other**: an enlarged bedroom-style rectangular studio with a wide 3-pane rear window and a through-wall box A/C unit mounted through the brick facing the narrow rear alley behind the exterior black iron staircase; a full-sized bed and shared desktop computer workstation; a compact kitchenette with a 4-burner stove, single sink, and **under-the-counter dorm fridge** (where David buys and assembles a **standalone rolling utility cart for their microwave and toaster**); a **square walk-in closet** with a carpeted floor; and an accessible bathroom with a **roll-under sink**, **toilet**, and **standing shower** (where David sets up a padded **shower transfer bench**).
      - **Across-the-Hall Neighbors (`July 16, 2003` Crossover with `books/rachel`)**: Directly across the hall in **`Apartment A5`** (`July 15, 2003 – November 2004`) live their former Delaney Street and VDU Youth Summer Camp neighbor **Michael Ragsdale** and **Ashley Raybourn** (`books/katie/e004/c002/p001.md` $\leftrightarrow$ `books/rachel/e013/c004/p001.md`).
      - **Origin of the Closet Sleeping Bag Sanctuary (`August 9, 2003`, `books/katie/e004/c002/p002.md`) & Later Coming Out of the Closet Into the Four-Person Nest (`2016+`, `books/katie/e011/c007/p001.md`)**: When their estranged mother **Brenda** tracks them to `801 Greenway Court` on an August night, the locked 24/7 front entrance door blocks her from entering the hallway, forcing Brenda to walk down the narrow rear alley behind the black iron staircase and search for the rear first-floor window of **Apartment A6** above the humming box A/C unit. Terrified and hiding with the apartment lights off, Katie and Nate retreat into their **compact square closet**, climb inside their **single shared sleeping bag** together on the closet carpet with the door latched shut, and fall asleep cuddled up in each other's arms. From that night on for more than thirteen years (`including at RRC in March 2007`), **the compact square closet becomes their actual bedroom** (while their full-sized bed out in the main studio apartment rarely gets used)—until redeemed **Brittany** (`March 2016+`) and rescued **Chelsea** live safely under their roof and patiently convince Katie and Nate to come out of the closet into a custom wheelchair-transfer-height **four-person cuddle nest** platform bed (`where Katie and Nate finally admit sleeping on carpeted closet floors really did hurt!`).
      - **Hurricane Isabel, Two Layers of Sandbags at `801 Greenway Court`, Seeing Michael Sad Across the Hall Without Ashley, & Two Weeks at `737 Arvin Road` (`Wednesday, September 17 – Thursday, October 2, 2003`, `books/katie/e004/c002/p003.md` $\leftrightarrow$ `books/rachel/e013/c005/p001.md`)**:
        - **Crucial Autistic Touch Boundary Rule**: Neither Katie nor Nate likes to be touched by anyone they do not know—they gladly hold each other's hands across their wheelchair armrests, gladly hug each other tight inside their shared sleeping bag, and are completely comfortable when their father **David Prairie** helps maneuver their wheelchairs, while maintaining a strict no-touch buffer with everyone else.
        - **Evacuation Eve (`Wednesday, September 17, 2003`, `3:15 PM`, Overcast Clouds & Rising Wind Before the Overnight Rainbands)**: As **Hurricane Isabel** approaches and **CVTA** announces the multi-day suspension of all bus service across Norfolk and Virginia Beach, landlords **Dave and Deb** stack **two layers of heavy burlap sandbags** inside the front door at the top of the lobby ramp/steps at `801 Greenway Court`. Inside `Apartment A6`, David helps 18-year-old Katie and Nate lift their desktop computer tower and CRT monitor onto the full-sized bed mattress, unplug their microwave/toaster cart, and pack their `24V` `SwiftChair` chargers and shared navy-blue sleeping bag. Stepping into the first-floor hallway, they cross paths with 18-year-old **Michael Ragsdale** locking up **`Apartment A5`** across the hall. Both Katie and Nate see how sad and lonely Michael is now that **Ashley Raybourn** isn't there anymore (having left for college at the end of August, with Ellen picking up Archie for the storm)—and while they feel deep sympathy for him, they know they cannot help him. Passing the upstairs neighbor from **`Apartment B5`** (hauling supplies upstairs for a hurricane party), Michael walks up to Hampton Boulevard under overcast clouds and rising wind (before any rain begins) to ride **three CVTA buses (`CVTA Route 2` $\rightarrow$ `CVTA Route 6` $\rightarrow$ `CVTA Route 12`)** to **Kemps River Drive**, where his cousin **Maria** picks him up, takes him to **Crown Burger** for food before the overnight rainbands arrive, and brings him to her house to stay in the **downstairs bedroom sharing a twin bed with Maria**. Meanwhile, Katie and Nate ride with their dad in his wheelchair-accessible minivan to **`737 Arvin Road`** in Virginia Beach (which David already has registered on **Commonwealth Power's Medical Priority list** for their electric wheelchairs).
        - **Two-Day Power Outage at `737 Arvin Road`, Dead Wheelchair Batteries, Crawling on the Floor, Dad Crawling With Them at Eye Level, & Prescribing Collapsible Manual Wheelchairs With Joystick Attachments (`Sept 18–22, 2003`)**: Even with a Medical Priority Flag on `737 Arvin Road`, Hurricane Isabel knocks out the primary transmission and feeder lines across Kempsville, leaving `737 Arvin Road` without electricity for **two days (`Thursday afternoon, Sept 18 – Saturday evening, Sept 20`)**. By Friday afternoon (`Sept 19`), the `24V` batteries in both of their nearly 200-lb `SwiftChair` powerchairs die completely. Forced to slide out of their chairs and crawl on their hands and knees to the bathroom, kitchen, and living room, 18-year-old Katie and Nate feel deeply humiliated and undignified. Seeing their distress, **David brings the sofa cushions, battery lantern, cooler of ginger ale, yellow legal pads, and fictional board/card games (`Solo`, `WordSmith`, `Real Estate Baron`, `Stage 10`, `Suspect`) down to the living-room carpet—and then drops to his own hands and knees and crawls with the twins wherever they go in the house so he stays at eye level with them!** Because neither Katie nor Nate has the wrist or upper-arm strength to push manual hand-rims far on their own, they and David work out that what they need—and what **Dr. Evelyn Carter** prescribes first thing Monday morning (`Sept 22`) through her seating clinic—are **two lightweight, collapsible cross-brace manual wheelchairs outfitted with right-armrest joystick power-drive attachments and removable under-seat battery packs** (giving them joystick self-propulsion, spare backup batteries, flat trunk folding when the battery box is unclipped, and freewheel manual pushability via the rear push-handles if every battery dies—the exact backup chairs kept at `737 Arvin Road` that they later bring to **Roosevelt Rehabilitation Center [`RRC`]** in March 2007 and collapse into Tina's compact car trunk for the Luray trip!).
        - **Stranded Wheel-less on Saturday Afternoon (`Sept 20`), Recharging Overnight, Assessing Damages at `801 Greenway Court` on Sunday (`Sept 21` — `The Sandbags Saved the Building!`), Grocery Shopping With Dad, & Setting Up a `Medical Priority Flag` for `Apartment A6` (`Sept 20 – Oct 2, 2003`)**: When landlords Dave and Deb message tenants at `1:30 PM` on **Saturday afternoon (`Sept 20`)** that the floodwaters around `801 Greenway Court` have receded, **Katie and Nate cannot even leave the living-room carpet to go inspect `Apartment A6`** because power has not yet returned to `737 Arvin Road`, both 200-lb `SwiftChairs` have dead batteries, and they do not yet have backup folding manual chairs (proving in real time why they urgently need them). Once the **Commonwealth Power** Medical Priority bucket truck restores electricity to `737 Arvin Road` at `6:15 PM` that **Saturday evening (`Sept 20`)** and both `SwiftChairs` recharge to 100% overnight, David drives the twins in their fully recharged `SwiftChairs` to `801 Greenway Court` on **Sunday (`Sept 21`)** to assess damages. Rolling up the front walkway in their charged powerchairs, they find out that while floodwater poured into the lower lobby and reached the lobby mailboxes on Thursday, **the two layers of sandbags at the top of the lobby ramp saved the building** (`A5` and `A6` are 100% dry, while the neighbor in `Apartment B5` reports that staying in the building meant being trapped on an **island**). After visiting Dr. Carter on Monday morning (`Sept 22`) to order their joystick-outfitted collapsible manual chairs and going **grocery shopping** with Dad on Wednesday (`Sept 24`), they head back to `801 Greenway Court` with groceries expecting to move back in—only to discover that even though **Tidewater General Hospital** (*Norfolk General Hospital*, the region's only **Level 1 Trauma Center** right across the way) has full power, **the power failure at `801 Greenway Court` is still in progress** because the building's grid is reportedly attached to the flooded **Midtown Tunnel** (`two full weeks` without power). Realizing the Medical Priority Flag had only been on the Arvin Road account, **David helps Katie and Nate get a Medical Priority Flag set up with Commonwealth Power for `801 Greenway Court, Apartment A6`** so an extended outage never happens again, and they stay at Arvin Road until power returns on October 2, 2003 (with their two backup collapsible manual wheelchairs with joystick attachments delivered and stored in reserve at `737 Arvin Road` by November 2003).
      - **How Nate Meets Tiffany Sloan & Why Katie Approves at First (`May 14–15, 2004`, `books/katie/e004/c003/p001–p002.md`)**:
        - On **Friday, May 14, 2004** (right after 19-year-old Nate and Katie finish their freshman spring finals at **`CCHR` Norfolk Campus**), they celebrate at **`Chapters & Co.` (*Barnes & Noble*)** inside **The Admiral Center** (*MacArthur Center*). While Katie is ordering two iced cocoas at the café counter, Nate stretches up from his `SwiftChair` in the Computer Books aisle to reach a thick paperback (*Cascading Style Sheets & Dynamic Web Design*), and a second copy tips off the high shelf—where 20-year-old **Tiffany Sloan** (accompanied by her seemingly warm mother **Sandra Sloan**) catches the falling book in midair, laughs (*"Whoa—careful! Death by cascading style sheets is a terrible way to start the weekend"*), and asks Nate about hand-coding `HTML` for her home webpage.
        - When Katie rolls up with their iced cocoas, Tiffany wins Katie's complete approval by moving two heavy café chairs out of the way for their wheelchairs without being asked, praising how close Nate and Katie are (*"If a guy isn't close with his sister, that's a giant red flag in my book!"*), walking them a block north to the **Monticello & Charlotte `CVTA Route #2` bus stop** during a sudden May cloudburst while holding her umbrella directly over Nate's electronic joystick controller (while Sandra shields Katie's joystick with her golf umbrella), and visiting **Apartment A6** the next afternoon (**Saturday, May 15, 2004**) with extra-cheese pizza, ginger ale (remembering their no-alcohol/no-coffee rule), and a `3.5"` floppy disk of her `HTML` file—kneeling on the carpet between their wheelchairs at eye level and leading Katie to give Nate her heartfelt twin-sister blessing (*"I really like her, Nate... you deserve someone sweet and patient who makes you happy"*).
  - **How Brandon Mercer & Tiffany Sloan Manipulated the Twins into Separating & Dropping Out (`Summer 2004 – November 2005`, Age 19–20)**:
    - Because both Katie and Nate are autistic, they take people at their word and trust easily when someone shows them intense affection.
    - In **Summer 2004** (Age 19), **Tiffany Sloan** (heavy-set build, shoulder-length dark reddish-brown hair with bangs, black-rimmed rectangular glasses) rushes Nate into a relationship at **`233 W. Ocean View Ave, Apt. #3, Norfolk, VA 23503`** (the ground-floor apartment at the far end of the white-painted `233` courtyard building at the **Sea Breeze West** apartments [*real-life Sea Isle West at 233/237 W. Ocean View Ave*]), manipulating Nate into dropping out of school, isolating him in West Ocean View, and trashing the apartment into a filthy, garbage-stuffed disaster (even piling foul-smelling dirty pots and pans inside the grab-bar bathtub and forbidding milk in the refrigerator under a fake claim of lactose intolerance while eating chocolate ice cream).
    - **The Failed "Brother & Sister Weekend" & Katie's *"Speak Now or Forever Hold Your Peace"* Oath (`September 2004`, Age 19 — Before Katie Meets Brandon Mercer)**:
      - Before Katie ever meets Brandon Mercer, nineteen-year-old Katie rides **`CVTA Route 1`** to **Ocean View Station** for a planned brother-and-sister weekend with just Nate at Apartment 3 (intending to sleep on the bright green three-cushion sofa beneath the front mini-blinds).
      - When Tiffany throws a jealous fit over Nate hugging his own twin sister (*"I am his girlfriend!"*), Katie snaps *"Don't yell at me like that!"*, takes Nate by the hand, growls ***"My brother only!"*** at Tiffany, and rolls out into the courtyard with Nate.
      - Outside on the courtyard walk, Katie warns an overwhelmed, tearful Nate that Tiffany is hurting him and swears a solemn twin oath: ***"If she ever pops The Question, I am telling you right now. When they say 'Speak Now or Forever Hold Your Peace,' I will be speaking up. It is my duty as your twin sister to protect you, and no, I never thought it would come down to this."*** (Under Virginia marriage law [`Code of Virginia § 20-89.1` & `§ 20-45.1`], an objection alleging that an autistic groom is being coerced and isolated against his will is a legal impediment requiring the officiant to immediately halt the ceremony and separate the groom into a private room away from the bride—where Nate would immediately ask Katie to take him home.)
      - **How Katie Meets Brandon Mercer in Norfolk, Moves to Williamsburg, & Nate's Christmas 2004 Trip to Newport, Rhode Island with Cousin Chelsea (`September 19 – December 27, 2004`, `books/katie/e005/c001/p002–p004.md`)**:
        - **Meeting Brandon at The Admiral Center (`Sunday, September 19, 2004`, `books/katie/e005/c001/p002.md`)**: On the day after Tiffany ruins their Brother & Sister Weekend at Sea Breeze West, while Katie is sitting on the patio of the bakery café at **The Admiral Center** in Norfolk, she meets 22-year-old **Brandon Mercer**—who is visiting Norfolk from Williamsburg (`224 Virginia Ave, Williamsburg, VA 23185`, near the **College of Charles & Anne [`C&A`]***) for the day and steps in to wipe off her table and buy her a fresh cup of hot chocolate and a bottle of flavored water after a hurried shopper bumps her table.
        - **How Brandon Presents Himself (`Zero Crystal Balls`)**: When Nate rides the `CVTA Route 1` bus down from Ocean View to apologize to Katie for snapping at her the night before, Brandon presents himself as a warm, level-headed gentleman. He immediately tells Nate that he already assured Katie last night's ruined weekend **wasn't Nate's fault** (explaining that any good guy gets blindsided when a girlfriend suddenly changes the rules after a lease is signed, and praising Nate for riding the bus down to check on his sister). When Brandon invites Katie to come visit Williamsburg and she tells him honestly that she wants to **slowly get to know him in Norfolk first**, Brandon respects her boundary completely—driving down to Norfolk every weekend at her pace through September, October, and November 2004 and winning over Katie, Nate, and their dad David.
        - **Katie's Move to Williamsburg & The "Honeymoon Freeze-Out" Intimacy Boundary (`Saturday, November 20, 2004`, `books/katie/e005/c001/p003.md`)**:
          - In **November 2004** (right after Michael Ragsdale moves out of `Apartment A5` across the hall at `801 Greenway Court`, leaving Katie alone at the back of the first floor before Thanksgiving and Christmas), Brandon gently convinces a lonely Katie to move out of `Apartment A6` into his step-free ground-floor apartment at `224 Virginia Ave` in Williamsburg.
          - **Canonical Relationship Intimacy Rule (`Option 3 — The "Honeymoon Freeze-Out"`)**: When Nate and Katie moved in with Tiffany (`August 2004`) and Brandon (`November 2004`), both twins genuinely believed they were in love, shared a bed with their partners, kissed, and cuddled—and naturally expected that once they were settled and ready, their relationships would eventually take the next step the way adult couples do. However, **neither relationship ever became sexual** for two reasons working together: (1) before separating, Nate and Katie made a mutual protective pact not to rush into anything sexual that could risk an unplanned pregnancy or health complication while living apart for the first time in their lives; and (2) almost immediately after the leases were signed at `Sea Breeze West` and `224 Virginia Ave`, Tiffany and Brandon's masks began slipping into laziness, mess, criticism, and control, freezing out romantic warmth before either twin ever felt safe enough to cross that line.
        - **Christmas 2004 in Newport, Rhode Island (`Friday, December 24 – Monday, December 27, 2004`, `books/katie/e005/c001/p004.md` — *The Worst Christmas Ever*, *The Christmas Eve Nightmare*, & *Four Days on Dirty Old Tonomy Hill*)**:
          - Because Aunt Liz died from smoking and Grandma also passed away earlier that year, Christmas 2004 is 22-year-old **Chelsea's** very first Christmas without her mother in **Newport, Rhode Island** (David Prairie's birthplace). When David pays for Nate's bus trip up to Newport so Chelsea won't be alone for Christmas, Tiffany refuses to let Katie come along and insists on accompanying Nate herself.
          - **Exact 18-Stop Northbound `Pathfinder` (*Greyhound*) & `OSTA` (*Ocean State Transit Authority*) Routing (`December 23–24, 2004`)**:
            1. **Norfolk, VA** (Start) $\rightarrow$ **Hampton, VA** $\rightarrow$ **Williamsburg, VA** $\rightarrow$ **Richmond, VA** *(change buses)*
            2. **Richmond, VA** $\rightarrow$ **Fredericksburg, VA** $\rightarrow$ **Springfield, VA** $\rightarrow$ **Washington, D.C.** *(change buses)*
            3. **Washington, D.C.** $\rightarrow$ **Baltimore, MD (`Downtown Baltimore` terminal at `210 W. Fayette Street` off `I-395`)** $\rightarrow$ **Newark, DE** $\rightarrow$ **Philadelphia, PA** $\rightarrow$ **New York, NY (`Port Authority Bus Terminal`)** *(change buses)*
            4. **New York, NY** $\rightarrow$ **Bridgeport, CT** $\rightarrow$ **New Haven, CT** $\rightarrow$ **Braxton, RI** $\rightarrow$ **Providence, RI** $\rightarrow$ **Boston, MA (`South Station`)** *(change buses)*
            5. **Boston, MA** $\rightarrow$ **Fall River, MA** $\rightarrow$ **Newport, RI (`Newport Gateway Center`)** *(change to **`OSTA Route 63`** up Broadway to **Dirty Old Tonomy Hill**, which in 2004 was still the old World War II–era public housing buildings)*.
          - **The Shower Interruption, The Multi-Day Stay (`Dec 24–27, 2004`), & Why Nate Never Stops Chelsea's Affection**:
            - When Chelsea hugs Nate in tears on her first Christmas without Aunt Liz, Tiffany reacts with immediate disgust (*"Another girl"* / *"You like my boyfriend?"*). While Chelsea is taking a shower, Tiffany screams at Nate in the living room; distressed by the sound of the screaming and fearing for her cousin's safety far more than her modesty, Chelsea runs out of the shower soaking wet and completely naked (*"Why are you two fighting?"*). Remembering from childhood that the family constantly had to remind Chelsea to stay dressed, Nate isn't shocked, keeps his eyes respectfully averted, wraps a thick dry bath towel around Chelsea's shoulders to protect her dignity, and guides her back into the bathroom as she takes a puff of her rescue inhaler and whimpers, *"I wish it was just you and Katie."*
            - That night after Christmas Eve dinner, when Tiffany spitefully accuses Chelsea of having a crush on Nate (*"First you have a sister and now a cousin. I'm starting to wonder where your loyalties are!"*), a sobbing, wheezing Chelsea pulls Nate into her bedroom, slams and locks the door in Tiffany's face (*"Just him!"*), starts pulling off her clothes for bed while Nate pulls the quilt up over her shoulders, and begs through heavy tears: ***"Promise you will never bring her again. Only Katie! Tiffany's mean to you and me too!"***
            - Across their multi-day stay (`Friday, December 24 – Monday, December 27, 2004`), because Chelsea does not want to hear Tiffany and Nate fighting in the living room, **Chelsea insists every night that Nate climb into bed with her behind her locked bedroom door** (with Nate staying fully dressed on top of the sheet under the edge of the quilt while she clings to his arm), leaving Tiffany out on the living-room couch, furious. Because of Chelsea's developmental disability, she does not understand what makes family love different from romantic love; Nate had never once in his life stopped Chelsea from hugging or kissing him in the past, and refuses to start pushing her away now because he doesn't want to scare his grieving cousin who doesn't understand how life works sometimes.
            - **AI-Only Lore Context Note *(Never Stated in Narrative Prose)***: Because of her developmental disability, Chelsea does not understand that as the tenant of the apartment, she actually has the legal power to throw Tiffany out of her apartment at any time.
          - **Exact Southbound Return Routing via the `West Main Road` Walking Path, `OSTA Route 60`, & `Pathfinder NYC Express` (`Monday, December 27, 2004`)**:
            - **Bypassing the 5-Minute Fall River Connection**: Nate and Tiffany's return **Pathfinder** tickets are printed starting in **Newport, RI $\rightarrow$ Fall River, MA *(change buses, where the connecting bus to Providence departs only 5 minutes later!)* $\rightarrow$ Providence, RI**. Knowing they will miss a 5-minute transfer in Fall River with Nate's `SwiftChair` wheelchair lift unloading, **Chelsea directs Nate and Tiffany to take the paved walking path from Tonomy Hill over to `West Main Road` and catch `OSTA Route 60` straight to `Providence (Kennedy Plaza)`**.
            - **Providence (`Kennedy Plaza`) Southward**: Board **`Pathfinder's NYC Express`** (as scheduled on their ticket from Newport, RI) $\rightarrow$ **New York, NY (`Port Authority`, change buses)** $\rightarrow$ **Philadelphia, PA** $\rightarrow$ **Wilmington, DE** $\rightarrow$ **Baltimore, MD (`Baltimore Travel Plaza` off `I-95` on O'Donnell Street)** $\rightarrow$ **Washington, D.C. (`change buses`)** $\rightarrow$ **Fredericksburg, VA** $\rightarrow$ **Richmond, VA (`change buses`)** $\rightarrow$ **Williamsburg, VA** *(where Nate looks out the coach window at the Williamsburg Transportation Center two blocks from `224 Virginia Ave` and seriously considers getting off the bus right there to go see Katie, before staying on beside Tiffany)* $\rightarrow$ **Hampton, VA** $\rightarrow$ **Norfolk, VA** *(transfer to **`CVTA` bus** [`Route 1`] to get back home to Ocean View)*.
            - **Canonical "What If" Lore Note (`Williamsburg Stop on December 27, 2004`)**: If Nate had actually gotten off that southbound Pathfinder coach in Williamsburg on December 27, 2004, Tiffany would have left him right then and there; Nate would have rolled straight over to `224 Virginia Ave` to see Katie, an angry Brandon Mercer would have tossed Katie out on the spot, and the twin siblings would have been reunited eleven months earlier!
  - **Architectural Floor Plan of `233 W. Ocean View Ave, Apt. #3` (*Sea Breeze West* — ~`600 sq. ft.` Far-End Courtyard Unit)**:
