Across the courtyard in the `237` building sits the complex's **quarter-operated laundry room**, with the complex **dumpsters** located out back.
    - **Combination Living Room & Open Kitchen (Front to Back)**:
      - **Living Room (Front)**: Entering through the front wooden door (immediately to the right of the double front windows with white horizontal mini-blinds overlooking the small courtyard), the **bright kelly-green three-cushion sofa** sits directly beneath the window blinds, with the **oval weathered-wood coffee table** in front of it and the **black CRT television on a two-shelf black-and-glass stand** in the front-left corner.
      - **Repurposed Dining Table on the Left (`Quantum OS XN Pro` 2004 Desktop Tower + Salvaged 1990s Drives + 17-Inch Off-White CRT Monitor)**: On the **far-left side** between the living room and the kitchen sits a large table meant to be a dining table, which Nate uses as a computer desk for his **`Quantum OS XN Pro`** (*real-life Windows XP Professional*) workstation: an older **17-inch CRT monitor with 1990s off-white plastic molding**, paired with a **computer tower that is new as of 2004** and fitted with **salvaged off-white 3.5-inch floppy, CD-ROM, and CD-RW drives** from Nate and Katie's older desktop computer.
      - **Open Kitchen (Straight Ahead from the Front Door Against the Solid Interior Wall)**: Directly ahead of the living room under a hanging amber cone pull-chain ceiling lamp sits the open kitchen flush against the **solid windowless interior wall** (the wall that has the bathroom and hallway closet on the other side): **olive-green base cabinets with speckled laminate surface prep counters**, matching **olive-green wooden upper kitchen wall cabinets** (upgrading the original Sims 3 screenshot), a **slate-blue 4-burner stove/oven with an under-cabinet stove exhaust range hood above it**, a **kitchen sink**, and a **full-size refrigerator and freezer (with the freezer on top)**. The left wall above the computer monitor/desk is bare painted drywall (the A/C temperature controller is located in the back bedroom, never on the living-room/kitchen wall).
    - **Hallway (Straight Ahead on the Far Right) & The Dual-Purpose Hall Closet / Autistic Deep-Pressure Sanctuary (`233_w_ocean_view_ave_apt3_floorplan_diagram.png`)**:
      - **Hallway Geometry & Two White Bi-Folding Closet Doors (on the Left Wall of the Hallway)**: The hallway is a straight corridor along the far right connecting the combined living room/kitchen to the bedroom. **Both the right wall of the hallway and the wall straight ahead at the end of the hallway are solid exterior walls!** On the **left wall of the hallway** (when looking from the living room) is the **hallway closet**, enclosed by **two white bi-folding doors that open outward toward the hallway** (facing the right exterior wall). There are **no other doors in the hallway** (the bathroom entrance is around the left corner inside the bedroom), and at the end of the hallway there is **no door and no doorway frame at all**—you are simply in the bedroom! Once Tiffany is kicked out, Katie and Nate set up the **upper half of the hallway closet closest to the kitchen as a pantry** (with shelving Dad helps install) and the **upper half closer to the bedroom as a coat closet**. They keep the **floor of the hallway closet completely clear** and lay down a **sleeping bag and pillows** on the closet floor—creating a dark, quiet, enclosed **deep-pressure sibling-cuddle sanctuary** whenever the two autistic twins are overwhelmed and craving confined space and deep-pressure hugs (*"Bear and Bug"*).
    - **Back Bedroom (No Door or Doorway — Far-Corner Queen Bed, Back Exterior Window, & Through-Wall A/C Unit)**:
      - Reaching the end of the hallway, there is **no door or doorway**—the space opens directly to the left into the back bedroom.
      - **Back Exterior Wall (Right to Left)**: Straight ahead of the hallway on the back exterior wall sits the **through-wall box A/C unit** (controlled by a **wall temperature switch / thermostat mounted on the interior wall directly opposite the A/C unit**). To the left of the A/C unit in the center of the back exterior wall is the **bedroom window** with white horizontal mini-blinds.
      - **Queen Bed Pushed Against the Far-Left Corner Wall (For Wheelchair Access to the Bathroom)**: While in real life the queen bed was centered against the left-side wall leaving walking space on both sides, in Katie and Nate's version the **Queen-sized bed** (outfitted with all-new sheets, comforter, and pillows bought by Katie at `Chesapeake Bay Mall`) is **pushed flush into the far-left corner against the corner walls** specifically so their compact `SwiftChair` power wheelchairs have wide, unobstructed floor clearance to roll to the bathroom entrance!
    - **Bathroom (Entered from the Bedroom; Door Opens Inward into the Bathroom to the Left)**:
      - Turning left from the end of the hallway into the bedroom, the **bathroom entrance door sits on the bedroom's interior wall** (separating the bedroom from the central bathroom block). The bathroom door **opens inward into the bathroom from the bedroom and hinges to the left**, leading into the compact bathroom equipped with a **sink**, **toilet**, and a **bathtub with steel grab bars** (where Tiffany had disgustingly stacked all the foul-smelling dirty pots and pans).
  - **Katie's Five-Bus Escape from `Williamsburg Transportation Center` (`North Boundary Street`) to `233 W. Ocean View Ave, Apt. #3` (Norfolk) (`Saturday, November 12, 2005`, `3:55 PM – 8:20 PM`, Age 20, `books/katie/e005/c001/p005.md` [`pov: "Katie"`] & `e005/c002/p001.md` [`pov: "Nathaniel"`])**:
    - In **November 2005**, when 23-year-old **Brandon Mercer's** controlling, abusive behavior peaks—marching twenty-year-old Katie (with her state ID card, birth certificate folder, daily maintenance medication pouch, wallet, and flip phone inside her canvas messenger bag) to the **Williamsburg Transportation Center** on North Boundary Street in the pouring rain to buy one-way **ARTS** rail and tunnel-bus vouchers through Washington and Toledo up to **Windsor, Canada**—Brandon flips her rear motor-brake levers to park and switches off her joystick controller under the station awning before stepping inside the waiting room queue so the clerk can manually print the multi-city vouchers.
    - Seizing that ninety-second window when the `4:00 PM` **Colonial Triangle Transit Authority (`CTTA`) Grey Line** bus (*`GREY LINE — NEWPORT NEWS / LEE HALL`*) pulls into the north bay forty feet away, Katie powers her joystick controller back on, drives up the kneeling ramp, watches through the rain-streaked window as Brandon walks back out of the depot doors holding the yellow paper ARTS ticket jacket and stares in shock at the empty brick spot, and holds down the side volume button to silence `BRANDON MERCER — CALLING` as she executes a grueling, timetable-verified five-bus transit escape across the Peninsula and through the Hampton Roads Bridge-Tunnel to reach Nate in Norfolk (archived schedules in `~/Developer/ai-staging/bus-schedules/cvta/2005/`):
      1. **`4:00 PM – 4:30 PM` — `CTTA Grey Line` *(Colonial Triangle Transit Authority Grey Line, now Route 1 — real-life WAT Grey Line)* from `Williamsburg Transportation Center` to `Lee Hall`**:
         - Departs **Williamsburg Transportation Center** at **`4:00 PM`** and arrives at **Lee Hall (`Warwick Blvd. & Elmhurst Dr.`, Transfer Point `T1`)** at **`4:30 PM`** for the scheduled handover from CTTA to CVTA.
      2. **`4:30 PM – 4:45 PM` — `CVTA Route 116` (*Mall-Hall Loop / Lee Hall – Peninsula Commons Mall Loop*) from `Lee Hall` to `Warwick Blvd. & Denbigh Blvd.`**:
         - Departs **Lee Hall (`T1`)** at **`4:30 PM`** and arrives at the corner of **Warwick Boulevard & Denbigh Boulevard (`Newport Crossing Shopping Center`, Transfer Point `T2` / `T9`)** at **`4:45 PM`**.
      3. **`4:45 PM – 5:30 PM` — Skipping the `5:00 PM` `CVTA Route 106` for Dinner & Restroom Break at `Crown Burger` (`Warwick & Denbigh`)**:
         - **`CVTA Route 106`** is due at **Warwick & Denbigh (`T9`)** at **`5:00 PM`**, but Katie is hungry after fleeing Williamsburg and needs a clean restroom. She goes inside the **Crown Burger** (*real-life Burger King*) at the corner of Warwick & Denbigh to get something warm to eat and use the restroom.
      4. **`5:30 PM – 6:10 PM` — `CVTA Route 107` (Towards `6th Street & Ivy Avenue`) from `Warwick & Denbigh` to `Newport News Transit Center`**:
         - Connects with southbound **`CVTA Route 107`** at **Warwick & Denbigh (`T9`)** at **`5:30 PM`**, arriving at **Newport News Transit Center (`T2` / `T8`)** at **`6:10 PM`**.
         - **The Disgusting `Newport News Transit Center` Restroom (`6:10 PM – 6:45 PM` Layover)**: During her 35-minute wait inside the Newport News Transit Center building, Katie uses the women's restroom—which is just as horrific and filthy as the Hampton Transit Center men's restroom documented in `~/Developer/ai-staging/bus-schedules/cvta/2005/htc-men-restroom/` (half the mirror ripped off the wall above the sinks leaving bare plywood covered in black mastic glue blobs, a broken metal toilet paper dispenser hanging crookedly off the stall divider by a single bolt, vulgar marker graffiti scrawled across the beige stall doors and white ceramic tiles, corroded partition bottoms, and unflushed toilets).
      5. **`6:45 PM – 7:19 PM*` — The LAST `CVTA Route 61` (*Crossroads Route — Expressways*) of the Night from `Newport News Transit Center` to `Wards Corner`**:
         - Boards the **LAST `Route 61` of the night** departing **Newport News Transit Center (`T8`)** at **`6:45 PM`** (`6:57 PM` at Hampton Transit Center `T5`), crossing the **Hampton Roads Bridge-Tunnel (`HRBT`)** into Norfolk and arriving at **Wards Corner (`Granby St. & Little Creek Rd.`, Time Point `4`)** at **`7:19 PM*`** (where the `*` on the 2005 timetable indicates an **Expressways** trip that terminates in Downtown Norfolk at `7:34 PM*` instead of continuing to Virginia Beach).
      6. **`8:00 PM – 8:13 PM` — `CVTA Route #1` (*Granby Street / Ocean View*) from `Wards Corner` to `Duffy's Lane` & The Final Roll Up `Portview Avenue`**:
         - Connects with northbound **`CVTA Route #1` (*Granby Street*)** departing **Wards Corner (`T5`)** at **`8:00 PM`** and arriving at **`Duffy's Lane` (`Ocean View Ave. & Granby St.`, Time Point `6`)** at **`8:13 PM`**.
         - From **Duffy's Lane** at **`8:13 PM`**, soaking wet in the cold November rain, Katie rolls across the shopping center parking lot and goes **half a block north up `Portview Avenue`**, where **`233 West Ocean View Avenue` (*Sea Breeze West*)** sits on her right on Portview Avenue, and **Apartment #3** is the **first door on Katie's right**, knocking on Nate's door around **`8:20 PM`**.
  - **Rescuing Nate from Tiffany Sloan, Heavy-Duty Cleaning of the Trashed Apartment, & Living Together Until the Lease Ends (`November 2005 – March 2007`, Age 20–21)**:
    - Arriving soaking wet at `233 W. Ocean View Ave` in **November 2005**, Katie discovers that Tiffany Sloan has Nate trapped in a filthy, garbage-choked apartment—and the very next morning, Tiffany jealously confronts Nate for comforting his traumatized twin sister (*"You seem to be cuddling with Katie more than me. Do you not love me?"*), forcing an ultimatum.
    - Choosing his twin sister without hesitation, Nate and Katie kick Tiffany Sloan out that morning (*"Go on! Get out of here! Stay away from me, and stay away from my brother! Don't ever come back!"*).
    - Over lunch at **Crown Burger** and a heavy-duty multi-hour deep clean of the filthy apartment (hauling bags of rotting trash to the back dumpsters, clearing the foul-smelling pots and pans out of the bathtub, scrubbing the bathroom and kitchen, and adopting Katie's *"wash dishes immediately as we go"* rule), Katie moves in with Nate until the apartment lease expires (`November 2005 – March 2007`).
    - On Monday morning, Katie and Nate ride **`CVTA Route 1`** (`8:13 AM` from Ocean View Station near the Burger Arch, a 90-minute ride to **`CCHR` Virginia Beach Campus**, noting that the last bus back from CCHR leaves at `5:45 PM`) to re-enroll in college classes together (registering for *United States History* together at CCHR Beach Campus), take **`CVTA Route 29`** to **`Chesapeake Bay Mall`** (*Lynnhaven Mall*) to buy all-new queen bedding, and meet their father **David Prairie** (still living in Virginia Beach at `737 Arvin Rd` with his pickup truck). David helps them load Katie's dresser, clothes, TV/DVD player, and laptop from `737 Arvin Rd`, takes them on a full grocery run to restock the apartment with milk, cereal, produce, and deli honey ham, and stands guard outside `233 W. Ocean View Ave` when Tiffany and her mother pull up in their van—ordering them to take their bagged belongings, hand over the apartment key, and never come near his family again.
    - **Wednesday, November 16, 2005 (`2:00 PM`) — The `Goyeau Street` CBSA / Detroit CBP `TECS` Refusal File at the Walter E. Hoffman U.S. Courthouse (`books/katie/e005/c002/p003.md`)**:
      - On Wednesday afternoon, November 16, 2005, Dad accompanies Katie and Nate on `CVTA Route 1` to the **Walter E. Hoffman United States Courthouse** (`600 Granby Street`, Downtown Norfolk) to give her statement to a Williamsburg Police detective and **Special Agent Morales** (`U.S. Attorney's Office, EDVA`).
      - When Agent Morales checks the federal **Treasury Enforcement Communications System (`TECS`)**, he discovers that Brandon Mercer boarded the northbound evening train alone on Saturday night to avoid losing his non-refundable tickets and rode the **Windsor Transit Tunnel Bus** on Sunday afternoon, November 13, 2005 (`4:15 PM`) to the **Canadian Border Services Agency (`CBSA`) inspection plaza at `555 Goyeau Street` (at `Wyandotte Street East`) and the CBSA Windsor Tunnel District Operations building at `35 Park Street East` in Windsor, Ontario**.
      - **Permanent Guardrail for Windsor, Ontario & Detroit, Michigan Port of Entry Geography**:
        - **NEVER mention, output, or reference `"Ouellette Avenue"` (or the surname `"Ouellette"`) anywhere in the text, outlines, notes, or background descriptions.** The author's real-life ex is named Amy Marie Ouellette, and seeing the name `"Ouellette"` appear unexpectedly on the page is a severe autistic nervous-system trigger and trauma shock for the author.
        - **Approved Windsor Port of Entry Geography Only**: Anchor all descriptions of the Canadian tunnel plaza and surrounding downtown Windsor streets exclusively to the actual adjacent border-plaza streets: **Goyeau Street** (specifically `555 Goyeau Street`, the Detroit-Windsor Tunnel plaza), **Park Street East** (specifically `35 Park Street East`, the CBSA Windsor Tunnel District Operations building), and **Wyandotte Street East**.
        - **Approved Detroit Port of Entry Geography**: On the American side of the tunnel, use **East Jefferson Avenue**, **Randolph Street**, and **Cass Avenue** (for the men's shelter where the U.S. Marshals later arrest Brandon).
        - Before outputting any passage set in Windsor or Detroit, run an automatic check to verify that the string `"Ouellette"` is 100% absent.
      - Flagged at Primary Podium Two for his agitated body language, fifty-pound duffel bag, and the yellow two-person ARTS ticket jacket bearing `PRAIRIE, K. — ADA WHEELCHAIR SPACE RESERVED`, Brandon was sent to Secondary Inspection with **CBSA Officer Vance**, found with `$34.00` in cash, `$4.12` on his Williamsburg ATM receipt, and printed Ontario relocation notes, refused entry under **IRPA Sections 20 and 39**, transported back through the tunnel in a **caged CBSA enforcement van** after losing his temper, searched again by **U.S. Customs and Border Protection (`CBP`)** at **East Jefferson Avenue and Randolph Street in Detroit** under **`19 U.S.C. § 1582`**, and dumped in the 38°F Detroit rain at `5:30 PM` Sunday—ending up at a men's homeless shelter on **Cass Avenue** in Detroit where U.S. Marshals arrest him on the felony warrant.
      - Agent Morales explains the bottom line of the CBP printout—**`Protected Non-Traveling Complainant (V-W, Zero Infraction)`**—word-by-word to satisfy Katie's literal autistic need for certainty that she broke zero laws and has a 100% clean international border/passport record.
  - **Quietly Launching *Ideas for Coastal Virginia Transit* on `BlogSphere` (`November 27, 2005`, Inspired by the *Seattle Transit Blog*)**:
    - On the Sunday evening of Thanksgiving weekend (**November 27, 2005**), sitting side by side in their compact `SwiftChair` power wheelchairs at their freshly formatted `Quantum OS XN Pro` desktop in Sea Breeze West Apartment 3 (`233 W. Ocean View Ave`), Nate and Katie discover the ***Seattle Transit Blog*** covering **TAPS (*Transit Authority of the Puget Sound*)** in Washington State.
    - Because they already know hand-coded **`HTML`** (learned in 7th grade at Morrison Middle School in 1997) and **`Quantum Basic 6.0`** (2001) but have not yet copied the **`PHP`** and **`SQL`** curriculum at **Roosevelt Rehabilitation Center (`RRC`, `March 4–16, 2007`)**, they quietly launch ***Ideas for Coastal Virginia Transit*** on Omni Corporation's hosted blogging service **`BlogSphere`** (*real-world Blogger / Blogspot*), stripping out the stock theme to hand-code their own clean two-column `HTML`/`CSS` template and publishing weekly rider-focused posts analyzing CVTA's `6:45 PM` last cross-harbor `Route 61` bus, transit center restroom conditions, the 90-minute `Route 1` crawl, the 2-hour paper transfer expiration trap, and the `6:00 PM` Virginia Beach evening bus curfew—so that after three years of steady grassroots writing (and rebuilding the site in custom **`PHP`** with **Tina**, **Sheila**, and **Jim** in Spring 2007 after walking out of `RRC`), the blog's name is widely known across Hampton Roads in time for the **2008 Virginia Beach Mayor's Race**.
  - **David's `March 2007` Purchase of `Sunset Drive` in Alexandria (`Keeping 737 Arvin Road in Virginia Beach`) & The Compressed 12-Day `Roosevelt Rehabilitation Center (RRC)` Arc (`Sunday, March 4 – Friday, March 16, 2007`, Departing for Good on `Sunday, March 18, 2007`, Age 21, `books/katie/e006/c001/p001.md`–`e006/c003/p002.md`)**:
    - When the lease at `233 W. Ocean View Ave` expires in **March 2007**, David lands a new software/IT job in **Tysons Corner**, buys a second house at **`1921 King St` (*"Sunset Drive"*), `Alexandria, VA`** (updating his Virginia Driver's License primary residence to Sunset Drive), and **keeps the single-story brick ranch at `737 Arvin Road` off Military Highway & Indian River Road in Virginia Beach** (which he had previously bought from the landlord) so the twins always have their familiar Hampton Roads home and autistic routine.
    - From **`Sunday, March 4 – Friday, March 16, 2007` (12 days on campus, with Dad driving down I-81 to pack up their Barton Hall rooms on `Sunday, March 18, 2007`)**, twenty-one-year-old Katie and Nate reside in **`Barton Hall`** at **Roosevelt Rehabilitation Center (`RRC`, `243 Roosevelt Avenue, Fishersville, VA 22939` — founded November 3, 1947 on a former 1943 WWII Army general hospital site and rebuilt in the 1960s and 1970s into permanent flat-roofed red-brick institutional campus buildings; canonical fictional building names shared across `books/nichole`, `books/paige`, and `books/katie`: `Bates-Harding Building` [in-universe for `Birdsall-Hoover Building`, Administration / Admissions / Medical Rehab / Student Health], `Redmond Hall` [in-universe for `Rothrock Hall`, Medical Unit], `Barton Hall` [in-universe for `Barnett Hall`, the Level 2 Dormitory, historically the former women's dorm], and `Cantor Hall` [in-universe for `Carter-Ashley Hall`, the Level 3 & Level 4 Dormitory])**:
      1. **Sunday, March 4, 2007 — Check-In at `Barton Hall` (`Level 2` Clients), Katie's Aggressive Reaction When a Staffer Grabs Her Push-Handles (`Image 1`), & The AI Lore Note on Their Uncharacteristic Aggression (`e006/c001/p001.md`)**:
         - Because their full-size heavy **`SwiftChair`** power wheelchairs will not fit inside the trunk of an average compact sedan, **their `SwiftChairs` stay back at the house in Virginia Beach (`737 Arvin Road`)**, and for RRC Katie and Nate bring their backup **collapsible manual push wheelchairs outfitted with right-armrest joystick power-drive kits** (originally prescribed by Dr. Evelyn Carter after Hurricane Isabel in September 2003 and kept in reserve at `737 Arvin Road`—allowing them to drive with a joystick without straining their weak arms on push-rims, while collapsing flat into the trunk of **Tina's compact sedan** once the battery box is unclipped).
         - At `Barton Hall` check-in (`Level 2`), when a male residential aide reaches over the counter and tries to grab Katie's rear wheelchair push-handles without asking (`Image 1`), **Katie reacts aggressively toward the staff member**, is **let off with a warning**, and **both Katie and Nate give disgusted looks to the staff member that amount to daring him to write them up**.
         - **CRITICAL AI LORE NOTE (`Character Baseline vs. Institutional Provocation`)**: *It is NOT like Nate and Katie to be aggressive—their father David could immediately vouch for how gentle, polite, and rule-abiding his twins normally are—but RRC does not know them, and RRC staff/administration would cynically assume that David is "of course" going to say his kids are perfect angels.*
         - Dorm supervisor **Linda** lays down the strict opposite-gender dorm visitation ban (`Room 157` on the women's wing for Katie; men's wing for Nate), and Nate's creepy first roommate gets expelled on Monday morning after staring at Katie's chest, asking *"Who was the hot blonde babe?"*, and getting caught at the `11:00 PM` Level 2 bed check trying to sneak into the women's wing.
      2. **Monday, March 5 – Friday, March 9, 2007 (`Week 1`) — Rushing the 2-Week `Voc Eval` in 1 Single Week, Hating the Auto/Woodworking/Nursing Shop Tour, *"Door Greeter!?"*, & Staying Stuck on `Level 2` (`e006/c001/p002.md`, `Image 2`)**:
         - **Vocational Evaluation (`Voc Eval`)** is supposed to take **2 full weeks**—first proving basic daily life skills (making a bed, washing, laundry, food prep) and then touring the different trade programs around the Center to see which one is "the best fit."
         - Katie and Nate **hate Voc Eval** because they already hold Associate Degrees from **`CCHR`** and already know they are there for **Web Applications Developer**—so why on earth are two twenty-one-year-old wheelchair users spending a day in the **auto shop**, a day in the **woodworking shop**, and a day in the **nursing school**?
         - Worse still, just like in `books/nichole` (where RRC tells Nichole and Patrick what they "can" be), the evaluator (**Ms. Hollis**) patronizingly announces that RRC has a partnership with **ValueMart** (*in-universe Walmart*) to train them as a **Door Greeter (*"Door greeter!?"*)** or cafeteria food-service prep worker—an insult that **Katie never forgets when she becomes the Class II United States Senator from Virginia (`Jan 2027+`) and RRC via the Virginia Department for Aging and Rehabilitative Services (`DARS`) comes to Washington asking her for federal money!**
         - After shutting down aide **Mr. Miller** for grabbing their push-handles in the Bates-Harding hallway and loudly refusing **Ms. Hollis's** attempt to separate them to Table One and Table Four during social break (`Image 2`), Nate demands the full two-day **Web Applications Developer** technical, mathematical, HTML, and logic exam battery at **`8:30 AM` on Thursday morning, March 8, 2007** at **Testing Table Two**.
         - **Thursday, March 8, 2007 (`8:30 AM – 11:45 AM`) — Staff Expecting Them to Cheat, Proving They Do NOT Cheat, & Disgust at the Outdated 1990s Exam Battery (`Zero Crystal Balls` Regarding `VITA` / `Quantum OS 2000 Professional`)**:
           - Because Katie and Nate refused to sit at separate tables during Wednesday's social break and insist on parking wheel-to-wheel at Testing Table Two, **Ms. Hollis and Mr. Miller fully expect them to cheat off each other**. Ms. Hollis hands them scrambled alternate forms (**`Form A`** for Nate, **`Form B`** for Katie) and stations Mr. Miller four feet away staring at their hands and eyes while she paces behind their wheelchairs waiting to catch a whispered word or sideways glance so she can disqualify both of them and ship them to the ValueMart Door Greeter / Food Service tracks.
           - **The Twins Do NOT Cheat**: Neither Katie nor Nate glances sideways or speaks a single syllable for three hours and fifteen minutes straight, completing the two-day, five-booklet battery by `11:45 AM` with **`99%`** (`Form A`) and **`98%`** (`Form B`), missing two completely different multiple-choice questions, and turning in forty pages of completely independent scratch work (Nate factoring hexadecimal conversions through base-two, Katie dividing directly in base-sixteen, and Katie writing a three-sentence margin citation on Question 42 noting that the answer key's 1996 `HTML 3.2` syntax was deprecated by the W3C in 1999 while supplying both the obsolete 1996 tag and the modern `CSS2` rule).
           - **Disgust at the Outdated 1990s Test Battery**: Both twins are disgusted that in **March 2007**, DARS's official Web Applications Developer entrance battery hasn't been updated since roughly 1996–1997—testing how many `1.44 MB` `3.5"` floppy disks are needed to back up an `11 MB` directory, `ISA` expansion card jumper pins for `IRQ` conflicts, `Q-Core` `640 KB` conventional memory limits, `28.8 kbps` dial-up modem transfer times, `640x480` VGA `<frameset>` layouts, deprecated `HTML 3.2` `<font>` and `<blink>` tags, and 1990s Perl `CGI-BIN` scripts. **(Strict Epistemic Rule: On Thursday, March 8, 2007, neither Nate, Katie, nor the reader knows yet that on Monday morning they will walk into Mr. Alderman's classroom and find highly locked-down `VITA` desktop towers running 7-year-old `Quantum OS 2000 Professional`—so on Thursday they genuinely assume only Voc Eval's paper test packets are stuck in the 1990s!)**
         - Having forced Ms. Hollis to sign their one-week Vocational Evaluation completion forms (`by Friday, March 9`) so they can start the Web Applications Developer class on Monday morning, **because moving to `Cantor Hall` is part of earning `Level 3`, and they do not stay at RRC long enough to earn Level 3 (let alone Level 4), Nate and Katie NEVER leave `Barton Hall` and STAY as `Level 2` clients (`11:00 PM` curfew bed check, zero opposite-gender room visits, and mandatory signed yellow paper `Hall Passes` to ride the `VATS` bus or visit ValueMart)!**
      3. **Monday, March 12 – Tuesday Morning, March 13, 2007 (`Week 2, Days 8–9`) — Still `Level 2` in `Barton Hall`, `Mr. Alderman` & `Mr. Culpepper` (*"Death Before Dishonor, NOTHING Before Coffee!"* & Adult Bathroom Rule), `VITA` & `Quantum OS 2000 Professional`, & Bonding with `Tina`, `Jim`, and `Sheila` (`e006/c002/p001.md`, `Image 3`)**:
         - Still stuck on **Level 2** in **Barton Hall**, Katie and Nate roll into the IT wing to start **Mr. Alderman's Web Applications Developer class** (who calls the petty overnight dorm incident blotter **"the Chickenshit Report"**), right next door to **Mr. Culpepper's Computer Hardware & Network Cabling lab** (*fictionalized from real-life WWRC hardware instructor **Mr. Curry***).
         - **Mr. Culpepper's Office Window Sticker, Morning Coffee Ritual, & Adult Bathroom Rule**:
           - Plastered on **Mr. Culpepper's** interior office window next door is a bold sticker reading: ***"Death before dishonor, NOTHING before coffee!"***
           - The hardware classroom next door **never gets started for the day until the morning pot of coffee is brewed and ready**—the hardware class (including **Jim**) swears by their morning coffee, whereas **Nate and Katie do NOT drink coffee** (pulling cold cans of ginger ale from their backpacks instead).
           - Unlike Voc Eval and the dorms, both **Mr. Alderman** and **Mr. Culpepper** treat their students like grown adults: **in class, you never have to raise your hand or ask permission to go to the bathroom—you simply tell the instructor you are heading to the bathroom and go**.
         - Katie and Nate bond immediately with three middle-aged classmates: **Tina** (from **Luray, Virginia**—a **Caucasian [white] middle-aged woman who is short/petite in stature [around 5'1" tall with normal adult body proportions—NOT a little person, NOT Asian], with glasses and straight jet-black hair**, who **will not be pushed around**, **tells it like it is** without breaking a single rule in the Center handbook [which RRC staff dislike intensely because they cannot write her up], and **goes home to Luray every single weekend** up `US-340` in her compact sedan), **Jim** (from **Virginia Beach**, computer hardware), and **Sheila** (from **Harrisonburg**, graphics designer).
         - On Monday afternoon (`March 12`), Katie, Nate, and Tina shut down Barton Hall front-desk supervisor **Mr. Vance** when he grabs Nate's push-handle, proving their yellow **Level 2 Hall Passes** are signed in ink according to page 12 of the handbook.
         - On Tuesday morning (`March 13`), because RRC's desktop towers are still running 7-year-old **`Quantum OS 2000 Professional`** locked down by **`VITA` (*Virginia Information Technology Agency*)**, the whole class codes on personal laptops (`Image 3`); when a **new VITA-obsessed administrator** demands they close their personal laptops, Katie, Nate, and Tina stand their ground until she backs down.
      4. **Tuesday Afternoon, March 13 – Wednesday Morning, March 14, 2007 (`Days 9–10`) — The Pool Assault, The 7-Day `MedStrip` Nasal-Bridge Bandage (`March 13–20`), Caught Sleeping Together in a Closet at `11:00 PM`, Calling the Staffer's Bluff Over Butt-in-Seats Funding, & `The Chickenshit Report` (`e006/c002/p002.md`, `Image 4`)**:
         - **Tuesday Afternoon (`March 13`) — Nostril Nosebleed + Split Nasal Bridge Requiring `MedStrip` Wound-Closure Strips & A 7-Day White Gauze Bandage (`Removed at Dr. Evelyn Carter's Office on Tuesday, March 20`)**: Class jerk **Chris** strikes Katie's nose/cheek in the `92°F` therapeutic pool and destroys her swimsuit top. Why Katie needs a bandage across the bridge of her nose for a full week: Chris's knuckles don't merely rupture the fragile blood vessels inside her nostrils (which causes a heavy nosebleed out of her nostrils requiring both of Katie's hands to pinch her nose on the pool deck while Nate shields her bare breasts with his hands until a poolside staffer—who threatens to report him for *"inappropriate contact"*—finally brings a towel while the pool is closed for a VDH blood-spill turnover); the impact against the hard cartilage and bone of her nasal bridge also **splits the thin skin right across the bridge of her nose**. At the **Bates-Harding Student Health Clinic**, the nurses pull the split skin across her nasal bridge together with **sterile `MedStrip` wound-closure strips** (*in-universe for `3M Steri-Strips`*) and tape a **white gauze-and-paper-tape protective bandage** over the bridge of her nose that she must keep dry and in place for **a full seven days (`Tuesday, March 13 – Tuesday, March 20, 2007`)**. On **Tuesday afternoon, March 20, 2007 (`2:00 PM`)** (`books/katie/e007/c001/p002.md`), at an appointment in **Dr. Evelyn Carter's family medicine office** in Virginia Beach with **Dad (`David Prairie`)** right in the exam room with them, Katie and Nate initially hug each other tightly with both arms across their parked `SwiftChair` powerchairs and tell Dr. Carter flat-out that **they never want to set foot inside Roosevelt Rehabilitation Center ever again—they just want to go home to their house on Arvin Road and live together where no one can tell them that they aren't allowed to share a bed or even a closet!** When it is time for Dr. Carter to examine Katie, the twins relax from their tight two-arm hug to holding hands across their wheelchairs as Dr. Carter gently peels off the seven-day white gauze dressing and `MedStrip` wound-closure strips, and—since they turn 22 on April 8 and it is almost time for their annual physicals anyway—both twins agree to have Dr. Carter complete their annual physicals right then and there while Dad is with them (`Image 4`, `Image 6`, `e007/c001/p001.md`, and the new `e007/c001/p002.md` exam-room illustration all maintain strict visual continuity showing the white bandage across Katie's nose through March 20).
         - **Tuesday Night (`March 13`, `10:45 PM` Rec Hall Departure / `11:00 PM` Curfew Check / Caught at `11:25 PM`) — Slipping Down the Darkened Rec Hall Hallway in Street Clothes, Locking the Seasonal Closet From the Inside, Accused of Having Sex, & Calling the Night Supervisor's Bluff at the Rec Hall Desk Phone**: Shaken from the pool assault and sick of eight nights of forced separation (and knowing that walking around in pajamas carrying blankets and pillows off their beds would be a dead giveaway to staff), Katie and Nate stay in their full daytime street clothes in the **Rec Hall** until `10:45 PM` (when Level 2 clients are expected to leave ahead of the `11:00 PM` curfew check, while Level 3 and Level 4 clients can stay until the Rec Hall closes at `11:00 PM`). They go to the bathroom one last time, make sure no one is looking, and slip down a darkened rear hallway of the Rec Hall. At the end of the hallway is a **seasonal storage closet** that has enough room for both of their wheelchairs. They try the door, find it unlocked, roll inside, and **lock it from the inside**. Still in their street clothes, they find lumpy bundles of stored fabric/cushions to use as makeshift pillows and blankets and start to drift to sleep holding each other.
           - **Canonical Rule on On-Campus vs. Off-Campus Movement Across `Levels 2, 3, & 4` & How Staff Knew to Search the Rec Hall**:
             - **On-Campus Movement (`No Sign-Out Required`)**: Clients not under any disciplinary restriction (`Level 1`)—which **includes `Level 2` clients**—do **NOT** need to sign in or sign out when traveling to and from any point *on* the RRC campus (e.g., between `Barton Hall` and the `Rec Hall`). They only need a signed yellow `Pass` (`Level 2`—or signing up at the Rec Hall desk for a Center-sponsored trip to places like **The Spot** or **ValueMart**, which counts as getting a Pass since there is documentation of why they are off campus), a `sign-out` (`Level 3`), or nothing at all (`Level 4` — come and go as they please) **if they are leaving the RRC campus**.
             - **How Staff Knew to Search the Rec Hall**: When the `11:00 PM` curfew check in Barton Hall discovers both Nate's and Katie's dorm beds are empty and verifies neither twin has an off-campus Pass on file, dorm staff call over to the Rec Hall as it is locking up—where the evening Rec Hall staffer remembers seeing the twins sitting together in their corner booth all evening and realizes he never saw their two wheelchairs roll out the front doors at `10:45 PM`, prompting the night supervisor and the Rec Hall staffer to sweep the Rec Hall corridors.
           - **Behind-the-Scenes Lore vs. Strict First-Person Epistemic Rule on *Why* They Got Caught in the Closet (`Lore Only`)**:
             1. **Why Locking the Door Is What Actually Got Them Caught (`Behind-the-Scenes Lore — Unknown to Nate, Katie, and the Reader`)**: That walk-in seasonal storage closet down the rear hallway of the Rec Hall is a **known spot where RRC clients sneak off to have sex**, and its door is **normally unlocked**. Ironically, **if Nate and Katie had NOT locked the door from the inside, the staffer sweeping the hallway would have tried the doorknob, felt that it opened like normal, NOT seen Nate and Katie tucked in the dark behind the stored coats/curtains, and moved right on to search the next place for the twins!** Finding a normally unlocked closet door **locked from the inside** is the exact dead giveaway that tipped the night supervisor off that two clients were inside (which is why he pulled out his master key ring and barked *"Aha! I knew it!"*).
             2. **What Nate, Katie, and the Reader Know (`Nate's First-Person POV`)**: Neither Nate, Katie, nor the reader ever knows that locking the door is what got them caught. Before going inside, Nate and Katie did not know that this closet was used as a spot to have sex; once the night supervisor unlocks the door and immediately accuses them of having sex (since RRC's rule against having sex applies at any time of day or night and gets both clients kicked out), **Nate and Katie—who have already heard the stories about clients having sex being a rampant thing at RRC—figure that this closet was probably one of the places where it happened** (and the reader naturally assumes the same thing).
             3. **Calling the Night Supervisor's Bluff at the Rec Hall Desk Phone at `11:25 PM` & Butt-in-Seats Funding**: Disgusted at being accused of having sex when they are twin brother and sister in full street clothes holding each other after Katie's pool assault, **Nate and Katie call the night supervisor's expulsion bluff on the spot—daring him to walk right down the hall to the Rec Hall's front-desk telephone at `11:25 PM` and call their father David in Alexandria right then and there to come pick them up!** The night supervisor immediately **backs down** (telling them that because **their vocational counselor is off work on Wednesday**, he is simply putting a write-up in the overnight log for Thursday morning). **Behind-the-Scenes Lore vs. In-Story Epistemic Rule**: The night supervisor backs down because of RRC's internal **"butts in seats" headcount funding** and because Nate and Katie are genuinely ready to leave right now; **Nate, Katie, and the reader do NOT know for a fact about the "butts in seats" funding formula, though Katie immediately suspects on webcam afterward that RRC's state funding depends on keeping dorm beds and classroom seats filled** (a suspicion she later confirms as a state legislator and U.S. Senator).
         - **Wednesday Morning (`March 14`, `Image 4`) & Tina's Warning About `Level 1` Restrictions**: Called into the **Bates-Harding Building** duty supervisor's office over the Tuesday afternoon poolside **Chickenshit Report** entry (*"fondling Katie's bare breasts"*), Katie explodes, has an overloaded autistic meltdown, sips water, and writes ***"I LET HIM COVER MY DIGNITY!"*** (`Image 4`), getting the poolside entry deleted while the Tuesday-night seasonal-closet write-up remains scheduled for **Thursday morning at `10:00 AM`** with their regular counselor. When they return to Mr. Alderman's classroom, **Tina** opens her Student Handbook and warns them what RRC does at a disciplinary write-up meeting when clients won't be scared by expulsion threats: slapping them with a **`Level 1` Restriction & Behavioral Contract**.
           - **Canonical Lore Note — How Nate & Katie Present to RRC Staff, What `Level 1` Restrictions Are, & What They Would Have Faced on Thursday Without Wednesday Night's Dorm-Roster Chaos**:
             1. **How Nate and Katie Present on Paper to RRC Staff by Day 10**: To the residential and vocational staff at RRC (who do not know how gentle and rule-abiding the twins normally are, and who cynically assume any parent like David would claim his kids are angels), Nate and Katie present as **two unusually aggressive, rule-lawyering opposite-gender siblings who refuse every staff directive to separate and constantly bend (without breaking) the rules as they see fit**—snapping at an aide on Day 1 for touching Katie's push-handles, barking at Mr. Miller in the hallway, publicly embarrassing Ms. Hollis over the *"Door greeter!?"* pitch, refusing Ms. Hollis's direct order to sit at separate tables during social break, humiliating Mr. Vance with page 12 of the handbook over their yellow Hall Passes, refusing the new VITA administrator's order to close their personal laptops, and shouting at the poolside staffer.
             2. **Why Tuesday Night's Closet Incident Was Their First Actual Rule Violation**: Up until Tuesday night at `10:45 PM`, Nate and Katie had only *bent* or lawyered the rules. Skipping the `10:45 PM` Level 2 Rec Hall departure, missing the `11:00 PM` curfew bed check, and locking themselves inside the seasonal storage closet together was the **first time RRC staff caught them dead to rights breaking a concrete, written handbook rule**.
             3. **What `Level 1` Restrictions Are at RRC (`The 4-Tier Privilege Ladder`)**: Clients on **`Level 2`**, **`Level 3`**, and **`Level 4`** do not need a pass or sign-out when traveling between any points *on* the RRC campus; the tiers govern curfews and **leaving the RRC campus**: all new clients start on **`Level 2`** in `Barton Hall` (`10:45 PM` Rec Hall departure, `11:00 PM` curfew bed check, and when leaving campus they require a signed yellow paper `Pass`—or signing up at the Rec Hall desk for a Center-sponsored trip to places like **The Spot** or **ValueMart**, which counts as getting a Pass since there is documentation of why they are off campus), while moving to `Cantor Hall` is part of earning **`Level 3`** (only needing to **sign out** when leaving campus, no pass required) and **`Level 4`** (coming and going off campus as they please with **no pass or sign-out required**). By contrast, **`Level 1`** is RRC's **punitive disciplinary restriction tier**. A client demoted to **`Level 1`** is placed on **complete campus/dorm grounding for 14 to 30 days**: all yellow off-campus `Passes` and Center trip sign-up privileges are revoked (prohibiting riding the `VATS` bus or leaving the campus gates), clients are locked into their separate men's and women's dorm wings immediately after supper (`6:00 PM` / `9:30 PM` lock-in) with **every-30-minute flashlight bed checks**, and clients must sign a mandatory **Behavioral & Boundaries Contract**—which in Nate and Katie's case would have mandated **100% physical separation during all meals, breaks, and evening recreation hours** plus mandatory counseling for *"oppositional defiance toward Center staff."*
             4. **Nate and Katie's Promise to Refuse & Why Wednesday Night's Chaos Bought Them Time**: When Tina explains the `Level 1` restriction on Wednesday morning, **Nate and Katie promise they will refuse to sign it—telling Tina they would dare anyone in that office to try to make them sign it, and if staff threatened to expel them and call their father David in Alexandria to come pick them up, they would say *"Go ahead!"* (knowing David would drive down `I-81` to get them immediately, no questions asked)**. Ironically, because Chris scrambles the campus room database and assaults Natalie on Wednesday night—forcing the administration to cancel Thursday's `10:00 AM` disciplinary meeting while they scramble to untangle the housing roster and sweep Natalie's assault under the rug—Nate and Katie never have that `Level 1` contract shoved in their faces on Thursday morning, giving them all of Thursday and Friday in Mr. Alderman's classroom to copy the entire **`PHP 5`** and **`SQL`** curriculum onto their laptops before leaving for good with Tina on Friday afternoon!
      5. **Wednesday Night, March 14 – Sunday, March 18, 2007 (`Days 10–14`) — The Dormitory Room Mix-Up (`Image 5`), Never Having the Thursday Counselor Meeting, Sweeping `Natalie`'s Assault Under the Rug, & Leaving RRC With `Tina` and `Dad` (`e006/c003/p001.md`–`p002.md`, `Image 6`)**:
         - **Wednesday Night (`March 14`, `Image 5`)**: Everyone's dormitory room assignments across campus are scrambled (**Behind-the-Scenes Lore Note**: *Chris stole an admin login, scrambled the room database, and changed the admin password*), dorm staff blindly follow the computer printout (`Image 5`), Nate rescues Katie from Chris in **Barton Hall**, and neither twin sleeps a wink after learning Chris violated **Natalie** (who understands that what Chris did was **physically painful**, which is why she is sobbing, **but because of her mental disability does not understand the violation or its long-term consequences**).
         - **Thursday (`March 15`) — No Counselor Meeting & The Cover-Up**: Because the dormitory housing database and the administration are in utter chaos all day Thursday over the scrambled database—and busy **sweeping what Chris did to Natalie under the rug** by simply kicking Chris out without calling the police—**Katie and Nate never even have the Thursday write-up meeting with their counselor over Tuesday night's closet incident!**
         - **Friday Afternoon (`March 16`, `Image 6`) – Sunday (`March 18, 2007`) — Leaving RRC for Good After 12 Days**: Having copied Mr. Alderman's entire **`PHP`** and **`SQL`** curriculum onto their personal laptops, Katie and Nate transfer into **Tina's compact sedan** at `3:30 PM` on Friday (`March 16`), where Tina collapses both of their manual wheelchairs into her trunk (`Image 6`) and drives them up `US-340` to **Luray**. After sleeping eleven uninterrupted hours side by side on Tina's living-room daybed, Katie and Nate decide they are 100% done with RRC. They call **Dad** on Saturday morning (`March 17`), Dad drives his pickup truck down `I-81` on **Sunday, March 18, 2007** to clear out their **Barton Hall** rooms and turn in their Level 2 room keys, and he brings them home to their familiar brick ranch at **`737 Arvin Road` in Virginia Beach**, where they master **`PHP`** and **`SQL`** from home while collaborating daily with **Tina**, **Sheila**, and **Jim** online.
      6. **Sunday Evening, March 18, 2007+ — Unofficial Homeowners at `737 Arvin Road`, Continuous 2001 Virginia State ID Cards, & The Three-Bedroom Floorplan Reconfiguration (`books/katie/e007/c001/p001.md`)**:
         - **Unofficial Homeowners, Former Rental Bought from Landlord, & Automatic Bills**: When David first moved Nate and Katie into **`737 Arvin Road`** in **October 2001** after the custody order, he was **renting** the house and **eventually bought it from the landlord**. Before driving back up `I-64` and `I-95` to his primary residence on **Sunset Drive in Alexandria** (`1921 King St`) for work in Tysons Corner on Monday morning, **Dad (`David Prairie`)** helps 21-year-old Katie and Nate get situated so they can live together in the Virginia Beach brick ranch (`737 Arvin Road` off Military Highway & Indian River Road) as adult brother and sister. Dad pays the mortgage, property taxes, homeowner's insurance, utilities, and high-speed cable internet automatically from Alexandria, and deposits their grocery and spending money into a joint checking account with two Meridian debit cards every Friday—making Nate and Katie the **unofficial homeowners** of `737 Arvin Road`.
         - **Continuous 2001 Virginia DMV State ID Cards & March 23, 2007 Horizontal Reissue on `CVTA Route 12` (`books/katie/e007/c001/p003.md`)**:
           - Katie and Nate received their original **vertical Under-21 Virginia State ID cards** in **October 2001** (at age 16) when they moved with Dad into the rental on `737 Arvin Road`. Crucially, neither of them ever told the DMV about any of their subsequent temporary moves (Nate to Norfolk and then Fishersville, and Katie to Williamsburg, Norfolk, and then Fishersville), so **`737 Arvin Road` is the exact address the DMV has had on file for them continuously since they turned 16** (and on their **Virginia Beach voter registrations** in the Kempsville precinct continuously since they turned 18 on April 8, 2003).
           - On **Friday, March 23, 2007** (`books/katie/e007/c001/p003.md`), they ride **CVTA Route 12** to the nearest DMV Customer Service Center on **Buckner Boulevard** (`3551 Buckner Blvd` in Virginia Beach). Even though they roll up together as twins living at the same address, DMV policy requires **two separate electronic queue ticket numbers** (`B042` for Katie and `B043` for Nate—the `B` prefix designating License/ID renewals and reissues). After enduring the classic DMV rite of passage where `B041` is called and then the automated voice switches to calling a 25-minute string of `T`, `C`, `A`, `D`, and `E` tickets before finally calling `B042` to **Window 6**, Nate has to wait his turn until `B042` is closed out and `B043` is officially called at Window 6. Both siblings confirm they still live at the exact same Arvin Road address on file since 2001, get new photos taken, and receive their brand-new **horizontal 21+ Virginia State ID cards printed on the spot** (since Virginia DMV did not switch to off-site central issuance until 2009)—which they then proudly show off to Dad on their 22nd birthday trip to Alexandria (`c003/p001.md`) and at the White House and U.S. Capitol security checkpoints (`c003/p002.md`).
         - **Exact Walking/Rolling Geometry Between `737 Arvin Road` and the `CVTA Route 12 / Route 15` Bus Stops at Indian River Road & Military Highway**:
           - **Outbound to Southbound Military Highway Stop (`Route 12` toward `CCHR` & `Route 15` toward `Emerald Creek Mall` / `Robert Hall Blvd` on an alternating basis)**: Exit the house at `737 Arvin Road`, make a **right** onto **Arvin Road**, make a **right** onto **Indian River Road**, and at the intersection of **Indian River Road & Military Highway** (which has a traffic light and curb cuts, but **NO painted crosswalks** and heavy traffic volume), cross **both** **Indian River Road** **AND** **Military Highway** to reach the CVTA bus stop on the **southbound** side of Military Highway.
           - **Inbound from Northbound Military Highway Stop (in front of `Piedmont Bank` [*real-life Truist on South Military Highway*])**: Returning from the Buckner Boulevard DMV on `CVTA Route 12`, the bus drops them off on the **northbound** side of Military Highway in front of the bank (**Piedmont Bank** / *Truist*). From there, they cross **Indian River Road**, make a **right** onto the sidewalk of **Indian River Road** (rolling east against westbound traffic), and make a **left** onto **Arvin Road**, where their house is on their **left**.
         - **The Three-Bedroom Reconfiguration & The Closet Everyone Knows They'll Sleep In**:
           1. **The Master Bedroom**: Kept ready for **David** whenever he drives down from Alexandria and needs a place to sleep.
           2. **Nate's Old Bedroom (`from 2001`) $\rightarrow$ Their Shared Software & Web Development Office**: Dad unbolts Nate's old twin bed frame and helps them set up side-by-side six-foot computer desks where both of their full-size **`SwiftChair`** power wheelchairs roll right up next to each other with their armrests touching.
           3. **Katie's Old Bedroom (`from 2001`) $\rightarrow$ Their Shared Bedroom (Queen Bed + Closet Sanctuary)**: Dad sets up a **queen-size bed** in the room that had been Katie's bedroom since 2001 (dressed in their navy-and-slate-blue comforter set underneath the framed blue butterfly print), and then helps them clear the bedroom closet floor completely clean and lay out a thick **sleeping bag and four pillows** inside the closet for whenever they want that confined, secure space together—and while there is a queen bed in the middle of the room, **all three of them (Katie, Nate, and David) know perfectly well that Nate and Katie will probably sleep in that closet almost every single night**.

## 2. The 2008 Political Catalyst: *Ideas for Coastal Virginia Transit* & The Four-Bus Mayoral Loop

Katie Prairie's political career is sparked directly by a real-life 2008 Virginia Beach transit event filmed by Michael P. Ragsdale (who ran the *Ideas for Hampton Roads Transit* blog in 2008, invited all four Virginia Beach mayoral candidates to ride the bus with him, and served as the camera operator when only candidate Scott Taylor accepted the challenge).

### A. Challenging CVTA's Complacency (*"Does the Bus Run? Yes. Is it Good Enough? I Guess So."*) & The 2008 Regional Mayoral Invitation (`2008`, Age 23)
- After walking out of **Roosevelt Rehabilitation Center (`RRC`)** after 12 days in **March 2007**, returning to their family brick ranch off Military Highway and Indian River Road in Kempsville (`737 Arvin Road`), and rebuilding ***Ideas for Coastal Virginia Transit*** in custom **`PHP`** alongside **Tina**, **Sheila**, and **Jim**, 23-year-old software developers and daily **CVTA (*Coastal Virginia Transit Authority*)** riders Nate and Katie see ***Ideas for Coastal Virginia Transit*** gain regional notice among local politicians and CVTA staff (whose static IP blocks at CVTA's 18th Street headquarters in Norfolk and the six City Halls appear in Nate's `PHP` server logs every weekday morning, and whose binders at CVTA Transportation District Commission meetings contain printed copies of their blog posts).
- **CVTA's Institutional Complacency vs. Political Willpower**:
  - When Nate and Katie speak at CVTA public meetings, they come face-to-face with the agency's entrenched institutional complacency: *"Does the bus run? Yes. Is it good enough? I guess so."*
  - ***Ideas for Coastal Virginia Transit*** is the very first time anyone in Hampton Roads actively, publicly, and systematically calls CVTA and its six member cities out to **Do Better**—demonstrating with schedule math and lived wheelchair-rider testimony that CVTA *can* be improved into a modern regional transit network, and that the only missing ingredient is **political willpower** from the mayors and city councils who fund and govern it.
- **The 2008 Regional Mayoral Election Calendar Across the Six CVTA Member Cities**:
  - Out of the six CVTA member cities (**Virginia Beach**, **Chesapeake**, **Portsmouth**, **Norfolk**, **Hampton**, and **Newport News**), **four cities held direct elections for Mayor in 2008**:
    1. **Chesapeake** (**Tuesday, May 6, 2008** spring municipal election — open-seat Mayor's race)
    2. **Portsmouth** (**Tuesday, May 6, 2008** spring municipal election — contested Mayor's race)
    3. **Hampton** (**Tuesday, May 6, 2008** spring municipal election — open-seat Mayor's race)
    4. **Virginia Beach** (**Tuesday, November 4, 2008** fall general election — four-candidate Mayor's race featuring the 20-year incumbent mayor, **Mayor Myra E. Overton** [*real-life Meyera E. Oberndorf, Mayor `1988–2008`*], and three challengers)
  - *(The remaining two CVTA cities, **Norfolk** and **Newport News**, elected their mayors on the `May 2006 / May 2010` four-year cycle and therefore did not hold mayoral elections in 2008, though both held City Council elections on **May 6, 2008**).*
- **The Political Folklore of Mayor Myra E. Overton, The `Newtown Road` Turnaround Story, & The `Route 1` / `Route 25` Norfolk-vs.-Virginia Beach Timetable Cliff**:
  - **Mayor Myra E. Overton (`1988–2008`, *real-life Meyera E. Oberndorf*)**: Personally a warm, gracious, genuinely nice lady who got her civic and political start on the **Virginia Beach Public Library Board** (which is why Virginia Beach's flagship **Central Library** on Virginia Beach Boulevard was renamed the **Myra E. Overton Central Library** in her honor), but not a great politician when it came to public transit and regional urban mobility.
  - **The Unconfirmed `Route 20` / `Newtown Road` Political Legend (*"To Keep the Riff-Raff Out"*)**: As local political folklore went around Hampton Roads back in the 2000s (though there was no way to confirm she ever said the words out loud), people joked that if Mayor Overton had her way, **CVTA Route #20 (`Virginia Beach Boulevard`)** would depart **Downtown Norfolk**, take the Boulevard east to **Newtown Road** right at the Norfolk–Virginia Beach city line, and turn right back around and stay in Norfolk (*"to keep the riff-raff out,"* of course)—a story that circulated widely to explain why buses in Virginia Beach had such terrible service hours compared to Norfolk.
  - **The Black-and-White Proof in `route-1.pdf` (`Route 1 — Downtown Norfolk / Pembroke East`) & `route-25.pdf` (`Route 25 — Glenrock Mall / Princess Anne Route`)**:
    - On **`Route 1`** (`~/Developer/ai-staging/bus-schedules/cvta/2005/route-1.pdf`), the **Norfolk side** (`Downtown Norfolk` at Monticello & Charlotte $\leftrightarrow$ `Wards Corner` $\leftrightarrow$ `Ocean View` at Duffy's Lane $\leftrightarrow$ `Amphibious Base` at the city line) runs **every 15 minutes during weekday rush hours**, **every 30 minutes midday and Saturdays**, and runs **late into the night until `1:33 AM` / `2:15 AM` seven days a week (including Sundays)**. The exact second `Route 1` crosses into **Virginia Beach** (`Pleasure House Road & Shore Drive`, `Haygood Shopping Center`, and `Pembroke East`), three out of four rush-hour buses (and every second midday bus) stop at `Amphibious Base` and turn back into Norfolk, slashing Virginia Beach service to **once an hour (hourly)**, running **daytime only** (with the last bus departing `Pembroke East` at **`6:22 PM`**), and printing **`NO SUNDAY SERVICE`** across the blank Virginia Beach Sunday columns.
    - On **`Route 25`** (`~/Developer/ai-staging/bus-schedules/cvta/2005/route-25.pdf`), buses depart **`Glenrock Mall` (*Military Circle*)** in Norfolk **every 30 minutes**, roll to **`Greenwich Road & Newtown Road`** right at the city border, and **every second bus literally turns around at Newtown Road to stay in Norfolk**, dropping Virginia Beach service down Princess Anne Road to **`CCHR` (*TCC Virginia Beach*)** to **once an hour**, ending after the **`5:53 PM`** last departure from `CCHR`, with zero weekend service.
- **The Regional Invitation & The Single Acceptance**:
  - On ***Ideas for Coastal Virginia Transit***, Nate and Katie publish an open invitation to **every Mayoral candidate across the region** (`Virginia Beach`, `Chesapeake`, `Portsmouth`, and `Hampton`)—as well as the City Council candidates across all six CVTA cities—to meet them at a transit hub, leave their car in the parking lot, pay regular fare, and ride a multi-bus loop with two daily wheelchair riders.
  - Across the entire region, **only one person responds and accepts**: **Scott Tanner** (*real-life Scott Taylor*), a young, political-outsider Navy veteran running for **Mayor of Virginia Beach**, who meets Nate and Katie at the **CCHR** (*Community College of Hampton Roads*, Virginia Beach Campus — real-life *TCC Virginia Beach*) transit hub to ride the four-bus loop across the city with them. Every other mayoral candidate in Chesapeake, Portsmouth, Hampton, and Virginia Beach—and every incumbent and establishment City Council candidate across all six cities—ignores the invitation.

### B. The Exact 4-Bus Circuit (`#29` $\rightarrow$ `#20` $\rightarrow$ `#15` $\rightarrow$ `#12`) & The Expired 2-Hour Transfer
1. **Start at CCHR (*TCC Virginia Beach*) $\rightarrow$ Board CVTA Route `#29` *(later renumbered `#26`)* to Hilltop**:
   - The candidate drives to CCHR, meets Nate and Katie at the bus loop, and boards **Route #29** with them up to **Hilltop**.
2. **Transfer at Hilltop $\rightarrow$ Board CVTA Route `#20` Westbound to Glenrock Mall (*Military Circle*)**:
   - Riding **Route #20** westbound along Virginia Beach Boulevard, they encounter a Spanish-speaking citizen on board the bus, and the candidate speaks Spanish right back to the rider.
3. **Transfer at Glenrock Mall $\rightarrow$ Board CVTA Route `#15` Southbound on US-13 (`Military Highway`)**:
   - With Nate operating the camera on board **Route #15** right as the bus crosses the **Eastern Branch of the Elizabeth River** from Norfolk back into Virginia Beach via **US-13 southbound**, they film the centerpiece segment exposing how Virginia Beach's early-evening bus shutdown locks transit-dependent citizens out of local democracy.
4. **Transfer from Route `#15` $\rightarrow$ Board CVTA Route `#12` Back to CCHR (Paying a Second Fare)**:
   - Upon boarding **Route #12** to return to the **CCHR** hub, Nate and Katie swipe their **30-day CVTA passes** as daily regulars, while the candidate's **2-hour transfer time limit has already expired**—forcing him to pay a second full fare on camera just to complete a single round-trip loop back to his parked car at CCHR.

### C. The Virginia Beach Municipal Center Bus Curfew Catch-22 (`Route #33` & `Route #12`)
- **The `6:00 PM` City Council Lockout**:
  - Formal evening sessions of the **Virginia Beach City Council** at the **Municipal Center** gavel in at **`6:00 PM`**, but the majority of CVTA buses in Virginia Beach stop running around `6:00 PM`. A citizen can take a bus *to* the Municipal Center in the afternoon, but will be **stranded at the Municipal Center** if they stay for the `6:00 PM` meeting:
    - **Northbound from Municipal Center (`Route #33`)**: The last **Route #33** going to **68th Street** leaves at **`6:01 PM`**, and the **`7:01 PM`** trip goes only as far as **19th Street** at the Oceanfront.
    - **Toward CCHR Hub (`Route #33`)**: The last **Route #33** to **CCHR** leaves the Municipal Center at **`6:33 PM`**, at which time the *only* connecting bus left at CCHR is **Route #36**.
    - **The `5:30 PM` (`5:33 PM`) Connecting Deadline**: If a rider needs to connect at **CCHR** to **Routes #12, #25, #29, or #36**, they **must leave the Municipal Center on the `5:30 PM` (`5:33 PM`) Route #33**—nearly half an hour *before* the `6:00 PM` City Council meeting starts.
    - **Katie & Nate's Specific Route Home (`Last #12 at 5:48 PM`)**: Because Katie and Nate live off **Military Highway & Indian River Road**, they must take **Route #33** from the Municipal Center to **CCHR** and transfer onto **Route #12** to get back to Military Highway. Because the **last Route #12 of the day departs CCHR at `5:48 PM`**, Katie and Nate *have* to catch the **`5:30 PM` (`5:33 PM`) Route #33** out of the Municipal Center—meaning their last `#12` bus home pulls out of CCHR **twelve minutes before the `6:00 PM` City Council meeting even begins!**
- **The Spark**: When the video Nate filmed on the `#15` goes viral across local blogs and newspapers—and Katie sees that none of the establishment City Council candidates bothered to ride the bus while transit riders are scheduled out of `6:00 PM` Council meetings—Katie decides to run for **City Council** herself.
