## 3. Katie Prairie's Step-by-Step Political Career Ladder (`2010–2026+`)

### Step 1: Virginia Beach City Council (`Kempsville District` — `Non-Partisan` under Virginia Law)
- **Council Seat & District**: **Virginia Beach City Council — `Kempsville District`** (*`District 2` under the pre-2022 residency-district system / `District 1` under the post-2022 single-member district map*).
  - *In-Universe Predecessor (`2000s – January 2011`)*: **Councilman Harold Dietz** (*real-life Harry E. Diezel*; also referred to in early draft notes as *Arthur Vance*).
  - ***Katie's Term (`January 2011 – January 2014`)***: **Councilwoman Katie Prairie (`Kempsville District`, Non-Partisan)**.
  - *In-Universe Successors (`January 2014 – Present`)*: **Councilwoman Amelia Thornton** (`2014–2022`) $\rightarrow$ **Councilman Marcus Vance** (`District 1 / Kempsville`, `2023–Present`).
- **Partisan Status**: **Non-Partisan** (as mandated by the Code of Virginia for municipal elections).
- **Term Length & Service**: **1 Term (4-Year Term)** — Elected **November 2, 2010** at **Age 25** in a historic **`94.1% to 5.9%` landslide** (living at the family brick ranch at `737 Arvin Rd` off Military Highway & Indian River Road in Kempsville; serving **`January 2011 – December 31, 2013`** before moving up to the Virginia General Assembly).
- **The Complete 2009–2011 Kempsville City Council Campaign & `94.1%` Landslide Arc (`books/katie/e006/c001/p002.md`–`e006/c002/p002.md`)**:
  1. **November 2009 — Silenced at `509 E. 18th Street` & Blackballed from `TRAC`**:
     - At the November 2009 CVTA board meeting in Norfolk, CEO **Leonard Sterling** and COO **Richard Halstead** cut off Nate's public-comment microphone at the 180-second buzzer while he is presenting their `PHP` schedule analysis of broken transfer connections and evening curfews. In the hallway afterward, Halstead dismisses Nate and Katie as *"statistically insignificant hobbyist bloggers,"* refuses to release raw GTFS schedule files, and secretly blackballs Nate's application to the citizen **Transit Riders Advisory Committee (`TRAC`)** as a *"known agitator."* When Kempsville incumbent **Councilman Harold Dietz** then strips late-evening bus service out of the spring 2010 Virginia Beach budget (*"almost nobody rides the bus out of Courthouse after dark anyway"*), Katie and Nate decide to run for Dietz's Kempsville seat.
  2. **Punch #1 (`Thursday, October 14, 2010`) — The `WHRC TV-15` Empty-Podium Debate & The `8:24 PM` Three-Bus Sprint Home**:
     - *Behind-the-Scenes Context (Unknown to Nate & Katie in POV until Election Night)*: Refusing to believe a 25-year-old transit blogger in a power wheelchair poses any threat, incumbent Councilman Harold Dietz never even RSVPs (neither confirming nor declining) to **`WHRC TV-15` (*Coastal Virginia Public Media*)** for the televised Kempsville debate at `5200 Hampton Boulevard` (adjacent to **Virginia Dominion University [`VDU`]**).
     - *On-Air Reality*: Because Dietz never declined, `WHRC TV-15` leaves his tall mahogany podium fully lit and empty right next to Katie's accessible desk for the entire live broadcast (`7:30 PM – 8:14 PM`).
     - *The `8:24 PM` Bus Sprint with Reporter Greg Mercer*: The second the cameras turn off at `8:14 PM`, ***Tidewater Pilot*** Metro reporter **Greg Mercer** asks for ten minutes in the lobby. Checking her watch (`8:15 PM`), Katie invites him to walk across all six lanes of Hampton Boulevard with them right then. Expecting a campaign SUV or a $50,000 wheelchair-lift van, Mercer is stunned when Katie and Nate stop at the southbound **`CVTA Route #2`** bus sign and explain the exact schedule math: they must catch the **`8:24 PM` Route #2** to **Downtown Norfolk Transit Center** (arriving just before `9:00 PM`), transfer to the **`9:15 PM` Route #20** out Virginia Beach Boulevard to **Glenrock Mall** (arriving at `9:45 PM`), and make the **very last Route #15 of the night** going down **Military Highway (`US-13`)** into Virginia Beach—or be stranded in Norfolk overnight. As the `8:24 PM` `#2` kneels and deploys its hydraulic ramp, Katie tells Mercer that **improving the CVTA bus system—specifically Virginia Beach's locally funded share of the regional network—is the entire reason she is running for City Council**.
  3. **Friday, October 15, 2010 — Front-Page Metro Headline & The `WHRC 89.5 FM` Call-In Switchboard**:
     - *The Tidewater Pilot* runs a Section B1 banner story (**"AN EMPTY PODIUM—AND AN 8:24 P.M. BUS TO CATCH"**) with side-by-side photos of the empty `WHRC TV-15` podium and Katie and Nate rolling up the ramp of the `8:24 PM` `#2` bus. At noon on non-profit public radio **`WHRC 89.5 FM`** (hosting ***Tidewater Forum* with Catherine Lindsey**, which legally cannot endorse candidates under 501(c)(3) rules), listeners keep all ten studio call-in lines lit for forty minutes straight cheering Katie on. Behind the scenes, Dietz's Oceanfront and Laskin Road donors threaten to freeze his final $40,000 TV buy as his internal polling crashes from an 8-point lead down to a razor-thin **`0.4%` (`48.2% to 47.8%`)**—inside Virginia's `0.5%` automatic recount threshold.
  4. **Punch #2 (`Thursday, October 28, 2010`, Five Days Before the Election) — The Fatal `WTGA 790 AM` Disability Attack & Letters to the Editor**:
     - Panicking to appease his donors, Dietz calls live into conservative talk radio **`WTGA 790 AM` (*Tidewater Talk 790*)**, mocks Katie's three-bus ride home as a "publicity stunt," and **attacks her disability directly**—asking why Virginia Beach taxpayers should pay tens of thousands of dollars to tear into the mahogany City Council dais in Building 1 to build a wheelchair ramp for one person, and questioning whether a disabled woman who needs her twin brother rolling beside her everywhere has the "stamina" for 12-hour Council sessions.
     - In a military city home to 80,000 veterans and wounded warriors, the backlash is nuclear: donors revoke their endorsements, `WTGA 790 AM` hosts distance themselves as wounded Navy veterans from Little Creek and Oceana flood the lines, and Saturday's *Tidewater Pilot* prints a full-page Opinion spread of **Letters to the Editor** (*"Dietz's attacks on buses, wheelchair ramps cross the line"*: **Master Chief Robert H. Callahan, USN Ret.** from Kempsville who lost both legs in Iraq in 2006; evening retail supervisor **Denise T. Holloway** from College Park; and retired Northwood High School teacher **Margaret L. Sterling** from Bayside).
  5. **Tuesday, November 2, 2010 (`7:19 PM`) — The `94.1% to 5.9%` Landslide & Multi-Station Post-Mortem**:
     - Nineteen minutes after polls close, **`WCVB-TV 10` (*Coastal News 10*)**, **`WTID-TV 3` (*NewsChannel 3*)**, **`WVHR-TV 13` (*13News*)**, and **`WHRC TV-15`** project 25-year-old Katie Prairie the winner in an unprecedented **`94.1% to 5.9%` landslide**, while VDU political scientist **Dr. Quentin Marsh** (on `WCVB-TV 10`), `WVHR-TV 13` (live from Dietz's empty Oceanfront ballroom), `WTID-TV 3` (highlighting how Nate's custom `PHP` precinct-tracking engine drove a 42% turnout surge along the `#12`, `#15`, `#20`, and `#36` bus corridors), and `WTGA 790 AM` dissect how the 1–2 punch of skipping the `WHRC TV-15` debate and attacking Katie's disability turned a `0.4%` race into a `94.1%` wipeout.
  6. **January–May 2011 — The Building 1 Dais Ramp, The `$1.42M` CVTA Escrow Vise, & Joining the CVTA Governing Commission (`TDC` & `TRAC`)**:
     - Upon taking her seat at the Municipal Center (where facilities crews replace the steps on the right side of the curved mahogany dais with a permanent carpeted wheelchair ramp), Katie—who, like her twin brother Nate, does not believe in any religion or any god—exercises her constitutional and statutory right under **Article VI, Clause 3 of the U.S. Constitution**, **Article II, Section 7 of the Constitution of Virginia**, and **Code of Virginia § 49-9** to make a solemn secular **affirmation** (`"I do solemnly affirm..."`) with her left hand on the **Constitution of the Commonwealth of Virginia**, omitting the optional parenthetical `"(so help me God)"`. On **January 18, 2011**, when CVTA CEO **Leonard Sterling** and COO **Richard Halstead** come before the Virginia Beach City Council begging for a `$1.42M` mid-year supplemental check, Councilwoman Prairie locks `$810,000` of executive perks in escrow until CVTA publishes raw open GTFS schedule data and implements the **Evening Civic Access Transit Pulse** (extending Route #33 out of the Municipal Center until `10:30 PM` on Council nights and funding a synchronized `9:00 PM` and `10:00 PM` connecting pulse for Routes `#12`, `#15`, `#25`, `#29`, and `#36` at CCHR). The City Council then votes **`11–0`** to appoint Katie as Virginia Beach's Commissioner on the **CVTA Governing Commission (`TDC`)**, where she becomes the only Commissioner who rides a three-bus chain (`#15` $\rightarrow$ `#20` $\rightarrow$ `#1/#3`) to `509 E. 18th Street` and immediately appoints **Nate** to the **Transit Riders Advisory Committee (`TRAC`)**.

### Step 2: Virginia House of Delegates (`85th House District` — `Democrat`, State Capitol, Richmond)
- **Why Katie Runs as a Democrat (`Lifelong Democratic Household — David, Katie, & Nate`) & Behind-the-Scenes Lore Note on Brenda (`Lore Only — Unknown to the Family in the Novel`)**:
  - **In the Novel (`books/katie/e007/c001/p002.md`)**: While Virginia Beach City Council is non-partisan by law, the reason Katie files and runs as a **Democrat** once she steps up to the state level (`House of Delegates`, `Senate of Virginia`, and `U.S. Senate`) is because **Katie, her twin brother Nate, and their father David Prairie have always been Democrats**. Nate and Katie grew up in a **Democratic household** with David, would have gladly voted for the Democratic candidates when they were teenagers if they had been old enough to vote, and **David, Katie, and Nate have voted Democrat in every single local, state, and federal election since the day they first became eligible to vote** (turning 18 on April 8, 2003).
  - **Behind-the-Scenes Omniscient Lore Note (`Strictly in Lore Notes Only — NEVER in the Novel Prose`)**:
    - **Why Brenda Reverted to Her Maiden Name (`Walsh`) in the 2001 Divorce Decree (`e003/c003/p001.md` & `p003.md`)**: Right after the August 2001 *Sunward Tropics* cruise, sixteen-year-old Katie and Nate told their father David that they never wanted Brenda carrying the **`Prairie`** surname anymore. Respecting his twins' wishes, David and his divorce attorney **Arthur Vance** used **Code of Virginia § 20-109.1** and **§ 20-121.4** to condition Brenda's release from the `$6,000+` cruise credit-card debt and her small walk-away relocation check from the Delaney Street house sale on Brenda signing the **Section 20-121.4 Maiden Name Restoration Order** reverting to her maiden name (**`Brenda Walsh`**). Spiteful and desperate for the debt release and cash check, Brenda signed it—leaving the **`Prairie`** name exclusively to David, Katie, and Nate.
    - **Brenda's Post-Divorce Far-Right Turn & Refusal to Vote for Her Own Daughter**: Because David, Katie, and Nate cut all contact with Brenda after July 2003, the family does not know what happens to Brenda's politics—but in canon lore, after the divorce, **Brenda becomes a die-hard right-wing Republican**, aligning herself with the in-universe **Tea Party (`Heritage Tax Revolt`)**, **MAGA (`Restore the Republic` / `RTR`)**, **MAHA (`Restore American Health` / `RAH`)**, and **President Garrett Thorne (`POTUS`)**. Deep down, Brenda is still bitterly resentful that sixteen-year-old Nate and Katie picked their father David over her in the August 2001 divorce (while Nate and Katie never forgive Brenda for forcing them onto the August 2001 *Sunward Tropics* cruise against their will and especially for trying to force two sixteen-year-old high school juniors to go to the ship's costumed children's character meet-and-greet in `e003/c001/p002.md`). Out of that bitter maternal grievance—masked behind her far-right politics—**Brenda NEVER votes for her own daughter Katie in a single election!**
    - **How Brenda Reacts in `2026–2027` When Katie Becomes *Her* U.S. Senator (The First Office Katie Holds Where Brenda Is a Constituent) & Her `RTR` Friends Piece It Together**:
      1. **Seeing Katie on Her Own Ballot (`November 3, 2026`)**: During Katie's years on the Virginia Beach City Council (`Kempsville`), the House of Delegates (`HD-85`), and the Senate of Virginia (`SD-7 / SD-22`), Brenda didn't live in Katie's districts and could tell herself Katie was someone else's representative. In **November 2026**, for the very first time, Brenda walks into her polling booth and has to stare at **`Katie Prairie — Democratic`** at the top of her own statewide U.S. Senate ballot, furiously voting for the Republican nominee instead.
      2. **Watching David, Nate, and Katie Celebrate on TV**: When the Decision Desks project Katie the winner at `8:42 PM`—and again on **January 3, 2027** when Katie solemnly affirms her U.S. Senate oath without *"So help me God"* and schools **`Beacon News`**, **`LibertyMax`**, and ***The 777 Circle*** live on the Capitol plaza—Brenda sits fuming in front of her TV watching her ex-husband **David** standing proudly beside the twin children who chose him over her.
      3. **The Maiden-Name Catch-22 & Doubling Down When Her `RTR` Friends Figure It Out**: Because Brenda reverted to her maiden name (**`Brenda Walsh`**) in the 2001 divorce decree, she cannot brag to her **`Restore the Republic` (`RTR`)** circle that the new U.S. Senator is her daughter without someone asking why David is in every photo with Katie and Nate while Brenda hasn't been near them in twenty-three years. When longtime Virginia Beach acquaintances in her `RTR` group inevitably connect the dots (*"Wait a second, Brenda—weren't you married to David Prairie over on Delaney Street in the nineties? Isn't Senator Katie Prairie YOUR daughter?!"*), Brenda's face turns purple and she **doubles down**—ranting that David "brainwashed" them into godless liberals rather than ever admitting why her twins locked her out of their lives.
      4. **The Ultimate Salt in the Wound (`Friday, April 16, 2027` — President Thorne's Third Impeachment, `68–32` Senate Conviction, Removal, & Permanent Disqualification) & Why Brenda's `RTR` Friends Cut Her Off**:
         - Three months into Katie's Senate term (`e006/c004/p002.md`), Brenda hosts six women from her local **`Restore the Republic` (`RTR`)** and **`Restore American Health` (`RAH`)** chapter in her Virginia Beach living room to watch `Beacon News` and `LibertyMax` air the final Senate roll call on **President Garrett Thorne's third impeachment**—smugly assuring her friends that just like Thorne's first two impeachments during his first term, the Senate will never reach 67 votes.
         - Instead, Brenda has to watch in horror as Republican institutionalists cross the aisle, the camera cuts to **her own estranged daughter, U.S. Senator Katie Prairie (`D-Virginia`)**, sitting in her compact `SwiftChair` at her mahogany Senate desk (with **Nate** beside her and **David** watching from the Family Gallery), and Katie's calm voice rings out **`"Guilty"`**—sealing the **`68–32` conviction** that immediately removes Garrett Thorne from the Presidency, followed minutes later by the **`58–42` disqualification vote** under `Article I, Section 3, Clause 7` forever barring Thorne from holding any federal office again, and the live swearing-in of Democratic House Speaker **Clara Morales** as President of the United States!
         - When **Sharon**—an acquaintance who knew Brenda back in the late 1990s before the 2001 divorce decree stripped her of the `Prairie` surname—stares at the television screen showing **David, Nate, and Senator Katie Prairie** embracing in the Capitol corridor and blurts out in the middle of Brenda's living room, *"Wait a second, Brenda... David, Katie, and Nathaniel Prairie? From Kempsville? Born in '85? The twins with the muscle condition? Brenda... **is U.S. Senator Katie Prairie your daughter?!**"*—Brenda panics and doubles down in trembling humiliation, claiming that *"David and the woke universities brainwashed them against their own mother."*
         - **Why Her `RTR` Friends—Who Also Blame the "Woke Liberal Witch Hunt Agenda" for Thorne's Removal—Never Look at Brenda the Same Way Again and Cut Her Off**:
           1. **Years of Active, Face-to-Face Deception Under `Brenda Walsh`**: If Brenda had been honest from the day she joined the chapter (*"Senator Katie Prairie is my estranged daughter and we haven't spoken since 2003"*), her `RTR` friends might have pitied her. Instead, hiding behind her restored maiden name **`Brenda Walsh`**, she sat at their kitchen tables, church coffee hours, and chapter meetings through the entire 2026 U.S. Senate campaign, the January 3, 2027 swearing-in controversy, and the April 2027 impeachment trial pretending *"that Prairie woman"* was a complete stranger—nodding along and trashing her as if she didn't know her. Discovering that a friend looked them in the eye for years and hid the fact that **she is the biological mother of the Democratic U.S. Senator from Virginia** makes Brenda look pathologically dishonest and deeply creepy.
           2. **Her "Woke Universities" Excuse Collapses Under Basic High-School Math**: Even as her `RTR` friends agree that a "woke liberal witch hunt" brought down Garrett Thorne, Brenda's excuse that *"David and the woke universities brainwashed my kids"* falls apart on the spot because Sharon knows the timeline: Katie and Nate were born in **1985**, got diagnosed with their progressive neuromuscular condition and started using wheelchairs in **September 2001** when they were **sixteen-year-old juniors at Northwood High School**, and Brenda and David divorced in **October 2001**. They weren't at a "woke university" in 2001—they were newly disabled sixteen-year-old teenagers in her own house! Even among die-hard conservative women in Virginia Beach, there is an unforgivable maternal taboo: **a mother does not lose both of her sixteen-year-old twins—and a father does not force his ex-wife to surrender the family surname in the divorce decree—right after the children end up in wheelchairs unless the mother did something truly shameful to her disabled kids.**
           3. **Everyone in Virginia Beach Has Seen How Close David, Katie, and Nate Are**: Unlike a distant celebrity politician, Katie, Nate, and David are local Virginia Beach fixtures whom people have watched on `WCVB-TV 10`, `WTID-TV 3`, and CVTA buses for seventeen years. Even conservatives who hate Katie's politics can see with their own eyes on the television screen that David is a devoted, quiet father who stood by his disabled twins for twenty-six years, and that Katie and Nate are disciplined, polite, inseparable twins with zero personal scandal—making Brenda's bitter rant in the living room sound unhinged.
           4. **Tribal Scapegoating & Paranoia in the Hour of Thorne's Downfall (`"Your Flesh and Blood Just Ended President Thorne — Were You a Mole?!"`)**: At the exact moment their supreme idol Garrett Thorne is convicted (`68–32`), stripped of the presidency, and barred for life (`58–42`), the `RTR` women are in raw, apocalyptic grief and rage looking for someone in the room to blame—and they realize the Democratic U.S. Senator who helped author the constitutional case against Thorne and just voted to destroy him came straight out of Brenda's womb. Worse, in `RTR`'s hyper-paranoid conspiracy culture, discovering that the mother of **Senator Katie Prairie (`D-Virginia`)** has been sitting inside their private `RTR` chapter meetings, group chats, and mailing lists under a different last name (`Walsh`) triggers immediate suspicion that Brenda couldn't be trusted around their chapter (even though Katie and Nate have no idea Brenda is in `RTR` and haven't spoken to her since 2003).
           5. **The Ultimate Poetic Justice & The Collapse of Brenda's Unauthorized Strip-Mall "Thorne Store" (`Suite 756`, `Timberline Shopping Center` on `SR-410` / Holland Road)**:
              - **Why Customers Falsely Believe the Store Is Authorized by President Thorne**: Just like the real-world roadside and strip-mall "Trump Stores" across America—where everyday shoppers routinely walked through the door **incorrectly believing the store was officially authorized by Donald Trump** and thought buying `$40` hats and `$60` flags was funding his campaign (when in reality the stores had **zero authorization**, bought bulk wholesale knockoffs from overseas factories, hid a tiny 8-point disclaimer behind the register, and pocketed 100% of the retail markup)—Brenda's `RTR` customers genuinely believe her store is an official Garrett Thorne outpost.
              - **The Exact Strip-Mall Location (`Suite 756`, `Timberline Shopping Center` on `CVTA Route 36`)**: Operating under **`Brenda Walsh`** so nobody on the Virginia SCC registry connects her to the rare `Prairie` surname, Brenda maxes out her credit cards (just like she did for the August 2001 cruise!) to lease vacant **`Suite 756`** in **`Timberline Shopping Center`** on the north side of **`SR-410` (*Holland Road*)** just east of the **`SR-225` (*South Independence Boulevard*) / `Silverleaf`** split (anchored by **`General Dollar`** [*Dollar General*], directly across `SR-410` from **`Holland Square`** anchored by **`Market Square`** [*Food Lion*]).
              - **The Left-to-Right 5-Bay Lineup (`Suites 732–756`) & The Ultimate Irony**:
                1. **`Don Miller Subs`** (*real-life Jon Smith Subs*)
                2. **`Neat Stuff` (`Suite 732`)** (*real-life Cool Stuff* — the retro-gaming, vintage toy, and collectibles sister store to **`Pixel Haven`** [*Video Game Heaven*], where **Katie and Nate** regularly ride **`CVTA Route 36`** to browse retro `Nova 64`, `Super Kinoka`, and `PlayBrick` games!)
                3. **`Halo Nails & Spa`** (*real-life Angel Nails & Spa*)
                4. **`Pho 79 Vietnamese Kitchen`** (*real-life Pho 78 Vietnamese Restaurant* — an immigrant-owned Vietnamese restaurant sharing a thin drywall party wall right next door to Brenda's *"America First"* white-nationalist store, filling Brenda's shop with the aroma of simmering star anise and beef pho broth all day!)
                5. **`Suite 756` — Brenda Walsh's Unauthorized "Thorne Store" (*The Tidewater Thorne & RTR Superstore*) & The Early 42nd Birthday Face-to-Face Encounter With `Sharron` (`Saturday, April 3, 2027`, `e006/c004/p002.md`)**: Three doors to the right of **`Neat Stuff` (`Suite 732`)**! On **Saturday, April 3, 2027** (during the Senate's Spring State Work Period ahead of the April 14–16 impeachment trials, and **five days before Katie and Nate's 42nd birthday on Thursday, April 8, 2027**), **U.S. Senator Katie Prairie**, **Nate**, and **David** ride **`CVTA Route 36`** to **`Timberline Shopping Center`** so Dad can treat his twins to their traditional early birthday lunch at **`Don Miller Subs`** and buy them their 42nd birthday retro `PlayBrick` (*Gorilla King '94*) and `Super Kinoka` cartridges next door at **`Neat Stuff` (`Suite 732`)**. Spotting the brand-new *Tidewater Thorne & RTR Superstore* banners at **`Suite 756`** for the first time—and having no idea who leased the storefront—Katie's Senatorial consumer-protection curiosity gets the better of her (`are the rumors true that this store is officially vetted and licensed by Garrett Thorne, his company, or his campaign?`), leading all three of them to roll inside `Suite 756`, uncover the hidden 8-point disclaimer behind the button jar and the **`PROPRIETOR: BRENDA WALSH`** license on the wall, and come face-to-face with **Brenda** (who had completely forgotten that her own twins' April 8th birthday was five days away!) for the first time since July 2003—right in front of three women from Brenda's **`RTR`** chapter (**`Sharron`**, **`Donna`**, and **`Carol`**), including former Delaney Street neighbor **Sharron** who remembers Nate and Katie growing up in the 1990s and demands: *"Wait—your daughter is OUR U.S. Senator?! AND their birthdays are coming up on Thursday, and you didn't even remember?! Brenda, it's time for you to come clean!"*
              - **The Triple Collapse (`April 3–30, 2027`, `e006/c004/p002.md` & `p003.md`)**:
                - Betting everything that Garrett Thorne will beat his third impeachment just like his first two, Brenda puts `$22,000` on her high-interest retail credit line in early April 2027 to stock dozens of boxes of unlicensed **`"THORNE: 3-0 UNDEFEATED"`** and **`"THORNE 2028"`** hats, flags, mugs, and life-size cardboard cutouts.
                - On **Saturday, April 3, 2027** (`e006/c004/p002.md`), after **Sharron** (the founder and president of the local Virginia Beach `RTR` chapter—which, just like the "Thorne Stores," is not officially endorsed or licensed by Garrett Thorne) exposes in the middle of `Suite 756` that *"Brenda Walsh"* is actually **U.S. Senator Katie Prairie's biological mother** who mistreated her disabled twins at age sixteen, forgot their upcoming 42nd birthday, and has been pocketing her chapter members' money for unlicensed overseas hats behind a hidden 8-point disclaimer, **Sharron, Donna, and Carol—horrified as mothers and grandmothers by what Brenda did to make her own two disabled children cut her off—unpin their crimson `RTR` lapel badges, drop them onto Brenda's glass counter, quit the `RTR` movement on the spot (with Sharron shutting down the chapter that night), apologize to Katie, Nate, and David, wish Katie and Nate a heartfelt Happy Forty-Second Birthday, and swear they are voting Democrat in the next election!**
                - Thirteen days later, on **Friday, April 16, 2027** (`e006/c004/p003.md`), **her own estranged daughter, U.S. Senator Katie Prairie (`D-Virginia`)**, votes **`Guilty`** (`68–32`) to convict and remove Thorne and **`Aye`** (`68–32`) under `Article I, Section 3, Clause 7` to **permanently disqualify Garrett Thorne from ever holding federal office again**—turning every single box of `"THORNE 2028"` and `"3-0 UNDEFEATED"` merch in Brenda's back room into worthless plastic in fifteen minutes flat!
                - And as the final cosmic insult, two weeks after his removal from office, Garrett Thorne—scrambling to monetize his name to pay his own legal bills—has **`Thorne Enterprises`** trademark attorneys send ruthless **Cease-and-Desist and Trademark Infringement Demand Letters** to every unauthorized "Thorne Store" in the country, meaning **Brenda's own idol's lawyers force her to shutter `Suite 756`**, leaving her completely alone with a broken strip-mall lease and a garage full of unsellable `"THORNE 2028"` hats.
- **Constitutional Title & District**: **Delegate Katie Prairie (`D-Virginia Beach`, `85th House District`)** in the 100-member **Virginia House of Delegates** (the lower chamber of the Virginia General Assembly in Richmond, representing Kempsville, Bellamy Manor, Brandon/Morrison, and Larkspur under the `2011–2023` decennial legislative map).
  - *In-Universe Predecessor (`2010 – January 2014`)*: **Delegate Richard Tatum (`R-Virginia Beach`, `HD-85`)** *(with adjacent `HD-81` represented by **Delegate Barton King, `R-Virginia Beach`** / real-life Barry Knight)*.
  - ***Katie's Terms (`January 2014 – January 2018`)***: **Delegate Katie Prairie (`D-Virginia Beach`, `85th House District`)** — Elected **November 2013** (Age 28) and re-elected **November 2015** (Age 30).
  - *In-Universe Successors (`January 2018 – Present`)*: **Delegate Andre Askew (`D-Virginia Beach`, `HD-85`, `2018–2024`)** (*real-life Alex Askew*) $\rightarrow$ post-2023 redistricting **Delegate Clara Fowler (`D-Virginia Beach`, `HD-96`, `2024–Present`)** (*real-life Kelly Convirs-Fowler*) and **Delegate Arthur Reed (`R-Virginia Beach`, `HD-98`, `2024–Present`)** (*real-life Andrew Rice*).
- **Term Length & Service**: **2 Terms (2-Year Terms, Odd-Year Elections — 4 Years Total)** — Serving **`January 2014 – January 2018`**.
- **Virginia "Citizen Legislature" Rhythm**:
  - Because the Virginia General Assembly is a part-time legislature that meets in Richmond for **60 days in even-numbered years** and **45 days in odd-numbered years** (January through late February / early March), Katie and Nate continue living in Kempsville (`737 Arvin Rd` off Military Highway & Indian River Road through October 2019, and `741 Lord Nelson Dr` from October 2019 onward once `CVTA Route 24` launches) for ten months of the year and stay together in an accessible apartment near Capitol Square in Richmond during the winter legislative session.
- **Legislative Highlights & The Viral January 2016 Bathroom Bill Speech**:

Katie Prairie (`D-Virginia Beach`, `85th District`)** becomes a national news sensation when she launches a fiery 95-second floor question on **Thursday afternoon, February 4, 2016**—which is also **Virginia Transit Legislative Day**, the annual day when transit riders across the Commonwealth travel to Richmond to lobby for public transit dollars—on the Floor of the Virginia House of Delegates against the Republican "anatomical sex" bathroom bill (`HB 663`, patroned by **Delegate Martin Cobb**, followed by `HB 1612` in 2017 patroned by **Delegate Robert Marsh**), demanding of the bill's patron why she, or any woman in Virginia, would have to pull her pants down to prove she has female parts just to use a public restroom. Captured and captioned by Nate from his ADA floor bay beside Delegate Ronald Villa, the clip is immediately shared across **VDU**, **CCHR**, and **CPI** by four transit-riding families across five novels who watched Katie's speech from the third-floor House gallery and visited `Room 518` that afternoon: (1) their childhood Delaney Street and `801 Greenway Court` (`Apartment A5`) neighbors **Michael and Rachel Ragsdale** (`books/rachel/e021/c002/p001.md`, with 28-year-old Michael in his blue manual wheelchair two weeks after bilateral Achilles heel cord lengthening surgery and 26-year-old Rachel guarding his push-handles, who visit at `2:15 PM`, where Michael shares his promise that if he had seen Delegate Cobb or Delegate Marsh heading toward the men's room in the General Assembly Building he would have wheeled right up and demanded they pull their pants down to prove they had male parts); and (2) at `2:40 PM` (forming a semi-circle of **seven wheelchairs** in `Room 518`), the New River Valley / **Blue Ridge Transit (`BRT`)** and **Radford Transit** delegations from Christiansburg and **Commonwealth Polytechnic Institute (`CPI`)** in Blacksburg—**Jordan** (in `*S.S. Freedom*` with his `mPad` AAC), **Aubrie Jean** (who grew up in Chesapeake's Emerald Creek neighborhood right across the city line from Kempsville), **Megan**, **Zoë**, and **Isabel** (`books/aubrie/e008/c001/p002.md`), alongside **Freddie** (in his matte-black titanium wheelchair `*The Rover*`), **Harper** (in her neon-purple wheelchair), **Paige**, **Jessica**, **Sarah**, and **Jenna** (`books/paige/e005/c007/p003.md`), and **Ryan O'Connell** (propelling his matte-black titanium manual wheelchair), **Cassidy O'Connell** (holding onto Ryan's rear push-handles like a rolling walker), **Holly O'Connell**, and twin cousins **Evan & Tyler Wright** of **The Stardust Engine** (`books/stardust-engine/e005/c001/p003.md`, who ride local **Blue Ridge Transit** buses whenever they stay local to Progress Street NE in Blacksburg and Christiansburg)—all of whom hate `HB 663` just as fiercely and blast Nate's captioned video across `CPI` and **Engine Room Records**. During Page orientation week in January, Sam, Mandy, and Sarah had taught Katie and Nate the difference between Richmond's **"Southside Virginia"** (Danville / `US-58`) and **"Southside Hampton Roads"** (Norfolk / Virginia Beach), and after Katie shielded them from an impatient shouting legislator and shared that she and Nate are autistic too, **Delegate Katie Prairie became the triplets' absolute favorite Delegate in the House**—inspiring all three triplets to choose **public transportation** as their #1 issue as House Pages because Katie and Nate famously ride the bus and train everywhere. ### Step 5: Senator Prairie's Signature Commute (`ARTS` Business Class from `NFK` to `ALX` Mobile Lift $\rightarrow$ Capital Transit Yellow Line Over the Charles R. Fenwick Bridge $\rightarrow$ `L'Enfant Plaza` $\rightarrow$ Blue Line to `Capitol South`)
- **The "Amtrak Joe" Parallel with Autistic Sensory & Transit-Insider Precision**:
  - Rather than isolating herself inside the Washington beltway bubble or riding in black SUVs up I-95, **U.S. Senator Katie Prairie (`D-Virginia`)** commutes regularly by rail on the **ARTS (*American Rail Transit Service*) Northeast Regional** from **Norfolk (`NFK`)** alongside Nate (and on her January 3, 2027 Swearing-In Day, their father **David Prairie**).
  - **Buying Regular Passenger `Business Class` Tickets for the Free Café Car Drinks, Conductor At-Seat Café Delivery, & The Twins' Identical Food Order (`"You Have My Biscuit!"` / `"You Have My Juice!"`)**:
    - Instead of asking for a government VIP car or special arrangements, Katie, Nate, and their dad buy regular passenger tickets in **ARTS Business Class**—specifically because Business Class comes with **complimentary non-alcoholic beverages (free apple juice, ginger ale, and soda for Katie and Nate, plus black coffee for their dad David) from the Café Car**!
    - **Why a Conductor Brings Their Food From the Café Car**: Because crossing the narrow, shifting steel vestibule diaphragm between railcars on a moving train is unsafe for `SwiftChair` power wheelchairs, Katie and Nate never cross between cars while the train is moving; instead, the Business Class conductor takes their order at their wheelchair mobility bays and brings their food and complimentary Business Class drinks back from the Café Car.
    - **Copying Each Other's Food Orders & Playful Twin Teasing**: Because Katie and Nate are twins, they copy each other for food all the time (for example, on the `6:05 AM` Sunday `Train 164` ride to *Meet the Nation* in `books/katie/e007/c003/p002.md`, both order a **bacon, egg, and cheese biscuit**, a **Danish**, and complimentary **apple juice**). Whenever they are wide awake and in a playful mood, they playfully tease each other about their identical food by saying, ***"You have my biscuit!"*** or ***"You have my juice!"*** (whereas at `6:20 AM` on `Train 164`, when they are half asleep on four hours of sleep, they are not in a playful mood and eat quietly in sleepy twin synchrony).
  - **Why She Switches at `ALX` (*Alexandria*) on the `ARTS Mobile Lift` Instead of Riding to `WAS` (*Washington Union Station*)**:
    - Because Katie and Nate are autistic, they strongly prefer **Alexandria (`ALX`)** over the cavernous, echoing, crowded sensory overload of **Washington Union Station (`WAS`)** (and skipping `WAS` also avoids the slow train crawl across the Long Bridge and through the First Street Tunnel).
    - Because **`ALX`** has a **low-level red-brick platform**, the station attendant rolls the portable **`ARTS Mobile Lift`** right up to the open Business Class vestibule door to lower Nate and Senator-Elect Katie Prairie down to the brick platform—leaving fellow passengers and commuters wondering in amazement why the newly elected U.S. Senator is getting off at Alexandria (*"Wait—is that Senator-Elect Prairie getting off the ARTS train on the mobile lift at ALX?"*) as she, Nate, and their dad head straight toward the adjacent **`King St–Old Town`** Capital Transit Metro station.
  - **The Yellow-to-Blue Line Shortcut (`King St–Old Town` $\rightarrow$ `L'Enfant Plaza` $\rightarrow$ `Capitol South`)**:
    - Although the **Capital Transit Blue Line** runs directly from `King St–Old Town` to `Capitol South`, taking the Blue Line all the way is the long way around via `Rosslyn` and `Foggy Bottom`.
    - Like true transit regulars, Senator Prairie and Nate tap their **`CapitalPass`** cards to board the **Capital Transit Yellow Line** at `King St–Old Town`, ride across the Potomac River over the **Charles R. Fenwick Bridge** into **`L'Enfant Plaza`**, and transfer to the **Blue Line** (or Orange/Silver Line) for the quick two-stop hop to **`Capitol South`**.
  - **Senator Prairie's Statewide Constituent Transit Rule**:
    - Throughout her tenure in the U.S. Senate, whenever Senator Katie Prairie visits constituents anywhere in the Commonwealth of Virginia (whether in Hampton Roads on **CVTA**, in Williamsburg on **CTTA / WCTA**, in Blacksburg/Roanoke on **Blue Ridge Transit (`BRT`)**, in Richmond on **JRTC (*James River Transit Company* / *JRTC Transit System*)**, or in Northern Virginia on **Capital Transit / BOLT / ARC / Fairfax Link**), **she always rides that region's local public transit system and pays regular fare as part of her constituent visit** so she experiences the buses, trains, lifts, ramps, and transfer schedules firsthand alongside everyday riders.
  - **Solemnly Affirming Her Oath on the Constitution Without *"So Help Me God"* (`Virginia Constitution` & `U.S. Constitution — Secular Affirmation & Debunking the 1789 Washington Myth`)**:
    - Because neither Katie nor Nate believes in any religion or any god, whenever Katie is sworn into Virginia municipal or state office (**Virginia Beach City Council**, **Virginia House of Delegates**, and **Senate of Virginia**), she places her left hand on a bound copy of the **Constitution of the Commonwealth of Virginia**, exercises her constitutional and statutory right under **Article VI, Clause 3 of the U.S. Constitution**, **Article II, Section 7 of the Constitution of Virginia**, and **Code of Virginia § 49-9** to say **`"I do solemnly affirm..."`**, and stops cleanly at **`"...according to the best of my ability."`**—omitting the optional parenthetical `"(so help me God)"`.
    - When she is sworn into the **United States Senate** on **January 3, 2027**, she places her left hand on a bound leather copy of the **Constitution of the United States of America** held by her twin brother Nate and solemnly **affirms** her oath (`"I do solemnly affirm"`, omitting *"So help me God"* under `Article VI, Clause 3`, `5 U.S.C. § 3331`, and `1 U.S.C. § 1`).
    - Whenever the GOP, right-wing cable outlets (**`Beacon News`** [*Fox News*], **`LibertyMax`** [*Newsmax*]), and her hometown Virginia Beach televangelist flagship ***The 777 Circle*** on the **Covenant Broadcasting Network (`TCBN`)** (*real-life The 700 Club / Christian Broadcasting Network* off Indian River Road & I-64 in Kempsville) have a field day attacking her for omitting *"So help me God"*, Katie gladly goes on camera and reminds everyone of the exact constitutional and historical truth: (1) **Article VI, Clause 3** explicitly guarantees Affirmation and bans all religious tests for public office; (2) even the Presidential Oath in **Article II, Section 1, Clause 8** does not contain *"So help me God"*; and (3) the claim that George Washington added *"So help me God"* in 1789 was **debunked by historians decades ago** (zero eyewitnesses in 1789 recorded it; Rufus Wilmot Griswold invented it 65 years later in an 1854 book, and Chester A. Arthur in 1881 is the first President confirmed to have said it).
  - **U.S. Senate Year-Round Session Rhythm vs. Virginia General Assembly Part-Time Sprint**:
    - Unlike the part-time Virginia General Assembly in Richmond (`60 days` in even years / `45 days` in odd years during Jan–Feb), the **U.S. Senate is in session almost year-round** (`~150–190 legislative days` across `32–36 weeks` from **January 3 through mid-to-late December**, with a `Monday 5:30 PM` first vote to `Thursday ~2:00 PM` last vote weekly rhythm). During session weeks, Katie and Nate ride **ARTS** north on Monday morning to sleep **3 nights (`Mon–Wed`)** in the ground-floor back master walk-in closet sleeping-bag sanctuary at David's house on **Sunset Drive** in Alexandria, and ride **ARTS** south on Thursday afternoon to sleep **4 nights (`Thu–Sun`)** at their own 50–50 home at **`741 Lord Nelson Drive`** in Kempsville (plus the 4–5 week August State Work Period and scheduled recess weeks).

## 4. Primary Residency in Kempsville (Virginia Beach) & Her Father's Two-Story Brick House on Sunset Drive (Alexandria)

- **Legal Domicile, Voter Registration, & 50–50 Homeownership in Kempsville (`Virginia Beach`)**:
  - Katie and Nate's permanent legal residence is the home they own **50–50 together in the Kempsville section of Virginia Beach**.
  - Kempsville is where both Katie and Nate claim official residency, where they are registered to vote, and where they pay their real-estate and personal property taxes to the City of Virginia Beach.
- **Sleeping in Virginia During U.S. Senate Sessions (`David Prairie's Sunset Drive House in Alexandria`)**:
  - When the U.S. Senate is in session, Senator Katie Prairie never rents an apartment or sleeps inside the District of Columbia—instead, she and Nate take the **ARTS** train up from Norfolk (`NFK`) and sleep at their father **David Prairie's** **two-story red-brick house on Sunset Drive in Alexandria, Virginia** (modeled on the brick Colonial architecture at `1921 King Street` / `Sunset Drive`, referred to in prose simply as **"Sunset Drive"** without a house number) so she can take the train for day trips into D.C. to conduct the Senate's business and still sleep in the Commonwealth of Virginia every single night.
  - David owned the Sunset Drive house long before Katie was first elected to the Virginia Beach City Council in November 2010; the Alexandria house is **100% paid off** and titled **100% in her father David Prairie's name**.

### A. Exterior Geography & Transit Sightlines (`Sunset Drive` $\leftrightarrow$ `King St–Old Town` & `ALX`)
- **Right on the Other Side of the King Street Railroad Tunnel/Underpass**:
  - The house sits immediately west of the steel railroad overpass/tunnel where King Street passes beneath the **Atlantic & Chesapeake Rail (`A&C Rail`** — *real-life CSX*) / **ARTS** tracks and the **Capital Transit** Metro tracks.
  - Looking out from the front yard and upstairs windows, you can see both **King St–Old Town Metro Station** and the elevated rail embankment of **Alexandria Union Station (`ALX`)** right across the underpass.
  - A wide, flat sidewalk runs straight through the short King Street railroad underpass into the **`King St–Old Town` Station bus loop** (served by **`BOLT`** [*real-life Alexandria DASH*] and **`Capital Transit`** buses), the **`King St–Old Town` Metro** entrance, and **`ALX`**—giving Katie and Nate effortless, step-free access to buses and trains while allowing Senator Katie Prairie to sleep in the Commonwealth of Virginia every single night.

### B. Two-Story Floor Plan & Vertical Platform Lift Architecture
- **Interior Through-Floor Vertical Platform Wheelchair Lift (`Ground Floor` $\leftrightarrow$ `Second Floor`)**:
  - To make the two-story brick house 100% accessible for both Katie and Nate's compact `SwiftChair` power wheelchairs, they install an **interior residential vertical platform wheelchair lift** (a quiet, enclosed through-floor vertical lift shaft with flush threshold gates on the first and second floors) alongside the central hallway staircase so both siblings can roll between the ground floor and the second floor at will (while David walks up and down the central staircase).
- **First Floor (Ground Level)**:
  - **Front Living Room & Dining Room (Facing the Street)**: Positioned at the front of the house behind the shuttered sash windows so anyone walking past on the sidewalk only sees a traditional living and dining room.
  - **Accessible Kitchen & Ground-Floor Roll-In Bathroom**: Located in the center of the first floor with roll-under counters and a curbless roll-in shower.
  - **Ground-Floor Back Master Bedroom (*Officially Katie's Bedroom — Shared by Katie & Nate*)**:
    - Built in the **private back corner of the ground floor** overlooking the fenced backyard—specifically positioned **in the back of the house so nobody on the street can ever peer into a front window to see two adult opposite-gender siblings sharing a bed**.
    - Furnished with their shared **Queen-sized bed** beneath the framed blue butterfly print and dual wall chargers for their two compact `SwiftChair` power wheelchairs.
- **Second Floor (Upper Level — Reached via the Vertical Platform Lift or Stairs)**:
  - **Bedroom 2 (*Officially Nate's Bedroom — Unused*)**:

  - **Bedroom 3 (*David's Bedroom*)**:
    - Upstairs bedroom for their father **David Prairie**, furnished with a **Full-sized bed** and adjacent upstairs bath.
  - **Bedroom 4 (*The Upstairs Three-Desk Coding & Remote-Work Office Overlooking the Trains*)**:

## 5. Senator Katie Prairie's Policy Platform, Constituent Transit Protocol, & 2026 GOP Campaign Rebuttals

Throughout her sixteen-year rise from the **Virginia Beach City Council** (`2011–2014`) to the **Virginia House of Delegates** (`2014–2018`), the **Senate of Virginia** (`2020–2026`), and her **November 3, 2026 election to the United States Senate** (`Sworn in January 3, 2027`), Katie Prairie builds an unmistakable legislative identity rooted in lived transit, disability, and working-class experience—drawing predictable bad-faith distortions from Republican opposition operatives.

### B. Senator Prairie's Statewide Constituent Transit Rule & Theme Park Rail-to-Bus Links

- **The Unbreakable Constituent Transit Rule (Always Paying Regular Fare)**:
  - Whenever Katie Prairie visits constituents anywhere in the Commonwealth of Virginia—whether in Hampton Roads (`CVTA`), the Historic Triangle (`CTTA`/`WCTA`), Richmond (`JRTC`), the New River & Roanoke Valleys (`BRT` / `RVT`), or Northern Virginia (`Capital Transit` / `BOLT` / `ARC` / `Fairfax Link`)—**she always rides that region's local public transit system and pays the standard regular fare**.
  - **February 2012 — Stopped at the Third-Floor Gallery by Capitol Police, Seated on the House Floor Near Delegate Ronald Villa (`R-Virginia Beach`, `21st District`), & The Spark to Run (`books/katie/e007/c001/p001.md` & `p002.md`)**:
    - After winning the non-partisan **Kempsville District** seat on the **Virginia Beach City Council** in November 2010 and passing her landmark **Evening Civic Access Transit Pulse** in early 2011 (`books/katie/e006/c001/p002.md`–`e006/c002/p002.md`), twenty-six-year-old Councilwoman Katie Prairie and Nate visit the Virginia State Capitol in February 2012 to testify for state transit matching funds and ride the elevator up to the third-floor public viewing gallery. **Virginia Division of Capitol Police** gently stop their wheelchairs outside the gallery doors because the small historic elevator locks out during a fire alarm, leaving no safe way to evacuate wheelchairs down three flights of narrow 18th-century stairs in an emergency.
    - Capitol Police speak to the Sergeant-at-Arms and House Leadership, who grant Nate and Katie (a wheelchair user and one ADA companion) the rare honor of coming down to sit right on the **first-floor House of Delegates Chamber Floor** beside **Delegate Ronald Villa (`R-Virginia Beach`, `21st House District` — adapted from real-life *Delegate Ronald Villanueva*)**, whom they remember and respect from his years serving At-Large on the Virginia Beach City Council when he fought to get more covered bus shelters built for CVTA bus riders.
    - Sitting at floor level beside Delegate Villa's mahogany desk when the incumbent 85th District Delegate (**Delegate Richard Tatum, `R-Virginia Beach`**) across the chamber votes against public transit operating funds without even looking up from his phone gives Katie the spark to run for the **85th House District** herself in November 2013 (`books/katie/e007/c001/p002.md`, where Katie, Nate, and Dad David brainstorm every statutory step at their Kempsville kitchen table, collect 412 verified voter signatures at the CCHR bus loop with Dad serving as Campaign Treasurer, defeat Tatum `54.4%` to `45.6%`, and wrap up her City Council service in December 2013)—and that same Capitol Police third-floor emergency-egress rule is why Nate holds a permanent ADA floor pass to watch Katie's floor speeches (including the February 2016 `House Bill 663` speech in `books/katie/e007/c003/p001.md`) from the wheelchair bay on the House Floor right beside Delegate Ronald Villa's desk.
  - **Richmond General Assembly Winter-Session Apartment (`Broad & Boulevard Lofts, Apartment 104`), `JRTC Route 6` Annunciator Rules, & Why Katie Never Has `HOUSE OF DELEGATES` / `SENATE` License Plates (`January 2014–2026`)**:
    - Five days ahead of Katie's very first Virginia General Assembly session (`Friday, January 3, 2014`, ahead of `Wednesday, January 8, 2014` Opening Day), twenty-eight-year-old Freshman Delegate Katie Prairie and Nate move into **Apartment 104** at the **Broad & Boulevard Lofts** on **West Broad Street near Boulevard** (at the edge of Scott's Addition and the Museum District) with their dad David helping them unpack their shared queen bed (using their own familiar comforter and pillows from Kempsville) and dual-laptop table overlooking West Broad Street.
    - Right outside the front doors on West Broad Street is the **`JRTC Route 6 (Broad Street)`** bus stop. Katie and Nate buy standard $60 30-day unlimited **JRTC (*James River Transit Company*)** magnetic swipe passes like any other bus rider, spend the weekend mapping the eleven-minute eastbound ride to **Ninth and Broad Street (Capitol Square)** and the ten-minute westbound ride to **Oak Lawn (`Market Square`, adapted from *Willow Lawn Shopping Center*)** for groceries, and shock veteran Delegates, State Senators with gold `VIRGINIA — HOUSE OF DELEGATES` and `VIRGINIA — SENATE` license plates, and corporate lobbyists on Monday morning (`January 6, 2014`) when they roll down the front wheelchair ramp of a regular city transit bus at Ninth and Broad, return their reserved General Assembly Parking Deck transponder so a Capitol custodian can have the spot, and use a 25-foot steel tape measure and `ADAAG Section 4.17.3` to force Capitol Facilities to install 60-inch outward-swinging wheelchair restroom stalls and blue ADA push-button door openers twenty hours before Opening Day.
    - **Canonical `JRTC` Automated Voice Annunciator System**:
      1. When the front doors of the bus open, the automated voice announces **once (not repeating)** using canonical fictional destination names: `"Route [number]. [Destination]. Welcome aboard JRTC!"` (e.g., `"Route 6. Oak Lawn. Welcome aboard JRTC!"` for outbound buses, or `"Route 6. Downtown. Welcome aboard JRTC!"` for inbound buses).
      2. Exterior/interior safety announcements: `"Caution! Bus is turning right. Pedestrians, look both ways!"` / `"Caution! Bus is turning left. Pedestrians, look both ways!"`, `"Caution: bus is kneeling!"`, `"Caution: lift is deploying!"`, and `"Caution: lift is stowing!"`.
      3. Interior GPS cross-street announcements: **JRTC buses announce every single cross street** even if it is not a bus stop. If the bus is going straight, it announces **just the cross street**; if the bus just completed a turn, it announces **both streets**.
    - **Medical Driving Restrictions, `HOUSE OF DELEGATES` / `SENATE` License Plates, & Family Driving Rule**:
      - Neither Katie nor Nate can drive due to their medical restrictions, and neither of them owns a motor vehicle—so Katie never applies for or holds `HOUSE OF DELEGATES` or `SENATE` DMV license plates (which in Virginia can only be issued to a vehicle registered to or leased by the elected lawmaker).
      - Whenever someone drives Katie and Nate in a private motor vehicle, **the only person they want driving them is their father, David Prairie**, in his own vehicle (unless, in her capacity as an elected official, security protocol ever mandates an official driver the way the President of the United States must always be driven by the Secret Service). For all their normal daily commuting in Hampton Roads (`CVTA`), Richmond (`JRTC`), and Washington/Northern Virginia (`ARTS` and `Capital Transit`), Katie and Nate ride public transit and pay regular fare.
  - She never blocks off a bus for a staged photo op or accepts VIP passes; she and Nate wait at the regular stop, roll up the bus ramp into the front wheelchair securement bays in their compact `SwiftChair` power wheelchairs, and tap their fare cards or drop full fare into the box alongside everyday riders.
- **1. The East–West Commonwealth Rail Corridor (`Norfolk` $\rightarrow$ `Richmond` $\rightarrow$ `Doswell` $\rightarrow$ `Shenandoah Valley` $\rightarrow$ `Christiansburg` $\rightarrow$ `Bristol`)**:
  - **Real-World Context**: Because Virginia's passenger rail lines were historically built solely as North–South spokes toward Washington, D.C., there is no direct East–West passenger train connecting Hampton Roads and Richmond to Charlottesville, the Shenandoah Valley (including Fishersville/Staunton/Waynesboro near `RRC`), the New River Valley (`Christiansburg`), or `Bristol`—forcing rail riders to detour all the way north to Alexandria (`ALX`) just to head southwest, or trapping travelers between tractor-trailers on I-81 and I-64 over Afton Mountain.
  - **Katie's Stance**: Champions the complete **East–West Commonwealth & Mountain Valley Rail Link**, routing **ARTS** trains from **Norfolk (`NFK`)** and **Richmond** north to the **Doswell Junction** diamond (where the North–South main line crosses the East–West mountain branch tracks), turning west through **Charlottesville** and the **Shenandoah Valley** (serving **Fishersville / `RRC`**), and continuing down the I-81 spine through the **Roanoke Valley**, **Christiansburg (`CPI`)**, and all the way to **Bristol** at the Tennessee line.
  - **GOP Rebuttal**: *"Katie Prairie wants to raid rural highway and gas-tax funds to waste billions on a 'Train to Nowhere' through the mountains instead of widening I-81 and I-64 for hardworking drivers and truckers."*
- **2. The Doswell Station (`DWL`) & `Old Dominion Adventure Park` Shuttle**:
  - **Real-World Context**: In Doswell (Hanover County), the East–West rail tracks run directly along the northern edge of **Old Dominion Adventure Park** (*real-life Kings Dominion*), less than half a mile from the front gate, yet thousands of seasonal teenage workers and visiting families have zero public transit access off I-95 Exit 98.
  - **Katie's Stance**: Champions a high-level, ADA-accessible **Doswell–Old Dominion Station** at the rail junction paired with a dedicated, ramp-equipped **Old Dominion Park Shuttle** connecting every incoming `ARTS` train straight to the theme park front gate—giving youth workers and car-free or disabled families direct rail-to-shuttle access.
  - **GOP Rebuttal**: *"Prairie is spending millions in taxpayer infrastructure money to build a 'Roller Coaster Train' for amusement park tourists."*
- **3. Promoting the Existing `WBG` Train-to-`Route 1` Bus Link to `Brewster Gardens`**:
  - **Real-World Context**: At the **Williamsburg Transportation Center (`WBG`)**, the local **CTTA / WCTA** (*real-life WATA*) **Route 1** bus already boards just steps from the **ARTS** train platform and runs directly to **Brewster Gardens** (*real-life Busch Gardens Williamsburg*), though many statewide travelers have no idea the connection exists.
  - **Katie's Stance**: Rather than wasting money on duplicative studies when a working bus line already runs, Katie uses her statewide megaphone—and rides the exact `ARTS` $\rightarrow$ `WBG` $\rightarrow$ **Route 1** connection with Nate while paying regular fare—to show families and hospitality workers how easily they can take the train to Williamsburg and hop on **Route 1** straight to **Brewster Gardens**.
  - **GOP Rebuttal**: *"Senator Prairie is acting like a taxpayer-funded travel agent for theme parks instead of focusing on serious highway congestion."*

Mark Cole, R-Spotsylvania; in-universe **Delegate Martin Cobb, `R-Spotsylvania`**)*, which mandated that all restrooms in Virginia public schools, state colleges (`CCHR`, `C&A`, `CPI`, `VDU`), and state/local government buildings be used strictly according to **"anatomical sex,"** enforced by a **$50 civil fine** issued by law enforcement. In **January 2017**, House Republicans followed up with **House Bill 1612 (`HB 1612`, the *"Physical Privacy Act"*)** *(patroned in real life by Del. Bob Marshall, R-Prince William; in-universe **Delegate Robert Marsh**)*.

Howell)* — Institutional presiding officer and top House leader at the marble rostrum; controls the chamber, recognizes members, maintains order, refers legislation to committees, and appoints all standing-committee memberships.

       - **Deputy Majority Leader: `Delegate Todd Gentry` (`R-Shenandoah`)** *(counterpart to Deputy Majority Leader Todd Gilbert)* — Assists Richard with floor management.

Chris Jones)* and **General Laws Chair `Delegate Gilbert Peace` (`R-Mechanicsville`)** (whose committee reported `HB 663` to the floor without catching its undefined enforcement mechanism).
    2. **Democratic Minority Leadership**:
       - **House Minority Leader: `Delegate Thomas "Tom" Toscano` (`D-Charlottesville`)**, **Democratic Minority Whip `Delegate Eileen Fillmore`**, and **Democratic Caucus Chair `Delegate Kenneth Plumley`**.
    3. **Delegate Katie Prairie (`D-Virginia Beach`, `85th District` — Second-Term Rank-and-File Opposition Member, Zero Leadership Office, & Three Standing Committees)**:
       - In 2016, thirty-year-old Katie holds **no leadership office**; her influence comes entirely from reading every bill line by line and being frighteningly good at her job.
       - **Katie's Three Standing Committee Assignments**:
         1. **House Committee on Transportation** (her signature assignment—she medically cannot drive, uses a `SwiftChair` power wheelchair, and rides the bus and train everywhere; whenever a colleague says *"People can always drive,"* Katie looks across the table and replies, *"No, they can't. I took the bus here."*).
         2. **House Committee on Health, Welfare and Institutions (`HWI`)** (her human-services, healthcare, Medicaid waiver, and disability policy portfolio).
         3. **House Committee on Education** (broadening her portfolio to public schools, students, teachers, and children).
       - **Crucially, Katie Is NOT on the House Committee on General Laws**: `HB 663` did not come through any of Katie's three committees—it came out of **General Laws** and landed on Thursday's Second Reading House Floor calendar.
- **The "Prairie-and-Triplet QA Test," Thirteen-Year-Old Sarah Hawkins Finding the Fatal Flaw, Majority Leader Richard Callaway Defending Katie's Question on the Floor, & *"Your Bill Killed Your Bill"* (`books/katie/e009/c003/p001.md` & `p002.md`)**:
  - **Pre-Session Discovery with Sam, Mandy, and Sarah Hawkins (`Thursday, Feb 4, 2016, 11:15 AM`)**: When 13-year-old eighth-grade House Page triplets **Sam, Mandy, and Sarah Hawkins** deliver the Thursday Second Reading calendar packet to `Room 518` and Katie hands each of them a copy of `HB 663`, **thirteen-year-old Sarah Hawkins** finds the fatal problem first—not as a political or constitutional argument, but as a frightened, literal thirteen-year-old autistic girl applying the proposed rule to herself (*I am a girl; the bill says I'm allowed into the girls' room because of my anatomy; how does the government know what anatomy I have?*). Pointing at Line 18, Sarah looks up at Katie and asks: ***"Delegate Prairie? ... Wait. Does somebody have to see my parts to prove I'm a girl?"*** Katie stops reading completely as Mandy looks over Sarah's shoulder (*"What?"*), Sarah explains (*"It says which bathroom I can use depends on my anatomy"*), and Sam asks (*"So how do they know?"*). Taking the bill back and reading Line 18 and Line 54 in silence, Katie says, *"That's a very good question."* As they walk through it together (Mandy: *"Maybe your birth certificate?"* -> Katie: *"The bill doesn't say that"*; Sam: *"A doctor?"* -> Katie: *"Doesn't say that either"*; Sarah: *"But they don't know"* -> Mandy: *"Everybody knows Sarah's a girl"* -> Sarah: *"But how?"* -> Sam: *"The bill says anatomy"* -> Katie: *"Exactly"*), the software-developer QA part of Katie's brain stress-tests the statute (`Input: accusation; Required statutory determination: anatomical sex; Evidence specified: none; Verification procedure: none; Authorized investigator: unspecified; Privacy protections for minors: unspecified`). First, Katie looks straight at thirteen-year-old Sarah to reassure her: ***"Sarah, nobody should ever have to see your private parts for you to use a bathroom."*** Relieved, Sarah says, *"Okay"*—and Katie looks back down at `HB 663`: ***"Which means the Gentleman from Spotsylvania has some explaining to do."***
  - **On the House Floor (`Thursday, Feb 4, 2016, 12:18 PM`)**:
    - When `HB 663` is called on the Second Reading calendar, thirty-year-old **Delegate Katie Prairie (`D-Virginia Beach`)** rises at her accessible desk and asks **Speaker William J. Harwell** if the Gentleman from Spotsylvania (**Delegate Martin Cobb**) will yield for a question.
    - As Katie methodically locks down that Line 18 defines compliance strictly by *"a person's anatomy"* rather than a driver's license or birth certificate, **Deputy Majority Leader Todd Gentry** leans across to **Majority Leader Richard Callaway** in alarm: *"Richard, Prairie's gutting the bill. Are you going to do something?"* Richard doesn't look up from the bill: *"Like what? ... She's asking him how his bill works. She's reading the bill. And Cobb seems to be doing an adequate job of gutting it himself."*
    - Holding up the printed text of `HB 663` from her `SwiftChair`, Katie translates Sarah's frightened question directly into legislative scrutiny: ***"Then if the gentleman says there will be no physical inspection, how does the Commonwealth establish that someone's anatomical sex does not match the designation of the facility? ... If I am accused of being in the wrong women's restroom, what am I supposed to show somebody to prove that I'm female? I return to the exact question a thirteen-year-old girl asked me twenty minutes ago when she read your bill: why on earth should I—or any girl or woman in the Commonwealth of Virginia—have to pull my pants down for a government official or a police officer to prove I have female parts just to use the ladies' room?!"***
    - At the words *"a thirteen-year-old girl asked me twenty minutes ago when she read your bill,"* **Majority Leader Richard Callaway** freezes for half a second and glances back over his shoulder at **thirteen-year-old Sarah Hawkins** standing at attention behind his desk; **Sarah looks right back at him and gives a single, solemn nod**, and Richard has his *oh, for heaven's sake—one of my thirteen-year-old Pages found the hole in this bill* realization.
    - As the gallery erupts and **Speaker Harwell** pounds his gavel (*"The House will come to order! The House will be in order!"*), chief co-patron **Delegate Robert Marsh** jumps up on a point of order claiming Katie's language is indecorous—and **Republican Majority Leader Richard Callaway** rises from his front-row center-aisle desk to address the Chair:
      > *"Mr. Speaker, the Gentlewoman's phrasing may be uncomfortable, but the Gentlewoman didn't introduce anatomy into this legislation. The bill did. The legislation before us expressly makes anatomical sex determinative. I believe she is entitled to ask the patron how the Commonwealth proposes to determine that fact."*
    - While Delegate Cobb stands frozen and beet-red for four full seconds stammering into his microphone, moderate Republicans across the chamber (including Virginia Beach **Delegate Ronald Villa** and Appropriations Chair **S. Christopher Jennings**) shake their heads in horror, and **Majority Whip Jackson Milligan** hurries up the aisle to whisper to Richard: *"Leader, we're bleeding votes."* / *"How badly?"* / *"Badly."* Richard snaps his binder shut—*"Pass it by"*—and moves that `HB 663` be passed by for the day.
    - **After the Floor Session Recesses (`1:00 PM` by the Clerk's Well — Birth of the Unofficial "Prairie-Triplets QA Department")**: When a furious Delegate Cobb confronts Majority Leader Richard Callaway and Speaker Harwell (while Sam, Mandy, and Sarah Hawkins stand at attention behind Richard's desk holding bill folders) and complains *"Prairie killed my bill!"*, Richard looks at him over his reading glasses: ***"No... Katie asked how your bill worked... Your bill killed your bill, Martin. And for your information, Sarah found this."*** When Cobb blinks at Sarah (*"Leader..."*), Richard says flatly: ***"Sarah is thirteen."*** / *"I understand that."* / ***"Apparently she understood the bill. You brought a statute onto the House calendar whose central operative concept collapses under the very first implementation question. Supporters will always tell you why your bill is wonderful; opponents will tell you where it breaks. You should've put this bill in front of Katie before it ever left committee... Of course she would've opposed it. That's why you should've put it in front of her. Give your bill to the smartest person you know who's determined to kill it. If it survives her, you might have something. Better yet, you should've given it to Katie and those three Pages who follow her around."*** When Speaker Harwell asks in disbelief *"Three thirteen-year-olds caught this?"*, Sarah nods solemnly (*"We caught it really fast, Mr. Speaker"*) as all three triplets try desperately not to laugh behind Richard's back (*"And don't you three start"*). As Cobb stalks off, **Speaker Harwell** mutters, *"Next time somebody brings us a bill about people's anatomy, Richard, have Prairie read it first,"* and Richard sighs: ***"Actually, Mr. Speaker, never bring a bill to this floor that failed Prairie—and have those three read it too."***
  - **The Five-Day Escalation From the House Floor to Sunday Morning's *Meet the Nation* in Washington (`Thursday–Monday, February 4–8, 2016`)**:
    - **Thursday (`Feb 4`)**: Captured on the House Chamber feed from Nate's ADA floor bay beside Delegate Ronald Villa (`R-Virginia Beach`, `21st District`) and captioned at `12:35 PM`, the 95-second floor exchange spreads across Virginia throughout Thursday afternoon, leads the `6:00 PM` **local television news in Richmond and Norfolk**, and crosses ten million views nationwide overnight.
    - **Friday (`Feb 5` — The Speakerphone Exclusive, Dual Autistic Meltdown, & Bipartisan Leadership Reassurance in `Room 518`)**: At `9:15 AM`, Katie answers Line One on speakerphone in `Room 518` (fifth floor) expecting a Kempsville constituent calling about CVTA buses, and politely grants a Sunday morning exclusive to **Arthur Vance** at ***Meet the Nation*** (which she and Nate remember watching with their dad David over breakfast, and which means she only has to explain the bill once before catching the `11:12 AM` train home). When Caucus Communications Director **Greg** panics in the outer room over ***Capitol Sunday***, ***This Sunday***, and ***Beltway Sunday*** holding on Lines 2–4 and yells into the phone that Katie broke booking rules without clearing it with leadership, Katie enters a full autistic meltdown and Nate enters a sympathetic feedback-loop meltdown—both crying and hugging tightly in their `SwiftChair` powerchairs terrified she will be expelled from the House of Delegates. Out of the corner of their eyes, Katie and Nate see **13-year-old eighth-grade House Page triplets Sam, Amanda ("Mandy"), and Sarah** (Sam is the fraternal triplet born first, followed by identical twin sisters Mandy and Sarah born second and third; all three are autistic, are each other's safe person, taught Katie and Nate the difference between Richmond's **"Southside Virginia"** along `US-58` near Danville and their home **"Southside Hampton Roads,"** regard **Katie as their favorite Delegate in the entire House** because she and Nate shielded them with their `SwiftChair` powerchairs during a noisy hallway meltdown in January, told them they are autistic too, never shout at them, and write every bill and room number clearly on a sticky note, and—inspired by Katie and Nate famously riding the bus and train everywhere—decided **public transportation is the #1 issue they want to work on as House Pages**) freeze in the fifth-floor doorway holding her yellow transportation folders and bolt down the hall together. Neither Katie nor Nate knows that Sam, Mandy, and Sarah are the blood nephew and nieces of the Republican **House Majority Leader** (`Uncle Richard`), who—along with his wife **`Aunt Marge`** (the triplets' favorite family members)—took legal custody of all three triplets after rescuing them about a year ago (`early 2015`) from the unspeakable horrors their mother and stepfather did to them. Sam, Mandy, and Sarah race downstairs together to tell `Uncle Richard` that their favorite Delegate from the other Southside—the Gentlewoman from Virginia Beach who uses a wheelchair—is crying on the fifth floor, and the **House Majority Leader** (who, along with Aunt Marge, genuinely likes Katie despite sitting across the aisle because of how kindly she treats Sam, Mandy, and Sarah every day) races back up with the triplets just as the Democratic **House Minority Leader** (`Tom`) arrives outside `Room 518`. Both floor leaders and all three triplets step in together (taking the meltdown personally regardless of party politics), Sam, Mandy, and Sarah hand Katie her sticky-note transportation dockets and quote **Page 42** in unison (*"A Delegate controls her own office communications at all times"*), and both leaders reassure Katie and Nate at eye level that all "unwritten rules" in Richmond are made up, explain the 10M+ viral views and how monumental a solo `9:00 AM` A-Block exclusive on *Meet the Nation* is, and then step into the fifth-floor corridor to dress Greg down.
    - **Friday Evening (`Feb 5`, `5:15 PM–7:45 PM` — Riding `JRTC Route 6` to Dinner at Oak Lawn with Uncle Richard, Aunt Marge, & the Triplets + Weekend JRTC Homework)**: After the Friday afternoon House session gavels out (with no evening Page study hall on Friday nights), **Sam, Mandy, and Sarah** beg their **Uncle Richard** and **Aunt Marge** to go for a real bus ride aboard **JRTC** with **Delegate Katie Prairie** and **Nate** to see firsthand how they get around Richmond. Under the supervision of Uncle Richard (as House Majority Leader), Aunt Marge, Katie, and Nate, all seven board the westbound **`JRTC Route 6` (*Oak Lawn*)** low-floor `Stalwart` bus at **Ninth and Broad**, watching the hydraulic ramp, four-point wheelchair securement bays, 30-day magnetic passes, and the onboard GPS cross-street annunciator as they ride six miles up West Broad Street (past Central Virginia University, the Science Museum, and Katie and Nate's apartment building at the Broad & Boulevard Lofts) to the **Oak Lawn Diner** for a family supper. Afterward, they ride the eastbound **`JRTC Route 6` (*Downtown*)** bus back to **Ninth and Broad** in Downtown Richmond, where Katie and Nate hand Sam, Mandy, and Sarah official JRTC system maps and pocket timetables with a weekend **homework assignment** to learn everything they can about JRTC (studying why `Route 6` works with 10-minute headways and identifying service gaps like early evening cutoffs and zero Sunday bus service to the suburban Staples Mill train station) before Katie and Nate catch the westbound `Route 6` bus back up to the Broad & Boulevard Lofts for the night.
    - **The Virginia Senate Page Program (`Ages 13–14`), the United States Senate Page Program (`Ages 16–17`), & Sam, Mandy, and Sarah's Full Career Arc in Katie's State Senate (`2020–2026`) and U.S. Senate (`Dirksen Room 511`, `2027+`) Offices**:

On the Virginia Senate floor (`2020–2026`), Katie and Nate immediately become the favorite Senator and brother of the eighth-grade Senate Pages because every floor errand from Katie's desk comes on a color-coded sticky note with the exact room number, committee name, and bill number in neat block print, and nobody in Katie's orbit ever yells.
        2. **United States Senate Page Program (`Ages 16–17`, High School Juniors)**: Unlike the U.S. House of Representatives (which abolished its Page program in 2011), the **United States Senate** maintains an active **U.S. Senate Page Program** for 16- and 17-year-old high school juniors (`30 Pages per semester`) who wear navy-blue suits on the Senate floor, live at the **Daniel Webster Senate Page Residence** on Capitol Hill, and attend early-morning classes at the **U.S. Senate Page School**.

During Katie's **May 2026 U.S. Senate Primary Tour** (`books/katie/e010/c003/p003.md`), when Katie, Nate, David, and the Room 214 trio ride **Bluewater Transit's *Emerald Line*** down `US-58` into Martinsville and transfer onto **Piedmont Regional Transit (`PRT`)** into **Danville**, 24-year-old Sam, Mandy, and Sarah—along with **Aunt Marge** and **Uncle Richard** (a 24-year House veteran, retired **Republican House Majority Leader**, and conservative elder statesman in the mold of John Warner / John McCain)—meet the bus in Danville. Right in front of the Roanoke, Lynchburg, and Danville television cameras, Uncle Richard peels off a royal-blue *Katie Prairie for U.S. Senate* sticker, puts it on his lapel, and crosses party lines to endorse Katie—telling every conservative voter along `US-58` how Katie and Nate shielded Sam, Mandy, and Sarah with their wheelchairs when they were overwhelmed 13-year-old eighth-grade Pages, rode `JRTC Route 6` with their family to supper, hired all three triplets together in her State Senate office so they would never be separated, and got the Martinsville-to-Danville bus link funded from the triplets' eighth-grade Page Mock Session bill. When a reporter asks if he is leaving the Republican Party, Richard replies **"No"**—and explains his institutionalist elder-statesman philosophy (*"The people of Danville didn't send me to Richmond to serve a President, and they didn't send me there to serve the Republican Party. They sent me there to serve them—to solve problems... Twenty-four years in Richmond teaches you that ideological speeches don't fill potholes... Katie and I disagreed plenty—if she tells you otherwise, don't believe her—but I never once doubted that she came to Richmond for the exact same reason I did: to do the work... I've supported Republican Presidents, I've opposed Democratic Presidents, and I've probably been angry at every Governor Virginia has had at least once. That's politics. Nobody elected me to swear personal allegiance to another politician"*), how first the **`Liberty Tax Movement`** (*in-universe Tea Party*) with its purity tests and then **`Restore the Republic (RTR)`** (*in-universe MAGA*) inverted his old party standard (`Party -> principles -> candidates`) into a personal fealty cult (`Thorne -> loyalty -> principles`), how **prior to 2016 he had never once voted for a Democratic candidate in his entire life, and then evolved through three distinct steps**: (a) voting for the Democrat for President in **`2016`, `2020`, and `2024`** when Garrett Thorne's own name was on the ballot (telling himself at first that it was just one man at the top of the ticket, before the **January 6th** attack on the U.S. Capitol became his breaking point), (b) crossing the Rubicon in the **`November 2025` Virginia Governor's Race**—the first vote that truly meant something structurally for a former Virginia House Majority Leader because **President Thorne wasn't on the ballot, yet the Republican candidate for Governor aligned themselves with Thorne and `RTR`** when Richard firmly believes **the Governor of Virginia should answer to the People of the Commonwealth of Virginia, not to President Garrett Thorne**, forcing Richard to vote for a Democratic candidate for Governor of Virginia to send the message that aligning with Thorne costs you conservative Virginia votes—and (c) **`2026`**, where he steps out from the privacy of the voting booth to publicly endorse and vote wholeheartedly **for** a Democrat in Katie Prairie: ***"I didn't leave the Republican Party. The Republican Party left me... When they call Katie Prairie an enemy of the Republic, I ask them one simple question: 'Have you ever actually worked with her? No? I have.' ... Before 2016, I had never voted for a Democratic candidate in my entire life. In 2016, 2020, and 2024, I voted against Garrett Thorne himself. In 2025, I voted against a Republican candidate for Governor to send a message that aligning yourself with Garrett Thorne costs you Virginia—because the Governor should answer to the People of the Commonwealth of Virginia, not to President Thorne! And this year is different again—because this year, I am not just voting against Thorne or his movement in the privacy of a voting booth. I am standing here in broad daylight voting wholeheartedly FOR Katie Prairie. I'm still a Republican. Katie's still a Democrat. I haven't changed parties. I've simply reached an age where I don't need anybody's permission to recognize a good public servant."*** Watching rural Southside voter pledges vertical-spike on Nate's laptop on the evening bus back to Bluewater, Sam, Mandy, and Sarah coin the phrase ***"the Uncle Richard Effect"***—which GNN Election Night data correspondent **Stephen Kovacs** highlights on the **GNN Big Board** on **November 3, 2026** (`books/katie/e011/c001/p001.md`) when Katie wins the Independent Cities of Danville and Martinsville by 22 points and shaves nearly 11 points off Republican Richard Sterling's rural margins across Pittsylvania and Henry Counties.
