Go Call Your Senator."*, Unloading on `RTR` Over `"Broken Katie Prairie"`, Uncle Richard at the Swearing-In, Endorsing Katie's `Guilty` & `Aye` Impeachment Votes, Cleaning Up the GOP, & Katie and Nate at Uncle Richard's Funeral)**:
           - **Building Her Class II Personal Office From Scratch (`books/katie/e011/c001/p003.md`)**: Modeled on Senior **Senator Thomas Keane's** (*Tim Kaine*) two-part operation (a Washington legislative, administrative, scheduling, and communications staff in `Dirksen Room 511` plus a Virginia constituent-services operation with caseworkers and geographically assigned regional directors, including a Southside and Eastern Shore Regional Director), Katie tells her transition team on Day One: ***"Sam, Mandy, and Sarah are coming with me."***
           - **Inseparable as People, Not Interchangeable as Professionals (`Interdependence Without Infantilizing`)**: Because Katie and Nate are autistic themselves, they design the triplets' jobs around adult interdependence rather than forced separation. All three are based together in **Dirksen Room 511** with workstations near one another, overlapping schedules, commuting together on Capital Transit, and eating lunch together whenever they want—with zero supervisors arbitrarily splitting them across cities to "build independence"—while each holds a real, distinct professional U.S. Senate position and salary suited to their individual talents:
             1. **Sam Hawkins (`Age 24`) — `Legislative Correspondent (Transportation, Infrastructure, Disability/Accessibility Policy, & Rural Development)`** (`$62,000/yr`): The policy gremlin who happily reads a 312-page FTA authorization bill at `11:00 PM`, coming full circle from carrying paper bills as a 13-year-old Virginia House Page in 2016 to drafting federal transit legislation for a U.S. Senator.
             2. **Mandy (`Amanda`) Hawkins (`Age 24`) — `Constituent Services Representative / Federal Caseworker`** (`$66,000/yr`): Who loves human-facing constituent casework helping Virginians stuck in federal bureaucratic systems (`VA`, `Social Security`, `Medicare` wheelchair authorizations), thriving on Katie's predictable six-step workflow (`Case Received` $\rightarrow$ `Privacy Act Release` $\rightarrow$ `Agency Liaison Contact` $\rightarrow$ `Scheduled Follow-Up` $\rightarrow$ `Constituent Update` $\rightarrow$ `Resolution & Closure`).
             3. **Sarah Hawkins (`Age 24`) — `Staff Assistant & Digital Accessibility Correspondent`** (`$58,000/yr` entry track growing into Digital Communications & Legislative Correspondence): Managing front-office phones, Capitol tour requests, visitor greeting, and mail triage while applying her sharp talent for digital communications and Section 508 / WCAG accessible graphics alongside Nate.
           - **Uncle Richard's Blessing, The Virginia Press Story (`Hawkins` vs. `Callaway`), & Unloading on `RTR` When They Attack His Kids and Call Katie `"Broken Katie Prairie"` (`books/katie/e011/c001/p003.md`)**: When the 24-year-old triplets come home to Danville and tell Uncle Richard and Aunt Marge Callaway that Katie offered all three of them jobs in Washington, saying they wanted to talk to him and Aunt Marge first, Richard stares at them and says: ***"You're twenty-four years old. Go call your senator."*** Because Richard Callaway is their maternal uncle (their mother's brother) and the triplets carry the surname **`Hawkins`**, Washington reporters scanning Katie's initial transition payroll filings don't immediately realize who `Sam Hawkins`, `Amanda Hawkins`, and `Sarah Hawkins` are related to until a veteran Richmond Capitol reporter cross-references them (`FORMER VIRGINIA GOP MAJORITY LEADER RICHARD CALLAWAY ENCOURAGES NEPHEW AND NIECES TO JOIN DEMOCRATIC SENATOR'S WASHINGTON STAFF`). When reporters ask if it bothers him that his kids work for a Democrat, Richard replies: ***"Which Democrat? ... Katie? Lord, no... I couldn't tell you another Senator in America I'd trust more with those three."*** And when a `Liberty First Network` correspondent repeats `RTR` attacks calling him *"RINO Richard"* and *"an Enemy of the Republic"* who is *"sending his autistic nephew and nieces to Washington after they were manipulated by Broken Katie Prairie,"* Richard first laughs dryly at `RINO` (**"Twenty-four years in the Virginia House of Delegates. Republican Majority Leader. And now I'm apparently a Republican In Name Only. That's an awfully long time to maintain the disguise"**) and then unloads when they attack his kids and Katie: ***"Sending them? Let me get something straight right now. My nephew and nieces are twenty-four years old. Katie Prairie offered them jobs in the United States Senate. They wanted those jobs. They asked Marge and me what we thought, and I told them to take the jobs. Nobody stole my kids. Nobody brainwashed my kids. Nobody sent my kids anywhere. And as for this 'Broken Katie Prairie' garbage? ... When my nephew and nieces were thirteen years old, that woman recognized when they were overwhelmed before half the adults in the Capitol did. She gave them instructions they could understand. She protected them when the House got too loud. She treated them with patience and dignity, and she never once screamed at my kids. If that's what Restore the Republic calls broken, then perhaps somebody ought to take a long look at what Restore the Republic calls whole. And if you think autism makes Katie Prairie broken, you're also talking about my nephew and my nieces—so choose your next words very carefully. You people didn't inherit my Republican Party merely because you inherited its name."*** That night at home with Marge, Richard sighs: ***"Twenty-four years as a Republican in the House of Delegates. Republican Majority Leader. And somehow all three of my kids wound up working for the Democratic Senator from Virginia Beach... I suppose this is my fault."***
           - **January 3, 2027 Swearing-In Open House in `Dirksen Room 511` (`books/katie/e011/c003/p002.md`)**: When Uncle Richard and Aunt Marge come to Washington for Katie's swearing-in and watch Katie hand 24-year-old Sam, Mandy, and Sarah their Senate onboarding folders beside their three badges in `Dirksen Room 511`—eleven years after Richard nervously sent three frightened 13-year-old rescued kids into the Virginia Capitol as House Pages—Richard asks softly, ***"All three?"*** and Katie looks mildly offended: ***"Richard. Of course all three."***
           - **April 16, 2027 — Endorsing Katie's `Guilty` (for Conviction) and `Aye` (for Barring From Office) Impeachment Votes, Cleaning Up the Republican Party Once President Clara Morales Is Sworn In, Returning to Voting Republican (`Except for Katie in 2032`), & Why Nate and Especially Katie Take Uncle Richard's Passing So Hard (`Like a Second Father`, `books/katie/e011/c004/p003.md`)**: When Senator Katie Prairie votes **`Guilty`** to convict and remove **SecDef Bret Halverson**, **VPOTUS Conrad Drayton**, and **POTUS Garrett Thorne**, and votes **`Aye`** to permanently bar all three men from ever holding federal office again, **Uncle Richard** goes on live Virginia television from his front porch in Danville to wholeheartedly endorse both her **`Guilty`** (conviction) and **`Aye`** (disqualification) votes. Once the new President (**President Clara Morales**) is sworn in, Richard works tirelessly across Virginia to clean up the Republican Party and bring it back to the honorable, problem-solving party he remembered when he served in the House of Delegates. With Thorne barred for life and the party reforming, **Richard goes back to voting Republican down the line—except for the one more time Katie Prairie runs for United States Senator during his lifetime (`November 2032`, her second Senate term, after Katie and Nate's father David Prairie has passed away)**. Uncle Richard passes away peacefully in Southside Virginia before Katie runs for her third Senate term (`2038`). **Nate—and especially Katie—take Richard's passing very hard**: especially after losing their own dad David before 2032, **Uncle Richard was like a second father to them, even though they disagreed politically**—the man who comforted them at eye level during their February 2016 `Room 518` meltdown, rode `JRTC Route 6` to supper with them on Broad Street, entrusted Sam, Mandy, and Sarah to their care, and defended them fiercely whenever anyone called Katie *"broken."* When **Katie and Nate** travel to Danville in their wheelchairs to attend Uncle Richard's funeral alongside **Aunt Marge** and **Sam, Mandy, and Sarah**, **Katie attends strictly in her personal capacity as Richard's friend—not in her official capacity as a United States Senator** (no Senate lapel pin, no press advisory, and declining the VIP dignitaries' section so she and Nate can sit and grieve privately beside Aunt Marge and the triplets as family and friends).

Fenwick Bridge** on the **Capital Transit Yellow Line** (passing the stalled `I-395` 14th Street Bridge traffic jam) into **`L'Enfant Plaza`** (`8:12 AM`), and ride the street elevator up to **7th Street SW** to board the northbound low-floor **`Capital Transit Route P6` bus** (receiving the automatic free rail-to-bus transfer discount on their `CapitalPass` cards) across the **National Mall** and along **Constitution Avenue** and **Louisiana Avenue** right to the **GBS News Washington Bureau at `400 North Capitol Street NW`** (`8:32 AM`—avoiding the cavernous Washington Union Station concourse). At `9:00 AM`, **Delegate Katie Prairie (`D-Virginia Beach`)** appears live one-on-one with **David Vance** in front of the curved Virginia State Capitol LED backdrop (with the camera framed wide to show her `SwiftChair` while Nate monitors off-camera by the confidence monitor), then takes the southbound **`Route P6` bus** back across the National Mall to **`L'Enfant Plaza`**, rides the southbound **Yellow Line** across the **Charles R. Fenwick Bridge** to **Alexandria**, and catches the **`11:12 AM` ARTS Train 143** (`ALX` $\rightarrow$ `RVR`) and lift-equipped **`ARTS Thruway Bus 8443`** (`RVR` $\rightarrow$ `RVM`) so she is back at the Broad & Boulevard Lofts in sweatpants by `2:05 PM`.
  - **How the Bill Dies**: Humiliated by the national media firestorm Katie's speech ignited—and terrified of going on the record in a roll-call vote—the **Republican-controlled House General Laws Subcommittee** kills their own party's bathroom bill via an **unrecorded voice vote to "Lay the Bill on the Table" (table it)**, sending the bill's furious patron stomping out of the room (*"I'm going to pray that you all get courage"*) while cementing Katie's reputation as a fearless statewide civil-rights fighter.
- **Katie's Stance**: Unapologetically pro-transgender rights: **transgender women are women**, transgender men are men, and every citizen deserves full civil rights, bodily autonomy, privacy, and equal protection under the law without state-sponsored bullying or bathroom policing.
- **GOP Rebuttal**: *"Katie Prairie became a darling of the national liberal media by throwing an outburst in Richmond to block common-sense privacy laws because she wants biological men in women's sports and men in girls' locker rooms."*
- **Katie's Counter-Punch**: *"When politicians write a bill ordering law enforcement to police 'anatomical sex' at the door of every public ladies' room in Virginia for a fifty-dollar fine, they shouldn't hide under their desks when a woman asks how they plan to enforce it. Transgender women are women, and no woman in Virginia should ever have to pull her pants down to prove she has female parts just to use the bathroom."*

#### 2. Universal Healthcare, Eliminating Medicaid Asset Caps, & Wheelchair Right-to-Repair
- **Real-World Issue**: Employer-tied health insurance networks routinely deny prior authorizations for power wheelchairs and custom parts, while archaic Medicaid/SSI asset and income cliffs strip disabled Americans of coverage if they save money, get promoted, or marry.
- **Katie's Stance**: Champions **Universal Healthcare** (cradle-to-grave medical, prescription, mental health, and long-term home-care coverage with zero prior-authorization games), abolishing Medicaid/SSI work and marriage penalties, ending Developmental Disability (DD) waiver waitlists (protecting adults like her cousin **Chelsea**), and federal **Assistive Technology Right-to-Repair** laws so wheelchair users can get their `SwiftChair` repaired immediately.
- **GOP Rebuttal**: *"Katie Prairie wants a socialist government takeover of healthcare that will outlaw private insurance for 160 million Americans, bankrupt the Treasury, ration care, and strip intellectual-property rights from medical device makers."*

#### 3. Border Security, the 1951 Geneva Refugee Convention, & Dreamers (`DACA`)
- **Real-World Issue**: Multi-year federal immigration court backlogs, humanitarian crises at the southern border, and legal limbo for DACA recipients ("Dreamers") and families fleeing cartel/political violence.
- **Katie's Stance**: Supports secure borders, modern port-of-entry technology to stop fentanyl trafficking, and surging immigration judges and asylum officers to streamline legal processing. Upholds black-letter treaty and statutory law: under the **1951 Geneva Refugee Convention** (and the **1967 Protocol** ratified by the U.S. Senate and codified into `8 U.S.C. § 1158`), **asylum seekers who cross the border and present themselves to authorities to request asylum are acting 100% legally**. Champions a clear statutory pathway to U.S. citizenship for **Dreamers (`DACA`)** and TPS holders fleeing violence in their home countries.
- **GOP Rebuttal**: *"Katie Prairie supports wide-open borders and mass amnesty for illegal border crossers, hiding behind 'United Nations treaties' to reward lawbreakers."*

#### 4. Late-Evening Local Bus Service (`CVTA Route #33` & `Route #12`) & Continuous ADA Sidewalks
- **Real-World Issue**: In Virginia Beach, the `6:00 PM` Municipal Center bus curfew forced riders needing `Route #12` at CCHR (`last bus at 5:48 PM`) to leave City Hall at `5:30 PM`—twelve minutes before `6:00 PM` City Council meetings even began—while arterial roads like Military Highway (`US-13`) lacked continuous curb-cut sidewalks.
- **Katie's Stance**: As her very first legislative fight on the Virginia Beach City Council, she championed late-evening CVTA bus pulses on Route #33 and the CCHR evening connecting routes (`#12`, `#25`, `#29`, `#36`)—working across the 11-member Council to build a coalition and win a majority Council vote so working-class and disabled citizens can attend `6:00 PM` civic meetings and still get home—and fought for continuous ADA sidewalks and concrete bus pads on every road project.
- **GOP Rebuttal**: *"Prairie wastes property-tax dollars running 'empty ghost buses' at night and holds road-widening projects hostage to a radical 'War on Cars.'"*

#### 5. Substance-Abuse Recovery vs. Personal Sobriety & Lethal Medication Contraindication
- **Real-World Issue**: The opioid and alcohol addiction crisis across Virginia, combined with Capitol backroom culture where lobbyists ply lawmakers with alcohol and cigars.
- **Katie's Stance**: Having vowed lifelong sobriety with Nate on January 1, 2003 after witnessing their mother's alcoholism—and taking a daily maintenance prescription medication (alongside Nate and their dad David) where mixing with alcohol or tobacco has a **known side effect of death**—Katie and Nate never drink alcohol or coffee (drinking milk, juice, soda/ginger ale, chocolate milk, hot chocolate, flavored water, or sports drinks, while David drinks black coffee), refuse all alcohol/tobacco PAC money, and champion trauma-informed addiction treatment and recovery programs.
- **GOP Rebuttal**: *"Katie Prairie is a self-righteous nanny-state prohibitionist who looks down on regular beer-drinking Virginians and wants to regulate Virginia's craft breweries, wineries, and Southside tobacco farmers out of business."*

#### 6. Abortion & Reproductive Freedom
- **Real-World Issue**: Post-*Dobbs* legislative battles over abortion bans and reproductive healthcare access in Virginia and nationwide.
- **Katie's Stance**: Abortion is a deeply personal medical decision strictly between a woman and her doctor—and is probably one of the hardest decisions a woman ever has to make. Politicians and prosecutors have no business inserting themselves into an exam room or criminalizing patients and physicians.
- **GOP Rebuttal**: *"Katie Prairie supports radical, taxpayer-funded late-term abortion on demand up to the moment of birth with zero limits or parental notification."*
- **Katie's Counter-Punch**: *"Nobody wakes up hoping to face the most agonizing medical decision of their life. When a woman and her family are facing a heartbreaking medical reality, the last person who belongs in that hospital room is a politician."*

#### 7. Solemnly Affirming Her Oath on the Constitution Without *"So Help Me God"* (`Article VI, Clause 3` & Debunking the 1789 Washington Myth)
- **Real-World Issue**: Christian nationalist pressure on lawmakers to swear on a Christian Bible and invoke a deity, versus Katie and Nate's secular convictions (believing in zero religion and zero god) and Katie's lifelong practice at every swearing-in (`City Council`, `House of Delegates`, `State Senate`, and `U.S. Senate`).
- **Katie's Stance**: Always places her left hand directly on the **Constitution of the Commonwealth of Virginia** or the **Constitution of the United States of America** (held by Nate), says **`"I do solemnly affirm"`**, and omits *"So help me God"* under **Article VI, Clause 3 of the U.S. Constitution** (the No Religious Test Clause), **Article II, Section 7 of the Constitution of Virginia** (`Code of Virginia § 49-9`), and **5 U.S.C. § 3331 / 1 U.S.C. § 1**.
- **GOP & Evangelical Media Meltdown (`Beacon News`, `LibertyMax`, & Virginia Beach's *The 777 Circle* on the `Covenant Broadcasting Network`)**: *"Katie Prairie refused to put her hand on the Holy Bible and refused to say 'So help me God' at her swearing-in, defying the sacred precedent set by George Washington in 1789!"*
- **Katie's Counter-Punch**: *"Have the commentators on Beacon News, LibertyMax, and The 777 Circle down on Indian River Road actually read the Constitution? Article VI, Clause 3 states that all Senators and state legislators shall be bound by Oath or Affirmation, and that no religious test shall ever be required as a qualification to any office under the United States. Even the Presidential Oath in Article II, Section 1, Clause 8 doesn't contain the words 'So help me God'—and historians debunked the myth about George Washington decades ago: zero eyewitnesses at Federal Hall in 1789 recorded Washington saying it; a writer named Rufus Wilmot Griswold invented that story sixty-five years later in an 1854 book, and Chester A. Arthur in 1881 was the first President confirmed to have said it. I wasn't elected to be anybody's pastor—I affirmed my oath on the Constitution because the Constitution is the supreme law of the land."*

#### 8. Kempsville 50–50 Homeownership, Her Father's Sunset Drive House, & The `ARTS` Commute (`NFK` $\rightarrow$ `ALX` Mobile Lift)
- **Real-World Issue**: Maintaining true Hampton Roads roots and legal residency in Virginia Beach while conducting U.S. Senate business in Washington, D.C., navigating autistic sensory needs, and working alongside Nate and their dad in Bedroom 4 of David's accessible two-story brick house on Sunset Drive just across the King Street underpass from `ALX` and `King St–Old Town` Metro.

When the Senate is in session, she buys regular passenger **ARTS Business Class** tickets from **Norfolk (`NFK`)** for the complimentary Café Car ginger ale and juice (plus black coffee when her dad David rides with them), detrains at **Alexandria (`ALX`)** via the portable **`ARTS Mobile Lift`** to avoid the sensory overload of Washington Union Station (`WAS`), sleeps at her father David's 100% paid-off Sunset Drive house (which David owned prior to her 2010 City Council election), and takes the Yellow-to-Blue Line Metro shortcut (`King St–Old Town` $\rightarrow$ `L'Enfant Plaza` $\rightarrow$ `Capitol South`) for day trips into D.C. to conduct the Senate's business—ensuring she sleeps in the Commonwealth of Virginia every single night.

Swamp."*

When I have to be up north for Senate votes, I don't use taxpayer allowances or corporate PAC checks to rent a luxury D.C. penthouse—I ride the train up from Norfolk, sleep under my dad's roof on the Virginia side of the Potomac in the house he paid off before I ever ran for City Council, and take the Metro into the Capitol for day trips. Wherever I lay my head at night—in my own home in Kempsville or my dad's house in Alexandria—I wake up in the Commonwealth of Virginia."*

#### 9. The Second Amendment & Common-Sense Gun Safety Reform
- **Real-World Issue**: Mass shootings, school shootings, and daily gun violence devastating communities across Virginia (from the 2007 college shooting in Blacksburg to the 2019 Virginia Beach Municipal Center shooting inside Building 2—the very municipal complex where Katie served on the City Council from `2011–2014`).
- **Katie's Stance**: Respects lawful gun ownership under the Second Amendment, is exhausted by senseless gun violence and school shootings, and champions common-sense gun safety laws—including universal background checks, extreme-risk protection orders ("red flag" laws), safe storage requirements to keep firearms out of the hands of children and schools, and closing gun-show and private-sale loopholes.

#### 10. The Three November 2026 Virginia Constitutional Amendments (`Vote YES on Questions 1, 2, & 3`)
- **Real-World & In-Universe Ballot Context (`November 3, 2026` Election)**:
  - Having co-patroned and voted twice across the `2025` and `2026` sessions of the Senate of Virginia to put all three legislatively referred constitutional amendments on the statewide ballot, State Senator and U.S. Senate nominee Katie Prairie campaigns across Virginia urging a **`YES` vote on all three questions**:
    1. **Question 1 (*Right to Reproductive Freedom Amendment*)**: Enshrines an explicit fundamental right to personal reproductive freedom (including prenatal care, childbirth, contraception, fertility treatments/IVF, miscarriage management, and abortion care) directly into the Constitution of Virginia.
    2. **Question 2 (*Marriage Equality Amendment*)**: Repeals the defunct 2006 ban on same-sex marriage from the Constitution of Virginia and replaces it with affirmative constitutional protection for the right to marry for consenting adults regardless of sex, gender, or race.
    3. **Question 3 (*Restoration of Voting Rights Amendment*)**: Replaces Virginia's Jim Crow-era system requiring case-by-case gubernatorial action, automatically restoring the right to vote to citizens with past felony convictions upon completion of their prison term.
  - **Canonical Story Outcome (`books/katie` Record)**: On Election Night (**Tuesday, November 3, 2026**), alongside Katie's U.S. Senate victory, **all three Virginia constitutional amendments pass statewide with `YES` votes**.
- **GOP Rebuttal**: *"Katie Prairie's three radical constitutional amendments lock unrestricted late-term abortion into Virginia's founding document, strip away religious liberties on marriage, and hand automatic voting rights to violent felons the second they walk out of prison without paying back their restitution."*
- **Katie's Counter-Punch**: *"Swearing my oath on the Constitution of Virginia means making sure that Constitution protects every citizen equally—protecting a woman's right to make her own healthcare decisions with her doctor, ensuring every loving couple's marriage is permanently recognized, and guaranteeing that once a Virginian has served their prison sentence, no politician can play gatekeeper with their right to vote."*

#### 11. The Local 1% Sales Tax Referendum to Fund Public School Construction & Modernization (`Voted YES` — *Canonical Outcome: `NO` Vote Prevailed*)
- **Real-World & In-Universe Ballot Context (`November 3, 2026` Local Referendum)**:
  - Following 2026 Virginia General Assembly legislation (supported by State Senator Katie Prairie in Richmond) granting all Virginia cities and counties the authority to hold a local referendum for a **1% local retail sales tax dedicated strictly to public school construction and major facility modernization** (exempting groceries and essential hygiene products), voters face the local ballot question on **November 3, 2026**.
  - **Katie's Stance (`Votes & Campaigns YES`)**: Remembering the aging infrastructure and accessibility gaps at Northwood High School when her and Nate's legs gave out in September 2001, Katie publicly endorses and casts a **`YES` vote** on the referendum—arguing that a dedicated 1% sales tax (shared by out-of-town tourists and shoppers rather than falling solely on local homeowners' real-estate taxes) is vital to replace crumbling roofs, failing HVAC systems, and inaccessible multi-story school buildings without working elevators.
  - **Canonical Story Outcome (`books/katie` Record)**: Despite Katie winning her U.S. Senate race and all three state constitutional amendments passing on **November 3, 2026**, voter fatigue over inflation and cost-of-living pressures causes **the local 1% school sales tax referendum to fail, with the `NO` vote succeeding**—giving Senator-Elect Katie Prairie an immediate, realistic motivation heading to Washington in January 2027 to fight for **federal school infrastructure and IDEA/ADA modernization grants** so local kids and teachers aren't left behind in aging classrooms.
- **GOP Rebuttal**: *"Even while Virginia families are crushed by high grocery and utility bills, tax-and-spend Katie Prairie pushed to hike your local sales tax by another 1% on everything you buy—and voters rightly rejected her tax hike at the ballot box."*
- **Katie's Reaction on November 4, 2026**: *"I respect the voters' decision at the ballot box—working families are stretched thin, and nobody likes paying higher sales tax. But the roofs on our public schools still leak, and disabled students still face broken elevators. If local taxpayers can't shoulder that burden through a sales tax, then my job in the United States Senate is to bring federal infrastructure dollars home to Virginia so our kids have safe, modern, accessible schools without squeezing local families."*

- **Real-World Issue**: Intrusive media and partisan scrutiny over why a 41-year-old female U.S. Senator is unmarried and lives with her twin brother Nate, paired with federal SSI/Medicaid rules that strip disabled adults of their healthcare, wheelchairs, and income if they get married.
- **Katie's Stance**: Following her 2005 escape from Brandon Mercer in Williamsburg and helping Nate break free from Tiffany Sloan at `233 W. Ocean View Ave` in Norfolk, Katie chose her lifelong twin sanctuary with Nate over ever letting a controlling partner into her life again. Even though Katie, Nate, and their father David have never received a dime of SSI, SNAP, or Medicaid, Katie is the Senate's fiercest champion for repealing the federal **SSI and Medicaid Disability Marriage Penalty**.
- **GOP Rebuttal**:

Whether I ever get married is nobody's business but mine. #### 13. The Official U.S. Senate Website Transition Audit (`Minified .zip Trick`, `Barely Commented Raw Code`, `Quantum Code`, `MagnaBook Pro`, `MagnaVoice`, `WCAG`, & `Section 508` Compliance) & The Build Team's Reaction
- **Real-World Institutional Context (`Friday, December 11, 2026 Senate Transition`)**:
  - Under **U.S. Senate Internet Services Rules**, a Senator's official government constituent website must be strictly separated from their campaign website (which Katie and Nate coded themselves in `PHP`) and hosted inside the **U.S. Senate Sergeant at Arms (`SAA`)** environment using an approved Senate vendor.
  - Under **Section 508 of the Rehabilitation Act** (`29 U.S.C. § 794d`) and **WCAG (Web Content Accessibility Guidelines)**, federal websites are legally required to be accessible to blind, low-vision, deaf, and motor-impaired users.
- **Why Katie & Nate Can Read Production Source Code Better Than the Build Team (`1997–2026+`)**:
  1. **`1997` (7th Grade at Morrison Middle School, Age 12)**: Nate and Katie learned **`HTML`** in 7th grade—and seeing his twelve-year-old twins hand-coding web pages in 1997 is what inspired their father **David Prairie** to go back to school at **`CCHR`** (*Community College of Hampton Roads*) to better himself (graduating in Summer 2003).
  2. **`2001` (Age 16, Year of the *Sunward Tropics* Cruise)**: They learned **`Quantum Basic 6.0`** alongside David using his mylar-wrapped Virginia Beach Public Library textbooks.
  3. **`March 4–16, 2007` (Age 21 at `Roosevelt Rehabilitation Center [RRC]` at `243 Roosevelt Avenue, Fishersville, VA`)**: They copied the **`PHP`** and **`SQL`** curriculum onto their laptops during their 12 days in the **`Web Applications Developer` course** at **`RRC`** and mastered server-side architecture alongside **Tina**, **Sheila**, and **Jim**.
  4. **`March 2007 – 2026+`**: After returning to their Kempsville house at `737 Arvin Rd` in March 2007 (and purchasing their second Kempsville home at `741 Lord Nelson Dr` 50–50 in **October 2019** when `CVTA Route 24` launched), they spent nineteen-plus years building and maintaining a network of heavily commented, WCAG-accessible production websites and desktop applications (`Quantum Studio 2026`, `Quantum Code`, `PHP`, `HTML5`, `CSS3`, `JavaScript`).
- **The Senate Website Build Team's Four-Stage Reaction When Senator Prairie Demands the Raw Unminified HTML**:
  1. **Stage 1 — Smug Beltway Condescension & The Minified `.zip` Trick (*"Just Send Her the Minified Bundle—She Won't Know the Difference"*)**:
     - The beltway digital contractor (charging the Senate Sergeant at Arms six figures per freshman website to slap a new banner onto a recycled boilerplate template) walks into the Dirksen basement transition suite expecting a twenty-minute rubber-stamp meeting with a non-technical politician.
     - Seeing Katie and Nate in their compact `SwiftChair` power wheelchairs, the vendor's Senior Account Director talks down to Katie in an overly bright, patronizing consultant voice while tapping a static PDF slideshow on his **`MagnaPad`** (*real-life iPad*): *"You don't need to worry about the technical weeds, Senator-Elect—my team handles all the magic behind the curtain! Just tell us if you like the shade of navy blue!"*
     - When Katie refuses to approve static pictures and asks for the source code, the build team back in Rosslyn tries to brush her off by sending a `.zip` archive (`prairie-senate-prod-bundle.zip`) containing **minified production files** (`app.min.js`, `styles.min.css`, and single-line whitespace-stripped HTML templates with every newline removed), assuming a newly elected U.S. Senator won't know what minified code is.
  2. **Stage 2 — Utter Confusion on Speakerphone (*"Wait... Why Is a U.S. Senator-Elect Speaking Our Exact Technical Engineering Language?"*)**:
     - With the vendor's lead engineers dialed in on the triangular conference speakerphone, Nate extracts the `.zip` archive on his **`MagnaBook Pro`** (*real-life MacBook Pro*), and Katie opens it in **`Quantum Code` (`QCode`** — *real-life VS Code*) on **`MagnaOS`** (*real-life macOS*).
     - Taking one look at the single-line minified wall of text, Katie doesn't blink: *"Did your build team seriously just send me a webpack-minified production bundle with stripped whitespace, mangled single-letter identifiers, and no source maps? I didn't ask for your compiled build artifacts—drop the raw, unminified source repository onto the transition VLAN share right now, or we're pulling your Sergeant at Arms task order before lunch."*
     - Over the speakerphone, the Rosslyn engineering bullpen goes dead silent in utter confusion—completely bewildered that a U.S. Senator-Elect is speaking fluent software-engineering shop talk (`minification pipelines`, `AST mangling`, `source maps`, `DOM trees`, `ARIA state attributes`, and `CSS specificity`) at the exact level of a principal developer.
  3. **Stage 3 — Twin Fury Over the Barely Commented Code & Every Single `WCAG` Failure in the Book**:
     - When the rattled lead engineer pushes the **raw, unminified source code** (`prairie-senate-src.zip`) to the shared drive and Katie and Nate open `header.html`, `styles.css`, `nav-controller.js`, and `casework-form.html` across split vertical panes in **`Quantum Code`**, **both Katie and Nate are furious** for two reasons:
       - **Fury #1 — The Unminified Source Code Is Barely Commented at All**: As disciplined developers who pad every source file with thorough architectural block comments and inline logic explanations so any developer can maintain it, Katie and Nate are appalled to find almost **zero code comments** across thousands of lines of government-funded code—just lazy copy-pasted spaghetti functions, dead CSS blocks, and a solitary `// TODO: fix later` from four years ago.
       - **Fury #2 — The Website as Written Fails Almost Every `WCAG` Pointer There Is**: Because **no U.S. Senator in congressional history had ever audited the raw source code of their official Senate constituent website template**, the build team had rubber-stamped their federal **VPAT (*Voluntary Product Accessibility Template*)** while failing nearly every **WCAG 2.1/2.2 AA** and **Section 508** requirement:
         - **No Skip-Navigation Link (`WCAG 2.4.1 Bypass Blocks`) & Broken Heading Hierarchy (`WCAG 1.3.1`)**: The template jumps straight from `<h1>` to `<h4>` tags used purely for font sizing, with no "Skip to Main Content" link for keyboard or switch users.
         - **`header.html`, Line 47 (Non-Semantic `<div>` Soup — `WCAG 2.1.1 Keyboard` & `4.1.2 Name, Role, Value`)**: The build team wrapped the *"Help with a Federal Agency"* dropdown menu in nested `<div class="nav-item" onclick="toggleMenu()">` tags with zero `role`, zero `aria-expanded` state, and no `tabindex`—meaning a blind veteran or a quadriplegic constituent using a Sip-and-Puff switch or keyboard `Tab` key cannot even open the navigation menu!
         - **`styles.css`, Line 112 (Stripped Focus Rings — `WCAG 2.4.7 Focus Visible`)**: The build team wrote `*:focus { outline: none !important; }` across the global stylesheet because their graphic designer thought the focus ring looked "cluttered"—leaving sighted keyboard users completely blind to where their cursor is on the page.
         - **`casework-form.html`, Line 204 (Unlabeled Constituent Casework Inputs & `2.6:1` Contrast Failure — `WCAG 1.4.3 Contrast` & `3.3.2 Labels`)**: On the Federal Casework Privacy Act Release Form (where disabled Virginians and veterans request help with delayed VA, Social Security, or Medicare wheelchair authorizations), the build team used `#999999` light-grey `placeholder` attributes on `#FFFFFF` backgrounds instead of explicit `<label for="...">` tags. When Katie presses `Command + F5` to turn on **`MagnaVoice`** (*real-life macOS VoiceOver*) at full volume next to the speakerphone and hits `Tab`, the screen reader echoes across the room and into the Rosslyn bullpen: **`"Edit text, blank. Edit text, blank. Image, hero_banner_final_v3.jpg. Unlabeled button."`**
  4. **Stage 4 — Shell-Shocked Humiliation & The Heavily Commented `.diff` Pull Request**:
     - Mortified and stammering over the speakerphone, the vendor's lead engineer asks: *"Senator-Elect... how on earth do you and your brother know how to read unminified DOM trees, CSS specificity, and WCAG accessibility trees like senior full-stack engineers?!"*
     - Katie's voice cuts through the speakerphone like a laser: *"Because my brother and I learned HTML in seventh grade in 1997—which is what inspired our dad to go back to school at CCHR—learned Quantum Basic 6.0 alongside our dad in 2001, copied the Web Applications Developer PHP and SQL curriculum onto our own laptops at Roosevelt Rehabilitation Center in Fishersville in March 2007 after refusing to let evaluators steer us into being ValueMart door greeters or lock us down on VITA's outdated computers, and spent the last nineteen years building production websites and desktop applications from these two wheelchairs! You sent us minified garbage to hide the fact that your unminified code doesn't have a single architectural block comment in it and fails every WCAG pointer in the book! Did your company actually sign a federal VPAT certifying to the Senate Sergeant at Arms that this template is Section 508 compliant, or did you just assume a Senator in a wheelchair wouldn't know how to read your code?"*
     - Right there at the conference table, Katie and Nate spend thirty minutes refactoring the templates into clean, semantic HTML5 (`<nav>`, `<button aria-expanded="false">`, explicit `<label for="...">` tags, `4.5:1+` contrast tokens, and high-contrast `:focus-visible` outlines)—**padding every single file with comprehensive architectural block comments and inline WCAG logic comments**—generate a `wcag-508-audit-fixes.diff` patch in **`Quantum Code`**, and order the build team to merge their pull request before January 3rd. Word immediately rips through every IT contractor on Capitol Hill: **"Never send minified code, uncommented spaghetti, or fake VPATs to Senator Prairie's office—she and her brother will demand your raw unminified repo, open it in `Quantum Code`, and debug you live on speakerphone."**

## 6. Senator Katie Prairie's January 2027 "Referendum Mandate" & Real-Time U.S. Politics Protocol

Following her **Tuesday, November 3, 2026** general election victory (having already defeated the retiring Class II Democratic Senator's handpicked corporate establishment heir in the **June 2026 Democratic Primary**), Senator-Elect Katie Prairie reads a viral post-election open letter shared across **The Grid** (*real-life Facebook*) by a grassroots voter named **Jess**—and adopts its core principles as her **absolute governing mandate** upon taking her seat in the United States Senate on **January 3, 2027**:

### A. The Eight Pillars of Senator Prairie's Governing Mandate (`January 2027+`)

1. **Won *Despite* the Party Establishment, Not Because of Them**:
   - Katie never forgets that the D.C. party establishment tried to block her in the June 2026 Primary in favor of a corporate-funded insider. She owes nothing to timid party leadership or K Street consultants who preached incrementalism while working families and disabled Americans were crushed.
   - She aligns herself squarely with the fearless, populist, constituent-first reform wing of Congress—insisting on bold, unapologetic communications that speak directly to everyday working people rather than sterile beltway talking points.
2. **Retiring "Compromise" With Bad-Faith Actors (*No Appeasing Bullies*)**:
   - Having dealt with bullies from the hallways of Northwood High School to the committee rooms of Richmond, Katie excises the hollow beltway cliché *"working with our friends across the aisle to water down our own bills"* from her vocabulary whenever basic civil rights, healthcare, transit, or democracy are on the line.
3. **Re-Engineer & Codify Everything Into Ironclad Federal Statute (*"The Messy-Divorce Prenup Standard"*)**:
   - **Don't Just Glue What They Broke Back Together—Re-Envision and Re-Engineer It**: As a software developer (`PHP` / `Quantum Studio 2026` / `Quantum Code`), Katie knows that unwritten "norms" and gentlemen's agreements are just unpatched security vulnerabilities waiting to be exploited.
   - **The Ironclad Prenup Rule**: Katie writes federal legislation the way a lawyer writes a prenuptial agreement heading into a second marriage after a messy divorce—**ironclad, irrefutable, and codified into black-letter statute down to the last line item ("down to who gets the coffee filters")**, leaving zero loopholes for rogue executives or hostile courts on reproductive freedom, voting rights, marriage equality, disability civil rights, Medicaid/SSI marriage equity, and wheelchair right-to-repair.
4. **Full Transparency, Releasing the Trafficker Files, & *"No One Is Above the Law"***:
   - Katie demands the complete, unredacted release of federal child-trafficking and corruption files (*the Epstein/trafficking dossiers*)—holding an absolute, zero-tolerance line: *"If you, your friends, your colleagues, or your billionaire benefactors are in those files, too bad. You abuse children, you're done. No one is above the law—reveal it, impeach them, prosecute them, and convict them."*
5. **Unapologetic Diversity, Equity, & Inclusion (`DEI` — Including Disability & Neurodivergence)**:
   - Katie refuses to let `DEI` be treated as a dirty word: **DEI stands for Diversity, Equity, and Inclusion** (and Accessibility), and she unapologetically brings it into every Senate hiring decision, judicial nomination, and committee room.
   - *"The people leading this country should look like the country they serve—working people, transit riders, wheelchair users, neurodivergent thinkers, and families of every background—not the billionaires who think they own them."*
6. **Proactive Receipts Before the January Fallout of the Opposition's "Big Beautiful Bill" Hits**:
   - Knowing that when the delayed healthcare cuts, tariff/cost-of-living spikes, and working-family fallout of the opposition's prior *"big beautiful bill"* hit voters in January 2027, the opposition will brazenly lie and blame the new Senate majority for the damage of their own law, Katie and Nate refuse to play defense.
   - Using Nate's live data dashboards, open town halls accessible by local public transit, and line-by-line legislative receipts, Senator Prairie calls out the lies *before* they take root—showing voters the exact bill text, roll-call votes, and effective dates that caused the harm.
7. **Line-by-Line Dismantling of Authoritarian Executive Blueprints (*"Blinders On, Elbows Out"*):**
   - Treating the opposition's authoritarian policy playbook (*Project 2025* / Schedule F civil-service purges / agency dismantling) like a hostile malware payload, Katie and Nate go through it **line by line**, authoring statutory patches and oversight blocks to repair every single agency and strip corrupt, grifter branding off public institutions.
8. **Real-Time U.S. Politics Synchronization Protocol (`November 2026 – January 2027+`)**:
   - Because `books/katie` has now caught up to **real-time U.S. political history**, future chapters in `books/katie/e005+` will actively track and react to real-world U.S. Senate and national political developments as they unfold—filtering all real-world politician names, news networks, and tech brands through RaggieSoft's copyright-safe in-universe naming rules (`llms-copyright-names.txt`).

## 8. Extended 2010–2045+ Campaign, COVID-19, Statewide Transit Tour, Refusing the Presidency, & Post-Senate Undercover Transit Auditor Lore

### A. The `TRAC` vs. `TDC` Pincer (`2010–2014`)
- Before Katie ever takes office on the Virginia Beach City Council in January 2011, **Nate** wins an appointed citizen-rider seat on **`TRAC` (*CVTA Transit Riders Advisory Committee*)** in `2009–2010`.
- When Katie joins the **Virginia Beach City Council (`Kempsville District`, `2011–2014`)**, she is appointed as a voting municipal commissioner on the **`TDC` (*Transit District Commission of Hampton Roads*, the governing board of `CVTA`)**.
- Together, the twins execute a devastating **inside-outside pincer**: whenever `CVTA` executives claim at the `TDC` dais that wheelchair ramps are "99% reliable" or that `Route #12` or `Route #15` doesn't need evening frequency, Nate presents timestamped `TRAC` rider logs from the public podium while Councilwoman Prairie motions from the `TDC` dais to freeze executive bonuses until the broken hydraulic ramps and missed pull-outs are fixed.

### B. COVID-19 Clean-Air & Non-Alcoholic Sobriety Protocols (`March 2020 – 2022+`)
- **Non-Alcoholic Sanitization (`Jan 1, 2003 Sobriety Vow Compliance`)**: Because Katie and Nate maintain an absolute, lifelong zero-alcohol vow (refusing even denatured ethyl/isopropyl alcohol hand sanitizers on their powerchair joysticks and armrests), they carry **`non-alcoholic benzalkonium chloride (BZK) antiseptic wipes`** and **`hypochlorous acid (HOCl) spray bottles`** in their under-seat backpacks to sanitize bus stanchions, fareboxes, elevator buttons, and their `SwiftChair` joysticks.
- **Respiratory & Clean-Air Gear**: They wear **`NIOSH-approved trifold N95 respirators`** fitted with **`airtight medical drinking valves`** so they can hydrate through straws on multi-hour `CVTA`, `JRTC`, and `ARTS` rides without breaking their mask seal, and Nate logs real-time ventilation data using a **`pocket NDIR CO2 monitor`** paired with a battery-powered **`portable personal HEPA air purifier`** clipped to their wheelchair armrests.
- **August 2020 Special Session at the Central Virginia University (`CVU`) Student Commons Ballroom**: During the pandemic special session in Richmond, while the House of Delegates meets in the `CVU` Siegel athletic arena, the **Senate of Virginia** convenes inside the **`CVU Student Commons Ballroom`** on West Broad Street (`JRTC Route 6`), with each Senator's desk enclosed in a three-sided **`clear polycarbonate shield`**.

### C. The 2026 Statewide Public-Transit Tour (`All 95 Counties & 38 Independent Cities — Zero Campaign Tour Buses`)
- **Official Campaign Theme Song (*"The Accessibility Project"* by The Paper Wall) & Full `/books/aubrie` Coalition**:
  - ***"The Accessibility Project"*** (`the-paper-wall/2003-eleventh-child-book-2/web-mp3/1-13-the-accessibility-project.mp3`) serves as the official theme song of **Katie Prairie for U.S. Senate**.
  - Katie receives explicit written, royalty-free licensing permission from **The Paper Wall** (**Julian Croft ["Leo"]**, **Maya Sterling ["Olivia"]**, **Izzy ["Luna"]**, **Bea ["Stella"]**, & **Charlotte ["Charlie"] Croft**, **Diana Hale**, **Ellie Cross**, & **Fiona Croft**, and **Holly O'Connell** at Engine Room Records) and from **the characters of `/books/aubrie` whom the song was written about** (**Jordan Shawn Kelly**, **Aubrie Jean Kelly**, **Madison Elizabeth Scott**, **Zoë Wren Brooks**, **Lauren Nicole Barnes**, **Hannah Parker Anderson**, **Mark Shawn Anderson**, **Sarah Kristyn Braithwaite**, **Jessica Gallagher**, **Isabel Claire Dawson**, **Jessamine "Jess" Mercer**, and **Daniel, Laura, & Megan Brooks**). Every eligible Virginian voter in the `/books/aubrie` family votes for Katie in both the **June 16, 2026 Democratic Primary** and the **November 3, 2026 General Election**, hosts her at the **Brooks Family Compound** along **U.S. Route 11 (`Lee Highway`)** (`BT Route 3`), and campaigns alongside her across the New River Valley, **Bluewater**, and **Abingdon**.
- **Refusing the Diesel Campaign Tour Bus & Bridging Rural Transit Gaps With David's Rented Wheelchair-Accessible Van**:
  - When Katie launches her 2026 U.S. Senate campaign in the living room of her and Nate's ranch home on **Lord Nelson Drive** in Kempsville with **David** and **Nate** by her side, K Street consultants try to lease her a luxury **`Sainte-Claire`** (*real-life Prevost*) motorcoach wrapped with her face. Katie flatly refuses: *"If a rural county or an industrial corridor doesn't have public transit, or if the bus ramp doesn't work, then I need to experience that exact gap the same way my constituents do."*
  - Whenever fixed-route public transit has a gap between systems (such as crossing the **17.6-mile Chesapeake Bay Bridge-Tunnel [`CBBT`]** where **neither `CVTA` nor `ShoreLine Transit` runs a bus**, bridging between the coalfields and **Cumberland Gap**, or climbing west from **Staunton** over **Shenandoah Mountain [`US-250`]** into **Palo Alto, Highland County**), **David Prairie** rents a wheelchair-accessible side-entry ramp minivan with four-point floor tie-downs to bridge Katie and Nate from one transit hub or rural community to the next.
- **1. The Eastern Shore (`Accomack & Northampton Counties` — Stranded at Both Ends of `US-13`: The `Pleasure House Road CVTA Transfer Point <-> Cape Charles` `CBBT` South Gap & The `Oak Hall -> Pocomoke City, MD / First State Transit` North Gap)**:
  - **CRITICAL CANON RULE**: Virginia's Eastern Shore is stranded on a public-transit island at **both** ends of `US-13`:
    1. **The South-End Gap (`17.6 miles` across the `CBBT` between the `Pleasure House Road CVTA Transfer Point` & `Cape Charles`)**: Even though **CVTA Routes `#1`, `#27`, and `#35`** converge at the **Shore Drive & Pleasure House Road Transfer Point** in Virginia Beach right at the southern foot of the **Chesapeake Bay Bridge-Tunnel (`CBBT`)**, neither `CVTA` nor `ShoreLine Transit` (*STAR Transit*) runs a public bus across the `17.6-mile` Bridge-Tunnel between **Pleasure House Road** and **Cape Charles**.
    2. **The North-End Gap (`~11 miles` across the VA/MD State Line to `Pocomoke City, MD` & `First State Transit`)**: At the northern end of Accomack County, `ShoreLine Transit` terminates at **Oak Hall / Chincoteague Junction**—stopping eleven miles short of **Pocomoke City, Maryland**, where **Maryland Shore Transit** (*real-life Shore Transit*) runs north to **Salisbury** and connects toward **Delaware's `First State Transit` (`FST`** — *in-universe for `DART First State`*).
  - Because no bus crosses the `CBBT`, Katie and Nate ride **CVTA Route `#1`** to the **Shore Drive & Pleasure House Road Transfer Point** at the foot of the bridge-tunnel, where **David Prairie meets them in a rented wheelchair-accessible ramp minivan** to drive them across the `CBBT` to **Cape Charles**. From Cape Charles, they board **`ShoreLine Transit` (`SLT`)** up `US-13` through **Eastville**, **Exmore**, **Onley**, **Accomac**, and **Oak Hall**, meeting poultry processing workers at **`Eastern Shore Poultry`** (*Perdue*) and championing dedicated federal interstate transit funds on the Senate Appropriations Committee to close **both** Delmarva gaps: (a) a permanent public-transit bus connection from **Cape Charles (`ShoreLine Transit`)** across the `CBBT` directly into the **Pleasure House Road & Shore Drive `CVTA` Transfer Point** in Virginia Beach—utilizing the Chesapeake Bay Bridge and Tunnel District's **`Class 8` ("Public Transit Bus") toll schedule of `$1.00 per crossing`** (which satisfies the Bridge-Tunnel District's revenue-bond covenant against `$0.00` free passage while discounting public buses from the `$41–$50` commercial motorcoach rate, billed automatically via the agency's fleet transponder as an eligible `FTA Section 5311(f)` operating expense) and attaching a **mandatory statutory stipulation requiring a reciprocal cross-honoring agreement for all fares and passes between `CVTA` and `ShoreLine Transit`**—and (b) extending `ShoreLine Transit` north across the Maryland border into **Pocomoke City** and **Salisbury** with a coordinated tri-state Delmarva bus connection to **First State Transit** (*DART First State*) in Delaware.
- **2. Roanoke, The New River Valley (`RVT`, `Blue Ridge Transit`, `Radford Transit`), & `Bluewater, Virginia` (`Bluewater Transit` & `The Maroon Express`)**:
  - Katie, Nate, and David ride **`ARTS` (*The Patriot*)** to **Roanoke's `Third Street Station`**, tap their contactless **Blue Ridge Go-Cards** (*"One Card. Three Systems. The Entire Valley."*) to board **Roanoke Valley Transit's (`RVT`) *ARTS Train Connector* / *Roanoke Express*** (`$3.00` Express fare; **never** called *"Valley Metro"* or *"Smart Way"*) down `I-81` to the **Christiansburg Transit Center at Falling Branch (`I-81 Exit 118`)**, up the **`BUSES ONLY` direct flyover ramp** onto the **US-460 Bypass** to **New River Valley Mall Transit Center**, and into the **CPI Transit Center** in **Blacksburg**.
  - Across Blacksburg, Christiansburg, and Radford, they ride **Blue Ridge Transit (`BT`)** **Route 1 (*"The Spine"*)**, **Route 2 (*"The Christiansburg Local"*)** past **Wades Lane**, the **Blue Ridge Regional Aquatic Center**, and the nearly completed **Christiansburg Cambria Station (`800 Depot Street NE`)**, **Route 3 (*"The Radford Link"*)** to the **Brooks Family Compound** on **U.S. Route 11 (`Lee Highway`)**, **Route 4** past the **Exit 118C hotels** and **Simmons Road NE (`CPI Christiansburg Campus` / *"the Law School"*)** to the **Woodland Drive VDOT 60-Foot Turnaround Loop (`3345 Roanoke Street`)**, and 100% fare-free **Radford Transit (*The Radford Connector*)** through **Prices Fork, Belview, Centerville, and Fairlawn** into **McConnell Transit Center**.
  - Joined by **Jordan Shawn Kelly**, **Aubrie Jean Kelly**, and **Madison Elizabeth Scott** (the original Bennett Hall **Room 214** trio), they board **Bluewater Transit's *Maroon Express*** (`$5.00` Express fare on the **Blue Ridge Go-Card**) aboard a 45-foot **Sainte-Claire 45H3** coach (`MAROON EXPRESS / BLUEWATER EXP via CBURG TC`) south via **Dublin Park & Ride / NRVRC Justin's Station (`I-81 Exit 98`)**, **Max Meadows Park & Ride (`I-81 Exit 84`)**, and **Bluewater Park & Ride (`I-77 Exit 14`)** into the **Bluewater Transit Center** directly across from **Bluewater High School** in **Bluewater, Virginia** (*real-life Hillsville, VA*, **Virginia's largest incorporated town** because it refuses to break away from deep-red **Carroll County** to become an independent city—which **Stephen Kovacs** highlights on the **GNN Big Board** on Election Night when he zooms into Carroll County to show the massive Democratic blue circle of the Town of Bluewater!). From Bluewater Transit Center, they also ride Bluewater Transit's regional plateau connectors: the **Purple Line** to **Galax Community College**, the **Gold Line** up `US-221` to **Floyd**, and the **Emerald Line** down `US-58` across **Lovers Leap** through **Meadows of Dan** and **Stuart (`Patrick County`)** to the **Martinsville Transit Center**, connecting with **Piedmont Regional Transit (`PRT`)** into **Danville**.
- **3. Southwest Virginia & The Coalfields (`Wythe, Smyth, Washington, Wise, Lee, Scott, Dickenson, Buchanan, Tazewell, & Russell Counties`)**:
  - Connecting from **Bluewater Transit** at **Bluewater Transit Center**, **Galax**, and **Max Meadows Park & Ride (`I-81 Exit 84`)**, Katie, Nate, and David ride **`Mountain Empire Transit` (`MET` — *The Highlands Express* / `MountainGo!`)** west down the `I-81` corridor through **Wytheville**, **Marion (`Smyth County`)**, **Abingdon (`Washington County` — accompanied by New River Valley volunteers Lauren Nicole Barnes & Hannah Parker Anderson)**, and **Bristol Union Station**, and then board **`Cumberland Coalfield Transit` (`CCT` — *in-universe for Four County Transit*)** cutaway buses with rear hydraulic **`Elevate-U`** wheelchair lifts through **Big Stone Gap** (**`Powell Valley Community College` [`PVCC`]**), **Norton**, **Wise** (**`Commonwealth College at Wise`**), **Clintwood**, and **Grundy**.
  - At a town hall in **Big Stone Gap**, retired coal miners with black-lung oxygen tanks and retired rail workers roll up alongside Katie and Nate's `SwiftChair` powerchairs—realizing this Virginia Beach Senator isn't an elitist coastal tourist when she knows the exact hydraulic whine of a rural transit lift and fights for rural clinic transit, universal healthcare, and **`Appalachian Electric Grid` (`AEG`)** utility rate relief.
- **4. Cumberland Gap National Historical Park With Dad (`David Prairie` & Frontier Ancestor `Silas Prairie`)**:
  - Bridging the rural transit gap in **David's rented wheelchair-accessible ramp minivan** to the westernmost tip of Virginia (`US-58` in **Lee County**), **David Prairie** takes Katie and Nate up to **Cumberland Gap** (`Pinnacle Overlook`), where they trace their family name back to their 1775 frontier trailblazer and surveyor ancestor **`Silas Prairie`** (*fictionalized counterpart to Daniel Boone*)—connecting Silas Prairie's 1775 Wilderness Road mountain pass to Katie and Nate's 21st-century fight to keep every rail line, bus corridor, and sidewalk open for all Virginians.
- **5. Katie's First Visit to The Cassini Center for Space Exploration — Which Becomes Her Favorite Place in the Commonwealth (`Palo Alto, Highland County, VA`, [`lore/astro-kids/`](file:///Users/michael/Developer/raggiesoft-servers/raggiesoft-narratives/lore/astro-kids/Cassini%20Center%20for%20Space%20Exploration/Cassini%20Center.md))**:
  - After riding **`Shenandoah Valley Transit` (`SVT Route 250 Connector`)** across **Waynesboro**, **Fishersville** (past **`Roosevelt Rehabilitation Center`**), and **Staunton**, fixed-route public transit ends at the foot of the Allegheny mountains. **David Prairie** drives Katie and Nate in their rented wheelchair-accessible ramp minivan west up **U.S. Route 250** over **Shenandoah Mountain** and **Bullpasture Mountain** into **Palo Alto, Highland County, Virginia** (`Cowpasture Road N / Route 614`).
  - There, Katie, Nate, and David visit **The Cassini Center for Space Exploration** for the **very first time**—built by **Valerie Anne Miller** (`45` in 2026, who greets Katie as *"Senator Prairie"* by her sitting Virginia State Senate title) for her 41-year-old non-verbal autistic, wheelchair-using triplet cousins **Lyra Carina Starling**, **Orion Rigel Starling**, and **Celeste Callisto Starling** (`born autumn 1985`—the exact same birth year and age as Katie and Nate!). Once Katie sees firsthand what the Cassini Center offers the Commonwealth—rolling side by side across the 48-inch zero-threshold polished concrete pathways, looking down into the **34-inch-high Starlight Wing and Cassini Experience tables**, parking all five wheelchairs in a circle inside the sensory-safe **Tranquility Dome Planetarium**, and peering at Saturn's rings through the **articulated wheelchair-height periscope eyepiece** of the 24-inch Ritchey-Chrétien telescope under Highland County's `Bortle 2/3` dark skies—**it becomes her absolute favorite place in the entire Commonwealth of Virginia**.
- **6. The Constituent Call From Natalie's Mother (`January 14, 2027`, `books/katie/e011/c003/p004.md`) & Holding `DARS` and `RRC` Accountable in `Dirksen Room 511` and the `Senate Appropriations Subcommittee` (`February 16–17, 2027`, `books/katie/e011/c003/p005.md`)**:
  - Riding **`Shenandoah Valley Transit` (`SVT Route 250 Connector`** — *in-universe for `BRITE Bus`* **)** between **Staunton**, **Fishersville**, and **Waynesboro**, Katie and Nate return to **`Roosevelt Rehabilitation Center` (`RRC`)**—where they spent 12 unforgettable days in **March 2007** (`March 4–16, 2007`) before dropping out with **Tina** and soon ditching the unofficial modules at home to do their own thing—speaking directly to current disabled vocational rehabilitation students.
  - On **Thursday, January 14, 2027** (`books/katie/e011/c003/p004.md`), just eleven days after swearing her Oath of Office, Katie answers a constituent rollover call directly on her desk phone in **Room 511 of the Dirksen Senate Office Building** from **Evelyn Brooks**, the 68-year-old mother and court-ordered legal **Guardian and Conservator** of 41-year-old **Natalie**. Mrs. Brooks reveals that during Katie's Fall 2026 Senate campaign, Natalie recognized Katie and Nate on a television commercial as the twins in wheelchairs from RRC who were only there for about a week in March 2007 and held her hand by the cafeteria when she was crying. Mrs. Brooks asks if **Nate** is still with Katie, and when Katie says yes, asks to be put on speakerphone so Nate can hear the whole story too—recounting in harrowing detail how **Christopher Vance** (`Chris`) used a scrambled opposite-gender keycard on Wednesday night (`March 14, 2007`) to lock himself in Natalie's dorm room and violently rape her; how RRC administration escorted Chris to a bus in Staunton at `7:50 AM` on Thursday (`March 15`) without calling police while keeping a bleeding Natalie inside the campus infirmary all day, bleaching her bloodied bedsheets in the campus laundry, and lying to Mrs. Brooks that Natalie was merely having a homesickness episode; and how Mrs. Brooks discovered the injuries at an `I-64` rest stop on Friday (`March 16`), took Natalie to the hospital ER, and was stonewalled by DARS behind `34 C.F.R. § 361.38`. Mrs. Brooks sends her notarized Guardianship/Conservatorship privacy waiver, formal constituent complaint, and March 16, 2007 hospital ER records by courier to Room 511, and Natalie says a sweet hello to Katie and Nate over the speakerphone.
  - On **Tuesday, February 16, 2027** (`books/katie/e011/c003/p005.md`), an all-new 2027 executive delegation (**DARS Commissioner Marcus Sterling** and **RRC Director Evelyn Cross**, neither of whom worked at RRC in 2007, and neither of whom knows Katie and Nate ever attended RRC since their biographies list only their **Advanced Studies Diploma from Virginia Beach City Public Schools** and their **Associate of Applied Science [`A.A.S.`] from the Community College of Hampton Roads**) comes into **Room 511 of the Dirksen Senate Office Building** asking Senator Katie Prairie for `$18.5 million` in federal Congressionally Directed Spending. When Katie asks hyperspecific questions about two 21-year-old twins in wheelchairs in March 2007 (`grabbed push-handles`, `auto/woodworking/nursing Voc Eval tour`, `ValueMart door greeter pitch`, `Level 2 yellow Hall Passes`, `accused of having sex in a locked seasonal closet`, `calling the night supervisor's expulsion bluff over headcount funding`, and `threatened with a Level 1 restriction`), the executives wonder out loud why the Senator is being so oddly hyperspecific about a twenty-year-old incident, squirm, deny any knowledge of twin siblings, and hide behind the federal Vocational Rehabilitation privacy wall (**`34 C.F.R. § 361.38`**)—until Katie makes them squirm and then drops the bomb that **she and Nate are those two March 2007 twelve-day RRC dropouts (`Status 28`) and waive their confidentiality on the spot!**
    - **Behind-the-Scenes Lore Note on What Is Going Through Commissioner Sterling's and Director Cross's Minds in Room 511 (`Lore Only — Since the Narrative Camera Is Locked to Nate's First-Person POV`)**:
      1. **Stage 1 — Smug Bureaucratic Overconfidence Walking Into Room 511**: Walking down the Dirksen hallway with their glossy spiral-bound `$18.5 million` funding presentation, Sterling and Cross assume this will be a ten-minute ceremonial rubber-stamp meeting: *"Senator Prairie is a forty-one-year-old wheelchair user from Virginia Beach. What disabled Senator on earth wouldn't sign off on $18.5 million for Virginia's flagship rehabilitation center?"* Having checked the *Congressional Directory* (which lists only Northwood High School and `CCHR`), neither of them has the slightest inkling that Katie or Nate ever set foot in Fishersville.
      2. **Stage 2 — Condescending Dismissal at Question #1 (*"Some Activist Sent Her an Email"*)**: When Katie first asks about a twenty-one-year-old twin brother and sister in wheelchairs having their push-handles grabbed at Barton Hall, Cross internally rolls her eyes, assuming a disgruntled former client or disability activist sent a complaint to the Senator's inbox, and tries to brush it off with a canned line about staff training.
      3. **Stage 3 — Baffled Unease at Questions #2 & #3 (*"Why Is a U.S. Senator Being So Oddly, Forensically Hyperspecific About 2007?"*)**: When Katie refuses to let them pivot and drills into hyper-granular 2007 operational details—two `CCHR` computer-science graduates forced to tour the **auto mechanics, woodworking, and nursing shops**, an evaluator pitching a **Waynesboro ValueMart door-greeter partnership**, **VITA's seven-year-old operating system** locking down local web servers on the classroom desktops, and staying stuck on **`Level 2` in Barton Hall** begging for **signed yellow paper Hall Passes** because moving to **Cantor Hall** required earning **`Level 3`**—alarm bells start ringing in both executives' heads: *"Wait... why does a United States Senator know the exact color of the Level 2 paper Hall Passes in Barton Hall and VITA's 2007 desktop permissions in the Bates-Harding computer lab?! No K Street lobbyist or constituent form letter would ever know that!"*
      4. **Stage 4 — Cold Creeping Dread at Question #4 (*"How Could Anyone Know About `MedStrip` Wound-Closure Strips and Plastic Bags of Curtains Inside a Seasonal Storage Closet at 10:45 PM?!"*)**: When Katie recounts the ninth afternoon (`Tuesday, March 13, 2007`)—the broken nose and split nasal bridge in the therapeutic pool, the twin brother covering his sister's bare chest while she held her bleeding nostrils with both hands until a towel arrived, the Student Health nurses pulling the split skin across her nasal bridge together with **sterile `MedStrip` wound-closure strips** under a **seven-day white gauze bandage**, slipping down the darkened hallway from the Rec Hall at `10:45 PM` in street clothes, locking themselves inside a **seasonal storage closet** using **lumpy bags of curtains as pillows**, the night supervisor unlocking the door with a master key after the `11:00 PM` curfew check and accusing them of having sex, and the twins daring him to call their father in Alexandria right then and there to expose the center's headcount quota—Sterling and Cross are internally panicking: *"Even if someone in Richmond leaked an old 2007 disciplinary log, how does she know the exact sterile `MedStrip` wound-closure strips the nurses taped across that girl's nose—and no state form in DARS history ever recorded what the pillows inside a Barton Hall storage closet felt like at 10:45 PM! How on earth could she know that?!"* Still blind to the fact that the two impeccably dressed forty-one-year-old `SwiftChair` users across the desk *are* those twins, they wonder out loud why she is being so oddly hyperspecific and throw up the **`34 C.F.R. § 361.38`** privacy wall—walking straight into Katie and Nate's trap.
      5. **Stage 5 — The Split-Second Math Click & Stomach-Drop Vertigo (*"41 Minus 20 Is 21... Twin Brother and Sister... Both in Wheelchairs... CCHR December 2006... OH GOD, IT'S THEM."*)**: The instant Katie laces her fingers through Nate's across their `SwiftChair` armrests and says *"Because the twenty-one-year-old twin brother and sister in those March 2007 Barton Hall files are sitting right across this desk from you,"* every piece of the puzzle slams together inside Sterling's and Cross's skulls at once: `2027 - 2007 = 20 years ago`; `41 years old - 20 = 21 years old`; fraternal twin brother and sister; both full-time wheelchair users; both graduated from `CCHR` in December 2006—three months before March 2007; and the only reason `RRC` wasn't listed in the *Congressional Directory* is because **nobody puts a place they dropped out of after twelve days on their resume!** Both executives feel the floor drop out from under their stomachs as they realize they just looked a sitting United States Senator in the eye, told her that her own broken nose and grabbed wheelchair handles never happened, and tried to lecture her that **`34 C.F.R. § 361.38`** prohibited her from discussing her own life without her own permission.
      6. **Stage 6 — The "ValueMart Door Greeter" & "`Status 28` Dropout" Institutional Mortification**: Looking at the gold Senate pin on Katie's lapel and Nate's `MagnaBook Pro`, Director Cross is hit by a wave of sheer professional horror: in March 2007, an RRC Vocational Evaluator looked at **Katie Prairie**—a woman who already held an accredited college degree in computer science, who was about to build a multi-decade production software company with her brother, and who would be elected to the Virginia Beach City Council, the House of Delegates, the Senate of Virginia, and the **United States Senate**—and told her that if coding was "too taxing," RRC could train her to sit by the sliding doors of the **Waynesboro ValueMart as a door greeter**. Worse still, in DARS's own mainframes in Richmond right that second, **the United States Senator who holds the gavel and purse strings over DARS's `$18.5 million` federal appropriation is officially coded as a `Status 28` ("Unsuccessful Rehabilitation — Client Dropped Out") failure!**
      7. **Stage 7 — Internal Panic Over How Their Own 2007 Redaction Blindfolded Them in 2027**: Commissioner Sterling's mind races in terror wondering why his legislative staff in Richmond didn't flag `"Prairie, Katie"` and `"Prairie, Nathaniel"` before letting him walk into Dirksen Room 511—only to realize the supreme poetic justice: back in April 2007, when DARS General Counsel blacked out Katie and Nate's names on the Tuesday pool log with a thick black magic marker as `[REDACTED UNDER 34 C.F.R. § 361.38 — STATUS 28 DROPOUT]` to keep Natalie Brooks's lawyer from finding them as witnesses, **DARS also guaranteed that its own 2027 leadership wouldn't recognize the two redacted `Status 28` dropouts on that page until they were sitting across Senator Prairie's mahogany desk!**
      8. **Stage 8 — The Second Nuclear Bomb (*"Wait... They Were Eyewitnesses to the Forty-Eight Hours Before Natalie Brooks Was Raped?!"*)**: For half a second after Katie reveals their identity, Sterling and Cross desperately hope they only have to grovel and apologize for the 2007 wheelchair push-handles, the `ValueMart` insult, and the seasonal storage closet write-up—until **Nate** quietly adds: *"And wait until you hear why our Thursday morning Level 1 restriction meeting with our counselor never happened on March 15, 2007,"* and Katie lays **Evelyn Brooks's notarized Guardianship/Conservatorship waiver and March 16, 2007 hospital ER/SANE report** on top of their glossy funding binder. At that moment, Sterling and Cross realize they aren't just facing an embarrassing twenty-year-old constituent grievance—**the United States Senator sitting on the Appropriations Subcommittee is a firsthand eyewitness proving that the exact male client who broke her nose and ripped her swimsuit top off in the pool on Tuesday (`March 13`) was left on campus by RRC while staff punished the twins, given a scrambled keycard to rape Natalie Brooks on Wednesday night (`March 14`), and whisked off campus in an RRC van on Thursday morning (`March 15`) while housekeeping bleached the sheets.**

Within forty minutes, the basement archives in Richmond and Fishersville pull the unredacted March 2007 (`03/04/2007 – 03/16/2007`) paper files for `Prairie, Katie` and `Prairie, Nathaniel` alongside the unredacted Tuesday pool log and Thursday infirmary/van records, confirming that every single word Senator Prairie and Nate said is 100% true. Terrified of being annihilated on live television cameras in front of Natalie and her mother in Dirksen Room 124 on Wednesday morning, **both Sterling and Cross beg Richmond for permission to withdraw from Wednesday's Subcommittee witness panel and drive straight back to Virginia**. Fenwick's` Bridge Home to `Sunset Drive`**:

Ten minutes later, Halverson holds his first Pentagon press briefing in over six months declaring **"total victory"** and calling impeachment a *"stunt"* and a *"circus"* (backed by Thorne and Drayton). Fenwick's` Rail Bridge, & Sleeping on "The West Side of the Tracks" on `Sunset Drive` (`5:15 PM`)**: Avoiding `Washington Union Station`'s echoing sensory overload whenever possible, Katie and Nate take the quiet **Capitol South** street elevator (`First and C Streets SE`, Blue/Orange/Silver pylon) to **L'Enfant Plaza** and board the **Capital Transit Yellow Line** across the Potomac on the **Charles R. ### E. Three Senate Terms (`2027–2045`), Uncle Richard's Final Vote for Katie (`2032`) & Funeral (`Before 2038`), Refusing the 2043 Presidential Nomination at `Military Hwy & Providence Rd`, The "Live-Bus Endorsement Interview" (`No Secret Service Bubble`), & Post-Senate Undercover Transit Auditing (`2045+`)
1. **Three Terms in the United States Senate (`Jan 3, 2027 – Jan 3, 2045`, Ages 41–59 — Senior Senator from Virginia, Full "A-Committee" Chairwoman, & Uncle Richard's Final Vote and Funeral)**:
   - Re-elected overwhelmingly in **2032** and **2038**, Senator Katie Prairie serves **eighteen years** (three full six-year terms) in the United States Senate, becoming **Virginia's Senior United States Senator**, ranking in the top 5 in Democratic Caucus seniority, and rising to **Full Committee Chairwoman of the Senate Committee on Banking, Housing, and Urban Affairs** (with 100% Senate jurisdiction over the **Federal Transit Administration [`FTA`]** and all public transit nationwide) alongside her senior seat on the **Senate Armed Services Committee (`SASC`)** and the **Committee on Health, Education, Labor, and Pensions (`HELP`)**—authoring landmark federal laws on universal public transit operations funding, wheelchair Right-to-Repair, SSI/Medicaid Marriage Equality, and mandatory Section 508 source-code audits.
   - **David Prairie's Passing (`Before 2032`), Uncle Richard's Post-2027 Party Cleanup & Final Vote for Katie (`2032`), & Why Nate and Especially Katie Take Uncle Richard's Passing So Hard (`Like a Second Father`, `Before 2038`)**: Sometime during Katie's first Senate term—after Garrett Thorne is convicted and barred from office on April 16, 2027, and **before Katie runs for her second Senate term in `2032`**—Katie and Nate's beloved father **David Prairie passes away** (he does not get to see his daughter run for re-election in 2032). Meanwhile, after President Clara Morales is sworn in (`April 2027`), **Uncle Richard** works tirelessly with institutional conservatives across the Commonwealth to clean up the Virginia Republican Party and restore it to the problem-solving party he remembered when serving in the House of Delegates. True to his word, Richard goes back to voting Republican—**except for the one more time Katie Prairie runs for United States Senator during his lifetime (`November 2032`, her second Senate term)**, when he and Aunt Marge proudly vote for Katie once more. When Uncle Richard passes away before Katie runs for her third Senate term in **2038**, **Nate and especially Katie take his death very hard—because he was like a second father to them, even though they disagreed politically**. **Katie and Nate travel to Danville in their wheelchairs to attend Uncle Richard's funeral strictly in her personal capacity as his friend—not in her official capacity as United States Senator** (leaving the Senate pin and official title behind to sit and grieve privately beside Aunt Marge and Sam, Mandy, and Sarah).
2. **October 24, 2043 (Age 58) — Quietly Refusing the 2045 Presidency From Home Surrounded by `Nate`, `Brittany`, & `Chelsea`, Signing Five Statutory Shields Against the Bubble, & Sleeping in Their Four-Person Nest ([`books/katie/e011/c007/p001.md`](file:///Users/michael/Developer/raggiesoft-servers/raggiesoft-narratives/books/katie/e011/c007/p001.md))**:
   - Ahead of the **2044 Presidential Election** (`for the term beginning January 20, 2045`), national party leaders and draft committees urge Senator Katie Prairie to run for President, with polling showing she would win in a landslide.
   - Out of deep respect for **Chelsea's** severe developmental disability (`refusing to subject Chelsea or Brittany to a noisy bus-stop press scrum or satellite trucks`), **Katie, Nate, Brittany, and Chelsea record and quietly upload an unedited four-minute video right from their living room on Arvin Road**:
     - **Why She Refuses the Presidency (*Remembering October 14, 2027 Inside the Bubble & Protecting Their Four-Person Sanctuary*)**: Remembering her and Nate's very first day inside **President Clara Morales's** Secret Service bubble flying into **Chambers Field** on **October 14, 2027** (`books/katie/e011/c006/p001.md`)—watching every cross-street frozen behind barricades and realizing Clara couldn't even step ten feet off a swept carpet for a cold drink—Katie knows that under `18 U.S.C. § 3056`, becoming President permanently locks you inside an armored motorcade, bans you from ever riding public transit unannounced, and fills your family home with armed agents and cameras where rescued **Chelsea** and redeemed **Brittany** deserve quiet sanctuary.
